 cāral nāṭa.  (kuṟun. 141)
paru - peru. cattāṉ - cettāṉ. alli - elli (iravu) mutaliya tiripukaḷil akaram ekaramākat tirivatāl, ataṉ nīḷa vaṭivamāṉa ākāram ēkāramākat tirivatum iyalpām.
e-ṭu:	cāṟu - cēṟu (tiruviḻā)
		cār - cēr
		cāral - cēral
		cāṇ - cēṇ (kaṉṉaṭam)
cāral nāṭaṉaic cāral eṉṟatu ōr iṭavāku peyar. atu muṉṉarē vaḻakkiṟantu, piṉpu ataṉ tiripāṉa cēral eṉṉum vaṭivamē vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu, eṉak koḷka.
lakaramey yīṟu cila coṟkaḷil ṉakaramey yīṟākat tirital iyalpu.
e-ṭu:	mēla - mēṉa
		vel - veṉ (veṟṟi)
		kal - kaṉ (tōṇṭu)
		āl - āṉ (3ām vēṟṟumaiyurupu).
im muṟaippaṭi, cēral eṉpatu cēraṉ eṉat tirintatu. cēral eṉpatu aṉṉīṟu peṟiṉ cēralaṉ eṉṟākum. atu vaṭamoḻi yiṟ kērala(ṉ), kēraḷa(ṉ) eṉat tiriyum. kēralaṉ eṉpatu kēraṉ, kēlaṉ eṉat tokum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kēraṉ eṉpataik kālṭuvel aṟiñarum kēlaṉ eṉpatai rā. irākavaiyaṅ kārum cēraṉ eṉṉum peyarkku mūlamākak koṇṭaṉar. itu talai kālākak koṇṭa taṭumāṟṟam.
iṉi, cārtal eṉpataṟkuc cāytal eṉṟum, cār eṉpataṟkut tāḻvāram eṉṟum poruḷiruttalāl. malaiccāralai allatu aṭivārattaik kuṟikkum cār eṉṉum collē cēr eṉat tirintu, vaḷḷal eṉpatu pōl allīṟu peṟṟuc cēral eṉṟāyatu eṉak koḷḷalum oṉṟu.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, varalāṟṟaṭippaṭaiyilē moḻi kaḷai āytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum oruvaḻippōkkāṉa kakara cakara māṟṟamaṉṟi iruvaḻip pōkkāṉa kakara cakarap parimāṟṟamē uṇmaiyākak koḷḷutaṟkuriya teṉṟum, cēraṉ eṉṉum kuṭavēntaṉ kuṭip peyar tūya teṉ collēyeṉṟum, kēraṉ, kēlaṉ, kēraḷaṉ eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil varupavaiyellām cēral eṉpataṉ tiripēyeṉṟum, aiyantiripaṟa aṟintukoḷka.
cēraṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ mūlam allatu varalāṟu vēṟu vakaiyākavum iruntirukkalām. iṟṟai aṟivu nilaiyil nām kāṭṭakkūṭiya mūlam cāral eṉpatē. uṇmai eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum. mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum tūya tamiḻk kuṭikaḷā yiruntatu pōṉṟē. ak kuṭippeyarkaḷum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷākavē iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatai maṭṭum uḷattil uṟutiyāy irut(tu)tal vēṇṭum. mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷil kakaram cakaramāvatē iyalpu.
e-ṭu: birk - birch, Kirk - church, whilk - which - sik - such, bik bitch, caster - chester, particle - parcel, kenotopion - cenotaph.
iv vāriya moḻiyiyalpai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭē, tamiḻilum avvāṟu kakaramē cakaramākumeṉak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. mēlai moḻi nūlār, tamiḻ vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu vantateṉa aṭip paṭaiyil avar tavaṟiṉatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. tamiḻ āriyat tiṟku muntiyatum mūlamumātaliṉ moḻinūl tiṟavukōl tamiḻi lēyē uḷḷatu. itai avar aṟiyār. aṟiyuṅkālam aṇuki varukiṉṟatu. (irā.pi. cētuppiḷḷai maṇiviḻā malar 1961)
kaccakkāyc cilli viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar: 
kaccakkāyaic cilliyāl aṭittu āṭum āṭṭu kaccakkāyc cilli.
āṭuvār tokai: 
palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi: 
āḷukkup pattiṟkuk kuṟaiyāta pala kaccakkāykaḷum, akaṉṟa cilliyum, ōraṭi viṭṭamuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamum, itai āṭu karuvi yām. cillikku vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaṭṭattil cappātti eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam: 
poṭṭalil itu āṭap peṟum.
āṭumuṟai: 
āṭakar nāṟkacat tolaiviluḷḷa uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ tattam cilliyai eṟivar. yāruṭaiyatu vaṭṭat tiṟku allatu vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviṟku mikaneruṅki yirukkiṉ ṟatō, avar muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. ēṉaiyō rellām tattam aṇmai muṟaippaṭi muṉ piṉṉāka āṭuvar.
āṭakarellārum tattam kaccakkāykaḷai vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ iṭṭa piṉ, ovvoruvarum uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu, vaṭṭat tiṟkuḷ parappi allatu kuvittu vaikkappaṭṭirukkum kaccak kāy kaḷait tattam cilliyāl aṭittal vēṇṭum. vaṭṭattiṟku veḷic ceṉṟa kāykaḷaiyellām āṭuvōr eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.
cilli vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ cellāviṭiṉum, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ceṉṟum kāykaḷai aṭittu veḷiyēṟṟāviṭiṉum, kāykaḷai veḷiyēṟṟiyakkālum cilliyum uṭaṉ veḷiyēṟāviṭiṉum, tavaṟām. tavaṟiviṭiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum; tavaṟāviṭiṉ toṭarntu āṭalām.
iṟutiyil, mikutiyāṉa kāykaḷai vaittiruppavar kelittavarāvar.
cēlam vaṭṭārattil kaccakkāykkup patilākac ciṟu curuṭṭup peṭṭiyaiyum vaittu āṭuvatuṇṭu. atu cikareṭpākuc cilli eṉappaṭum.  (ta.nā.vi.)

kaṭavuḷ 
māntaṉ iyaṟṟa muṭiyāta iyaṟkaiyai iyaṟṟiya oru talaivaṉ iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟum avaṉ ellāvaṟṟaiyum kaṭantavaṉ eṉṟum kaṇṭu allatu koṇṭu avaṉaik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟaṉar mutaṟṟamiḻar.
(kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyarkku ellāvaṟṟaiyum iyakkupavaṉ allatu celuttupavaṉ eṉṟum poruḷuraikkalām. kaṭavutal - celuttutal) col. 33
kaṭavuḷ
ciṟu teyvam - immaip payaṉaiyē aḷippavaṉākak karutap peṟum iṭatteyvaṅkaḷum, pūtat teyvaṅkaḷum, naṭukal teyvaṅkaḷum.
peruntēvaṉ - immaiyil ivvulaka iṉpattōṭu maṟumaiyil vīṭṭaiyum aruḷum iṟaivaṉ. ellāvaṟṟaiyum kaṭantu niṟkum kaṭavuḷ. (ti.ma.14.)
kaṭavuḷ uṇṭā?
kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpārum illai yeṉpārum, toṉṟu toṭṭu ulakil iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. uṇṭeṉpārē iṉṟum perumpālarēṉum, illai yeṉpār tokai varavara vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu.
kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ
(1)	katiravak kuṭumpattaic cērnta kōḷkaḷ ellām, iṭaiyaṟātu ōr oḻuṅkāka iyaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
	ōr ūril ūrkāvalō araciyalāṭciyō ciṟitu nēram illāviṭiṉum, kalakamuṅ koḷḷaiyum kolaiyum nērkiṉṟaṉa. uyiraṟṟa nāḷum kōḷum pāvaiyāṭṭup pōl oḻuṅkāka āṭivariṉ, avaṟṟai āṭṭum ōr āṟṟal iruttal vēṇṭum. av vāṟṟal aṟivaṟṟa tāyirukka muṭiyātu. av vaṟivē iṟaivaṉ.
(2)	iv vulakam muḻuvataṟkum, katiravaṉ pakal viḷakkākavum tiṅkaḷ irā viḷakkākavum eṇṇiṟkum eṭṭāta kālattiliruntu viḷaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
	oru vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟi vaippatu atiṟ kuṭiyirukkum makkaṭkē. makkaḷillā vīṭṭil viḷakkut tāṉākat tōṉṟi eriyātu. pala vulakaṅkaṭkum iru cuṭaraiyum viḷakkāka ēṟpaṭuttiyavaṉ oruvaṉ iruttal vēṇṭum.
	vēlai ceyyāta tūkka vēḷaiyākiya irākkālattiṟku, veppamāṉa neruppoḷi viḷakkākātu kuḷirnta niḻaloḷi viḷakkāka viruppatum, kavaṉikkattakkatu.
(3)	piṟa kōḷkaḷaip pōṟ cuṟṟātu orē yiṭattilirukkum katiravaṉ, pattut ticaiyum oḷi camamākap paravumāṟu uruṇṭaiyā yiruppatum, aḷaviṭap paṭāta nīḷ peruṅ kālam erintu variṉum ataṉ eriyāvi kuṉṟi yaṇai yātiruppatum, iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa iṟumpūtuc ceyti yātalāl, atai yiyakki yāḷum oru param poruḷ iruttal vēṇṭum.
(4)	kōḷkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭātu taṉ taṉ pātai vaṭṭattil iyaṅkumāṟum, ivai cuḻaluṅkāl avaṟṟiṉ mēluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ nīṅkāvāṟum, ovvoṉṟaiyuñ cūḻa oru kavarcci maṇṭalam amaintiruppatum, iyaṟkaikku appāṟ paṭṭa ōr āṟṟaliṉ amaippē.
(5)	kālamum iṭamum toṭakkamum īṟum illātavai yātalāl, iṟṟai makkaḷulakan tōṉṟumuṉ, eṇṇikkai yaṟṟa uyirulakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi yaḻintiruttal vēṇṭum. itait tāṉ,
	paṭaittu viḷaiyāṭum paṇpi ṉōṉum
	tuṭaittut tuyartīr tōṟṟat tōṉum
	taṉṉil vēṟu tāṉoṉ ṟilōṉum
	aṉṉōṉ iṟaiva ṉākumeṉ ṟuraittaṉaṉ
	- maṇimēkalaikku aṟivuṟuttiya civaṉiyat tarukki  (caivavāti).
(6)	māntaṉ tōṉṟi aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭāyiṟṟeṉa vaittuk koḷḷiṉum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa talai muṟaikaḷ kaḻintiruttal vēṇṭum. pattukkaṇakkākat toṭaṅkiya makkaṭ tokai iṉṟu nūṟu kōṭik kaṇakkākap perukiyuḷḷatu. ovvoru talai muṟai yilum ettaṉaiyarāyiṉum, attaṉaiyarum aṭaiyāḷaṅ kāṇumāṟu vevvēṟu mukavaṭiviluḷḷaṉar. kai varaiyum vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu aṟivu nirampiya oru pērāṟṟaliṉ ceyalē yākum.
(7) 	kaṭavuḷai nampiṉōr kaiviṭap paṭār. eṉpatu, iṉṟum cilar vāḻkkaiyil meyppikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(8) 	uṭalnalam, maṉanalam, matinalam mutaliya nalaṅkaḷ uḷḷārum illārum paṭaikkappaṭṭiruttal.
(9)	pañcam, koḷḷainōy, peru veḷḷam, nila naṭukkam mutaliya iyaṟkai aḻivu nikaḻccikaḷ nērtal.
kaṭavuḷ illai yeṉpataṟkuk kāṭṭappaṭum cāṉṟukaḷ
(1) 	kaṭavuḷ puṟak kaṇṇiṟkup pulaṉāvatillai.
(2) 	oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muraṇpaṭṭa palvēṟu mataṅkaḷ ulakil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
(3) 	nallōr palar, vaṟumai, nōy, piṟarāl tuṉpam mutaliya vaṟṟāl varuntik kuṟuvāḻkkaiyarāyccāka, tīyōr palar ellā vakaiyilum iṉpuṟṟu nīṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.
(4)	pala aḵṟiṇai uyiriṉaṅkaḷ piṟavaṟṟaik koṉṟu tiṉpaṉavākavē paṭaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(5)	cilar ettuṇai urukkamāy iṟaivaṉai vēṇṭiṉum, tām virumpiyataip peṟuvatillai.
iṅṅaṉam, kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpataṟkum illai yeṉpataṟkum kāṭṭappaṭum cāṉṟukaḷuḷ uṇṭeṉpataṟ kuriyavaiyē mikuntum valimaiyuḷḷaṉavākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. kāṭci yaḷavai pōṉṟē karuttaḷavaiyum uṇmaiyaṟiyum vaḻiyākum.
kaṭavuḷ eṅkum niṟaintu āvi vaṭiviliruppatāl, avarai oruvaṉum puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇa muṭiyātu. muraṇpaṭṭa mataṅkaḷ māntar paṭaippu. nallōrkku maṟumaiyil nal vāḻvirukkalām. pal piṟavi nampikkaiyāḷar nallōr tuṉpattaip paḻaviṉaip payaṉ eṉpar.
māntaṉ matiyāṟṟal maṭṭiṭṭatātaliṉ, iṟaivaṉ āṭci yiluḷḷa ellāvaṟṟaiyum aṟiya muṭiyātu.
āḻa vamukki mukakkiṉum āḻkaṭalil
nāḻi mukavātu nāṉāḻi.  (mūturai. 19).
nallōrkku nērum tīṅkukaṭku, avar paḻampiṟappiṟceyta tīviṉaikaḷaik karaṇiyamākak kāṭṭuvar koṇ muṭipāḷar.
eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, iru cārārum tat tam koḷkaiyai etirk koḷkaiyār nampumāṟu nāṭṭamuṭiyā tiruppatāl, karuttu vēṟupāṭṭiṟ kiṭan tantu, oru cārārai yoru cārār paḻikkātum pakaikkātumiruppatē uṇmaiyāṉa pakut taṟivām.
mēlum, kaṭavuḷ uṇmaiyāka illaiyeṉiṉ, nampāta vaṉukkuk kēṭillai. orukāl, uḷḷāreṉiṉ avaṉukkuk kēṭuṇṭām. ātalāl, nampā matattiṉarum nampu matattaik kaṭaippiṭippatē cālac ciṟantatām.
kaṭākam
kaṭākam - kaṭāha
kaṭakam - kaṭākam = vaṭṭamāṉa kopparai, peruveḷik kōḷakai. (va.va: 103)
kaṭi
kaṭi-kāt (khad) - i.vē. 
kaṭittal = pallāl veṭṭutal, kaṭittut tiṉṉutal. (va.va: 103)
kaṭikai1
kaṭikai1 - kaṭa, kaṭī, kaṭikā (gh, t)
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = kuḻi, āḻam. kuṇṭu - kuṇṭāṉ = kuḻinta allatu kuṇṭāṉa kalam. kuṇṭāṉ - kuṇṭā.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭikai - kuṭikai - kuṭukkai - kuṭuvai.
kuṭikai = nīrkkalam (kamaṇṭalam).
arumpuṉaṟ kuṭikai mītu  (kantapu. kāviri. 49)
kuṭikai - kaṭikai = nīrkkalam, nāḻikai vaṭṭil, nāḻikai, maṅkala nāḻikai kuṟikkum kaṇiyaṉ, maṅkalap pāṭakaṉ.
kaṭikai - kaṭiñai. vaṭavar kāṭṭum kaṭa (gh) eṉṉum mūlam kuṭam eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripē.
kaṭikai + āram = kaṭikaiyāram - kaṭikāram.
āram eṉpatu ōr īṟu.
o.nō: kūṭāram, koṭṭāram, vaṭṭāram.
kaṭīyantira, kaṭikā yantira eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ puṇarppiṉiṉṟu kaṭikāram eṉṉum teṉcol vantataṉṟu.
kaṭikai2 - 
kaṭa, kaṭā (gh, t) kuṭi = kulam, kūṭṭam. kuṭi - kuṭikai - kaṭikai = ciṟu kūṭṭam, ūravai.
iṅkuk kai eṉpatu ciṟumaipporuṭ piṉṉoṭṭu (dim.)
vaṭavar kāṭṭum kaṭ (gh) eṉṉum mūlam toṭarpaṟṟa palvēṟu poruḷkoṇṭa col. kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil atu kuṭa eṉṉum teṉcoṟṟiripākum.
kul - kuḷ - kuḻu - kuḻa - kuṭa - kuṭam = tiraṭci. kuṭattal = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal. (va.va: 103-104)
kaṭicol
kaṭico lillai kālattup paṭiṉē  (ecca. 56)
eṉpatu oru tolkāppiyac cūttiram.
itaṟkuc cēṉāvaraiyar urai varumāṟu
itaṉ poruḷ: ivai toṉṟutoṭṭaṉa vallaṉaveṉṟu kaṭiyappaṭuñ collillai; avvak kālattut tōṉṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭu māyiṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: campu, caḷḷai, caṭṭi, camaḻppu eṉa varum. ivai toṉṟutoṭṭu vantaṉavāyiṉ. mutalākātaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ
cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai auveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē  (tol. eḻuttu. 62)
eṉa vilakkār āciriyar: ataṉāṉ avai piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya collēyāmeṉpatu.
iḵtu eḻuvakai vaḻuvamaitiyuḷ oṉṟākātu ōr pātukāva lātaliṟ kiḷaviyākkat tiyaipiṉmaiyāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉā reṉpatu.
iṉi oru cārārurai: iṉṉa anuvatikkuṅ kālamā makkālattu, avai vaḻuvaṉmai ellā āciriyarkkum uṭampāṭākaliṉ, ataṉait taḻuvik koṇṭavāṟeṉka. ivai yiraṇṭum ic cūttirattukkup poruḷākak koḷka.
iṉi oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṟ putiyaṉa tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōlap paḻaiyaṉa keṭuvaṉavum uḷaveṉak koḷka. avai aḻāṉ puḻāṉ mutaliyaṉavum, eḻuttiṟ puṇarnta coṟkaḷ ikkālattu vaḻaṅkātaṉavumām.
taṉṉuraiyum maṉṉuraiyumākac cēṉāvaraiyar kūṟiyaīrurai kaḷuḷ, muṉṉuraiyē cūttirattiṟkup poruntiya uṇmai yuraiyām.
campu caḷḷai caṭṭi camaḻppu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, tol kāppiyarāṟ koḷḷappaṭāviṭiṉum allatu tolkāppiyar kālattu vaḻaṅkavēyillai yeṉṟu (orucārār koḷkaippaṭi) koḷḷiṉum. avai tūya teṉcoṟkaḷ eṉpatai evarum maṟukkamuṭiyātu.
campu eṉpatu oruvakaik kōrai. camputal eṉpatu erital. campu - cāmpu - cāmpal. cāmpaṉ - cāmpāṉ = piṇattaic cuṭupavaṉ.
avvak kālattil tōṉṟum putuc coṟkaḷait taḻuvik koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṉum vitiyaik kūṟum vēṟoru tolkāppiyac cūttiram.
kuraṅkiṉ ēṟṟiṉaik kaṭuvaṉ eṉṟalum
marampayil kūkaiyaik kōṭṭāṉ eṉṟalum
cevvāyk kiḷiyait tattai eṉṟalum
vevvāy verukiṉaip pūcai eṉṟalum
kutiraiyuḷ āṇiṉaic cēval eṉṟalum
 iruḷniṟap paṉṟiyai ēṉam eṉṟalum
 erumaiyuḷ āṇiṉaik kaṇṭi eṉṟalum
 muṭiya vanta avvaḻak kuṇmaiyiṉ
 kaṭiya lākā kaṭaṉaṟin tōrkkē  (marapu. 69)
eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ veḷippaṭai itaṉ uraiyil. pērāciriyar,
 kaṭaṉaṟintōr eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaḻakkiṉuñ ceyyuḷiṉum avai vantamaiyiṟ kaṭappāṭaṟivōrkkuk kaṭiyalākāteṉṟa vāṟu. iṉṉum ipparikārattālē kōḻiyai vāraṇameṉṟa lum, verukiṉai viṭaiyeṉṟalum pōlvaṉa palavuṅ koḷka. avai,
kāṉa vāraṇa mīṉuṅ
kāṭāki viḷiyu nāṭuṭai yōrē  (puṟam. 52)
vāraṇak koṭiyoṭu vayiṟpaṭa niṟīi  (tirumuruku. 20)
verukku viṭaiyaṉṉa veruṇōkkuk kayantalai  (puṟam. 324)
 eṉa varum, eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ic cūttirattilum itaṉuraiyilum kāṭṭappeṟṟa coṟkaḷ yāvum tūya tamiḻ eṉpatu veḷippaṭai.
kaṭuvaṉ = kaṭumaiyāṉatu. kōṭṭāṉ = kōṭṭil vacippatu.
kōṭu = marakkiḷai. tattai = ilai kāy kaṉiyoṭu tottik koṇṭu kiṭappatu.
tottutal = oṭṭutal. uṭumīṉ tottap polikaṉa kakkiri (kamparā. piramāt. 117)
tottutal = toṅkutal. ceccaik kaṇṭat tottūṉ pōla (ñāṉā. pāyi. 5, 12)
 iv viru poruḷum tattaip peyarkkup poruntum. tottu - tottai - tattai.
pūcai eṉpatu vīṭṭuppūṉai. pūcu - pūcai - pūṉai - pūñai. nilattiṟ pūciṉāṟpōla metteṉṟu naṭappatu pūcai. pūṉaiyaip pūcu pūcu eṉṟaḻaippatu nellai vaḻakku.
cēval eṉpatu potuvāy āṇpaṟavaiyaik kuṟippiṉum, āṇ vilaṅkaiyum kuṟippataṟkuc ciṟitum vilakkillai. cēval eṉpataṉ mūlamāṉa cē eṉṉuñ col vilaṅkiṉ āṇaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
cē = kāḷai. cēṅkaṉṟu = kāḷaṅkaṉṟu. āṇkaṉṟu. cē - cēvu - cēval.
ēṉam = kariya vilaṅku. paṉṟi. irumai = karumai. iru - eru - eru mai - kariya māṭu. iru - iṉu - eṉu - ēṉu - ēṉam. ēṉu - ēṉai yāṉai. teluṅkil erumaiyai eṉumu eṉṟum, yāṉaiyai ēṉuka eṉṟum kūṟutal kāṇka. yāṉaiyai ēṉaiyeṉṟu iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭuk kuṭiyāṉavar kūṟuvar.
kaṇṭi = kaṭumaiyāṉatu. kaṇṭam = kaṭumai, koṭumai. kaṇṭaṉ = koṭiyavaṉ. āṇerumai mikak koṭiyatu. ataṉ koṭu maiyai iyaṟkainilaiyiltāṉ kāṇamuṭiyum. puliyiṉum maṭaṅkaliṉum kāṭṭerumai koṭitā yiruttalpaṟṟiyē. atu kūṟṟuvaṉukku ūrtiyākak kūṟappaṭṭateṉka. erumaimaṟam eṉṟu puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālaiyum (7: 13).
oruva ṉoruvaṉai yuṭaipaṭai pukkuk
kūḻai tāṅkiya erumaiyum  (poruḷ. 72)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamum kūṟum puṟattuṟaiyai nōkkuka.
vāraṇam = nilattaiyum kuppaiyaiyum vārik kiḷaippatu. kōḻi eṉṉum peyarum ip poruḷuṭaiyatē.
viṭai = paruttatu. parutta āṇvilaṅku. viṭaittal = paruttal. vilaṅkilum paṟavaiyilum āṇ paruttiruttalaik kāṇka.
iccoṟkaḷellām tūya teṉcoṟkaḷāyiruttalālēyē kaṭiya lākā kaṭaṉaṟin tōrkkē eṉṟār tolkāppiyar. itaṉāl, avvak kālattut tōṉṟum putupputu coṟkaḷel lām tūya teṉ coṟkaḷāyiruntāl, avaṟṟai vilakkutal kūṭātu eṉpatē. kaṭico lillai kālattup paṭiṉē eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ poruḷākum.
paḻaiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum
vaḻuvala kāla vakaiyi ṉāṉē  (naṉ. 452)
eṉṉum poruḷum ituvē.
ikkālattut tōṉṟik kaṭiyalākāc coṟkaḷ. maḷikai (palacarakkuk kaṭai). ceyalāḷaṉ, coṟpoḻivu, mānāṭu, tērvu (parīṭcai) mutaliyaṉa.
māntar eṉṉum colliṉ orumai vaṭivam vaḻaṅkiya paṇṭai nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭamaiyiṉ, cila pulavar māntaṉ eṉṉum collai ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟilar. palarpāṟ colliṉiṉṟu orumaic collai vakuttuk koḷvataṟkup pulamai vēṇṭuva tillai; pakuttaṟivē pōtiyatākum.
cilar pulavar. allatu pulavar cilar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya paṇṭai vaḻakku vīḻntu. iṉṟu cila pulavar eṉṉum vaḻakku eḻun tuḷḷatu. cila eṉṉum col aḵṟiṇai vaṭiviṉa teṉṉuṅ kāraṇattiṉāl, aṟiñar cilar ataṉai ēṟkiṉṟilar. āyiṉum, nalla, periya, vanta, pōṉa mutaliya akaravīṟṟuk kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷum terinilaip peyareccaṅkaḷum pōla, cila pala mutaliya akaravīṟṟuk kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷum koḷḷap paṭuvataṟkut taṭai yillai yeṉka.
ivaiyum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavumē kaṭico lillai kālattup paṭiṉē eṉṉum cūttirattāl taḻuvappaṭumēyaṉṟi, `capāṣ, `calām eṉṟa coṟkaḷum ivaipōṉṟa piṟavumalla eṉpatu, tamiḻarum tamiḻ māṇavarum, tamiḻp pulavarum kavaṉittaṟi vārāka.
capāṣ calām eṉṟa urutuc coṟkaḷ iṟṟaikku 300, 400 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē ciṟanta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cila pulavar karutukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻil coṟkaḷ taḻuvap peṟutaṟku avaṟṟiṉ takutiyum tūymaiyum kāraṇamākumēyaṉṟi, avai vaḻaṅkum 300, 400 āṇṭukkālam kāraṇamākātu. aṅṅaṉam kālam kāraṇamāyiṉ. 23 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil taḻuvappaṭumāṟu iṉṟu evarum ettuṇai ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷaiyum tamiḻiṟ pukuttalāmē!
iṉi, capāṣ calām eṉṟa urutuc coṟkaḷait tamiḻiṟ pukuttiyavar pulavar perumakkaḷ (aruṇakirinātarum, kumara kurupararum) eṉṟu oru kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟait taḻuva virumpum tamiḻppulavar.
ittakaiya iṭarppāṭṭu nilaikaḷil, tamiḻ ciṟantatā, tamiḻil vīṇāka ayaṟcoṟkaḷaip pukutti ataṉ tūymaiyaik kulaikkum pulavar ciṟantavarā eṉṟu nōkkutal vēṇṭum. tamiḻppulavariṉum tamiḻ ciṟantatu eṉpatai evarum maṟukkār.
mēlum, aruṇakirinātarum kumarakurupararum irunta kālam collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum illāta kālam. vaṭa moḻi uṇmaiyil tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum, tamiḻ ataṉ kiḷai moḻiyeṉṟum, vaṭacoṟkaḷ kalappatu tamiḻukkup perumai eṉṟum karutappaṭṭa kālam; pala tuṟaikaḷil pakuttaṟivaic caṟṟum payaṉpaṭuttātu tamiḻaṉ taṉmāṉa maṟṟirunta kālam. it takaiya nilaiyil vēṇṭātu pukuttap peṟṟa ayaṟcoṟkaḷai aḷavaiyākak koḷvatu, tamiḻaṟiñarkkuc caṟṟum poruntātu.
emmoḻiyilāyiṉum oruporuṭku allatu karuttiṟkuc collirukkumpōtu, atai nekiḻaviṭṭu ataṟkīṭāka ayaṟ collai mēṟ koḷvatu aṟivuṭaimaiyākātu. tamiḻukkō iv varampu mikak kaṇṭippāṉatu.
ayaṟcoṟkaḷāl maṭṭumaṉṟi, ayaṉmoḻikaḷālum aḷa viṟantu nerukkuṇṭu. iruppatā iṟappatā eṉṉum nilaiyil tamiḻ tattaḷippatu ikkālam. ittakaiya nerukkaṭiyāṉa nilaiyil, poṟuppu vāynta pulavar tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaiyum nilaiyai yum kākkavēṇṭiyatu kaṭaṉ.
tamiḻukkuk kēṭāṉa pala ayalnāṭṭuk karuttukaḷ tamiḻariṉ uḷḷattiṟ kuṭikoḷḷat toṭaṅkuṅkāl, avaṟṟaip peyart teṟivatu tamiḻp pulavar kaṭamaiyāyirukka, aṅṅaṉam ceyyātu avaṟṟai mēṉmēlum vērūṉṟac ceyvatu avarkkut takumā? etu tamiḻ eṉṟum, eṅṅaṉam tamiḻai vaḷarttal vēṇ ṭum eṉṟum, aṟiyātu māṇavar iṭarppaṭum ikkālattil, tamiḻp pulavarē tamiḻait taḷarttaṟku aṭikōlvatu ettakaiya kēṭāṉa ceyal.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmai muṉmai tāymai talaimai mutaliya paṇpu kaḷai aṟitaṟkup piṟkālat tamiḻākiya purāṇat tamiḻum kal veṭṭut tamiḻum uṟutuṇai ceyyā; mēṟkaṇakku nūlkaḷai yum uḻavar tamiḻaiyum nōkkutal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam nōk kātu maṉam pōṉavaḻi pēcuvatum eḻutuvatum. oruvaṉukkup pakaivar avaṉ vīṭṭārē eṉṉum maṟai moḻiyaiyē meyppikkiṉṟatu.
ētēṉum oru putukkaruttut tōṉṟiṉāl allatu koḷḷap paṭṭāl. ataṟku ēṟkeṉavē uḷḷa paḻañcolliṉiṉṟē putuccol tirittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ituvē tiruntiya moḻiyiṟ kaiyāḷa vēṇṭiya neṟimuṟaiyākum.
Radius eṉṉum paḻañ colliṉiṉṟu Radio eṉṉum putuccol, āṅkilattil tirikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
putiya mēlaik kalaikkaruttukaḷai yuṇarttattakka putuc coṟkaḷai ākkikkōṭaṟku vēṇṭiya karuvic coṟkaḷ, tamiḻil nirampavuḷa. putukkaruttukaḷai yuṇarttum putuccoṟ kaḷaip puṉaitaṟkē tamiḻ vaḷampeṟṟirukkumpōtu. paḻaṅ karuttukaḷai yuṇarttum paḻañcoṟkaḷait taḷḷiviṭṭu avaṟṟukkup patilāka ayaṟcoṟkaḷaip pukuttit tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaik kulaittaṟkut tamiḻ eṅṅaṉam iṭantarum!
ākavē, capāṣ calām eṉṟa ayaṟcoṟkaḷāl tamiḻukkup peruntīṅkēyaṉṟic ciṟu naṉmaiyumillai yeṉṟukoḷka. (centamiḻc celvi mē 1949)
kaṭimaram
 paṇṭait tamiḻaracar ovvoru marattaik kaṭimaram allatu kāval maramākak koṇṭu ataip pakaivar aṇṭātapaṭi karuttāyk kāttu vantataṉālum, kā eṉṉum cōlaip peyarkkuk kāttaṟporuḷum iruppatālum, ovvoru tiruppatiyilum kaṭavuḷ uruviṟku niḻal tantu niṟkum maram talamaram eṉap pōṟṟappaṭutaliṉālum, muṟkālattil makkaḷ aṭimutal muṭivarai kiḻaṅkum pālum kīraiyum pūvum kāyum kaṉiyum niḻalum uṟaiyuḷumākap palavakaip payaṉutavik kātta maraṅkaḷaiyum cōlaikaḷaiyum teyvaṅkaḷāka vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉa ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu. (col. 19.)
kaṭu
kaṭu 1 - kaṭ (dd)
kaṭu = vaṉmai (kaṭiṉam), kaṭumai. kaṭṭeṉal = vaṉmaiyā yiruttal. kaṭukkeṉal = vaṉmaiyāyiruttal.
kaṭu2 - kaṭu (t)
 kaḷ = muḷ. kaḷḷi = muṭceṭi. kaḷ - kaṭu = muḷ (tivā).
 kārppu, kaippu, mikuti, vemmai, koṭumai.
 kaṭu - kaṭi = kūrmai, mikuti, kārppu.
vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamillai. krut (veṭṭu) eṉṉum vaṭa col kattu (veṭṭu) eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟiripātaliṉ, mūlamākātu. (t.t.: 104)
kaṭuku
 kaṭuku - kaṭuka (t) - i.vē.
 kaṭu - kaṭuku = kāramuḷḷa poruḷ.
 kaṭuku ciṟuttālum kāram pōkātu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. kaṭuku - kaṭukam.
 ammīṟu peṟṟa vaṭivē īṟukeṭṭu vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷatu. (t.t.: 104)
kaṭaikkaḻakam
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālak kuṟunila maṉṉar
akutai, atti, atiyamāṉ takaṭūr porutu vīḻnta eḻiṉi, atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉañci, atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉañci makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉi, antuvañ cāttaṉ, antuvaṅkīraṉ, ampar kiḻāṉ aruvantai, aviyaṉ, ātaṉaḻici, ātaṉuṅkaṉ, āntai, āmūr mallaṉ, āy aṇṭiraṉ, iyakkaṉ, iruṅkōvēḷ, iḷaṅkaṇṭīrakkō, iḷaṅkumaṇaṉ, iḷaviccikkō, iḷa veḷimāṉ, īrntūr kiḻāṉ tōyaṉ māṟaṉ, eyiṉaṉ, eḻiṉi, ēṟṟai, ēṟaikkōṉ, ēṉāti tirukkiḷḷi, ollaiyūr kiḻāṉ makaṉ peruñcāttaṉ, ōymāṉ nalliyak kōṭaṉ, ōymāṉ villiyātaṉ, kaṅkaṉ, kaṭṭi, kaṭiya neṭuvēṭṭuvaṉ, kaṇṭīrak kōpperu naḷḷi, kantaṉ (nāñciṟ porunaṉ), karumpaṉūr kiḻāṉ, kumaṇaṉ, koṇkāṉaṅ kiḻāṉ, ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, cōḻanāṭṭup piṭavūr kiḻār makaṉ peruñ cāttaṉ, cōḻiyavēṉāti tirukkuṭṭuvaṉ, tantumāṟaṉ, taruma puttiraṉ, taḻumpaṉ, tāmāṉ tōṉṟik kōṉ, teṉ paratavar, tērvaṇ malaiyaṉ, toṇṭaimāṉ, nampi neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ, naṉṉaṉ, naṉṉaṉ cēy naṉṉaṉ, nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉ, nālaikiḻavaṉ 
nākaṉ, neṭuvēḷātaṉ, paḻaiyaṉ, piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ, pulli, pūntuṟai (puṉṟuṟai), poṟaiyāṟu kiḻāṉ, malli kiḻāṉ kāriyāti, malaiyamāṉ cōḻiyavēṉāti tirukkaṇṇaṉ, malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri, mukkāval nāṭṭu āmūr mallaṉ, mutiyaṉ, mūvaṉ, maiyaṟkōmāṉ māvaṉ, mōkūrp paḻaiyaṉ, vallār kiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, valvil ōri, vāṭṭāṟṟeḻiṉi yātaṉ, viccikkōṉ, veḷimāṉ, vēṅkai mārpaṉ, vēḷ evvi, vēḷ pāri, vaiyāvikkōp perum pēkaṉ mutaliyōr.
ivaruḷ, toṇṭaimāṉ naṉṉaṉ mutaliya ciṟṟaracarum, vaḷḷal kaḷum muṭiyaṇintavarāvar. kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattiṟpotuvāka mūvēntar kaikaḷum tāḻntuviṭṭataṉāl, kuṟu nila maṉṉar talaiyeṭuttu muṭiyaṇintu koṇṭaṉar. cēraṉ ceṅkaṭṭuvaṉ ēḻaracarai veṉṟu, avar muṭikaḷai mālaiyāka aṇintirun tāṉ. avveḻuvaruḷ aivar kuṟunila maṉṉar.
vilkeḻu tāṉai vicciyar perumakaṉ
vēntaroṭu poruta ñāṉṟai  (kuṟun. 328)
eṉpatu, kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ vēntarai etirttataik kūṟutal kāṇka .
kuṟunila maṉṉaruṭ peru vaḷḷalkaḷ
atiyamāṉ, āy, ōri, kāri, naḷḷi, pāri, pēkaṉ, kumaṇaṉ, nalliyakkōṭaṉ eṉpavar peruvaḷḷalkaḷ. ivaruḷ mutaleḻuvar ilakkiyap pukaḻ peṟṟavar. avaruḷḷum pāri perum pukaḻ peṟṟavaṉ. avveḻuvar koṭaip poṟaiyai yum avarukkuppiṉ nalliyakkōṭaṉ oruvaṉē tāṅkiṉā ṉeṉṟu, nallūr nattattaṉār pāṭuvar (ciṟupāṇ: 113 - 15). tam tampiyāl nāṭukoḷḷappaṭṭu kāṭu pōntirunta kumaṇaṉ, peruntalaiccāttaṉārkkuk koṭukkat taṉṉiṭam poruḷiṉ maiyāl, taṉ talaiyai veṭṭikkoṇṭu pōyt taṉ tampi yiṭaṅ koṭuttup perum poruḷ peṟumāṟu taṉ vāḷaik koṭuttu,
cātali ṉiṉṉāta tillai yiṉita tūum
īta liyaiyāk kaṭai.  (230)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭāṉāṉ.
iṉi, evvi, naṉṉaṉ, paṇṇaṉ mutaliya vēṟupala vaḷḷalkaḷu miruntaṉar.
ōmpā vīkai māvē ḷevvi  (puṟam. 24.)
icainal līkaik kaḷiṟuvīcu vaṇmakiḻ
pārattut talaivaṉ āra naṉṉaṉ  (akam. 152).
taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuri yāḷaṉ
paṇṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi  (akam. 54)
ivvaḷḷalkaḷ iṉṟēl, kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattiṟ pulavar pāṇar kūttar porunar mutaliya iravalar vāḻntirukkavum, tamiḻum icaiyum kūttum vaḷarntirukkavum, muṭiyāteṉṟē kūṟalām. māperuñ celvarāṉa mūvēntarum, pirāmaṇarkkut toṇṭun tāṉamuñ ceyvatilum, avar ēviya vēḷvikaḷai yellām iyaṟṟuvatilumē, kālattaiyum kuṭikaḷ paṇattai yum celaviṭṭu, ēḻaip pulavark kellām eṭṭāk kaiyarākavē yiruntu vantaṉar.
āriyat tol kataiñar, tamiḻ vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ talaittiṟak koṭaic ciṟappaiyum poṉṟāp pukaḻaiyum maṟaittaṟ poruṭṭu, varaiyātu koṭuppavaṉ talaivaḷḷal eṉṟum, kēṭkak koṭuppavaṉ iṭaivaḷḷal eṉṟum, pukaḻak koṭup pavaṉ kaṭaivaḷḷal eṉṟum, vaḷḷalkaḷai mūvakaippaṭutti, cempiyaṉ mutaliya eḻuvar talaiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷum, akkuraṉ mutaliya eḻuvar iṭaiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷum, āy mutaliya eḻuvar kaṭaiyeḻuvaḷḷalkaḷum, āvar eṉṟu poruttamiṉṟi uṇmaikku māṟāka vakuttuḷḷaṉar. irunta vūrkaḷaiyel lām iravalarukkuk koṭutta āyum pāriyum, pulavaṉukkut talaiyaiyuṅ koṭutta kumaṇaṉaiyum, otta koṭaiyāḷikaḷ varalāṟṟiṟkut terintavarai oruvarumillai.
cempiyaṉai āriyaṉeṉṟu karutik koṇṭu, avaṉait talai yeḻuvaḷḷalkaḷuḷ oruvaṉākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. avaṉ tamiḻa kattuc cōḻaruḷ oruvaṉ iṭaiyeḻuvaḷḷalkaḷuḷ oruva ṉākak koṇṭa kaṉṉaṉaip perumaippaṭutta, koṭaikkuk kaṉṉaṉ eṉṟum, kārttikaik kappāl maḻaiyu millai, karuṇaṉuk kappāl koṭaiyumillai eṉṟum, kūṟuvar. `koṭukkaṉuk kappāl maḻaiyumillai, kumaṇaṉuk kappāl koṭaiyumillai. eṉṟē paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅkal vēṇṭum. koṭukkaṉ = tēḷ, naḷi (kārttikai) mātam. kaṭaikkaḻakak kāla vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ tokaiyai aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭē, ēṉaikkālattār tokaiyaiyum ēḻēḻāka vakuttiruttal kāṇka.
aṇṭiraṉ eṉpatu kaṇṭīraṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟa ōr iyaṟpeyar. tamiḻakattiṉ teṉkōṭiyiluḷḷa potiyamalai nāṭṭai āṇṭa āy aṇṭiraṉ teluṅkaṉallaṉ. aṇṭiraṉ eṉṉuñ coṟku āntiraṉ eṉṉuñ colloṭu toṭarpillai.
vaṭapāṟ kuṟunila maṉṉar
erumaiyūraṉ
iṟṟai maicūr nāṭṭiṉ paḻampeyar erumai nāṭu eṉpatu. erumaikaḷ mikkiruntataṉāl annāṭu appeyar peṟṟatu. ataṉ talainakar erumaiyūr. atilirun tāṇṭavaṉ erumaiyūraṉ. talaiyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻi yaṉāl vellappaṭṭa eḻuvaruḷ erumaiyūraṉum oruvaṉ.
koycuvaṟ puravik koṭittērc ceḻiyaṉ
ālaṅ kāṉat takaṉṟalai civappac
cēral cempiyaṉ ciṉaṅkeḻu titiyaṉ
pōrval yāṉaip polampūṇ eḻiṉi
nārari naṟaviṉ erumaiyūraṉ
tēṅkamaḻ akalattup pularnta cāntiṉ
iruṅkō vēṇmāṉ iyaltērp porunaṉ eṉ
ṟeḻuvar nalvalam aṭaṅka orupakal
muracoṭu veṇkuṭai akappaṭut turaicelak
koṉṟukaḷam vēṭṭa  (akam. 36)
eṉṟu nakkīrar pāṭutal kāṇka.
nērā vaṉtōḷ vaṭukar perumakaṉ
pēricai erumai naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷatai  (akam. 253)
eṉpatil, erumai eṉpatu vaṭukar talaivaṉ oruvaṉiṉ peyaraik kuṟittatēyaṉṟi, avaṉai erumai nāṭṭoṭu toṭarpu paṭuttiya taṉṟu.
erumaikku vaṭamoḻiyil makicam (mahiṣa) eṉṟu peyar. ataṉāl erumaiyūr eṉpataip pirāmaṇar makicapuri eṉa moḻi peyarttaṉar. accol ataṉ āṅkila vaṭivai yoṭṭi iṉṟu maicūr eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
māvā rātē (273) eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaip pāṭiya erumai veḷiyaṉār eṉṉum pulavar, erumaiyūriṉarāvar. iruḷ kiḻip patupōl eṉṉum 72-ām akap pāṭṭaip pāṭiya kaṭalaṉār ivar makaṉār āvar.
naṉṉaṉ
erumai nāṭṭiṟkum kuṭamalaikkum mēṟkiluḷḷa teṉ kaṉṉaṭappakuti, koṇkāṉam eṉṉum peyarkoṇṭa tākum. koṇku tuṟaimukam allatu kaṭaṟkarai. koṇkaṉ neytal nilattalaivaṉ. koṇkāṉam kaṭaṟkarai nilamātalāl ap peyar peṟṟatu pōlum!
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil koṇkāṉattai āṇṭavaṉ naṉṉaṉ. avaṉ talainakar kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil eṉpar. ēḻiṟ kuṉṟam eṉṉum perumalaiyaiyum; pāḻi, pāram, viyalūr, pirampu eṉṉum pērūrkaḷaiyum, uṭaiyatu koṇkāṉam. avvūr kaḷuḷ pāḻi oru vallaraṇ nakar. atu ēḻil malaiyiṉ eḻu kuvaṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa pāḻiyai araṇākak koṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. naṉṉaṉ oru koṭaiyāḷiyākavum iruntāṉ.
poṉpaṭu koṇkāṉa naṉṉaṉ naṉṉāṭ
ṭēḻiṟ kuṉṟam peṟiṉum  (naṟ. 391).
icainal līkaik kaḷiṟuvīcu vaṇmakiḻ
pārattut talaivaṉ āra naṉṉaṉ
ēḻil neṭuvaraip pāḻic cilampil   (akam. 152).
cūḻi yāṉaic cuṭarppūṇ naṉṉaṉ
pāḻi aṉṉa kaṭiyuṭai viyaṉakar  (akam. 15).
naṟavumakiḻ irukkai naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ
vayalai vēli viyalū raṉṉa 	 (akam. 97).
naṉṉaṉ vēḷir marapaic cērntavaṉ eṉpatu, vēṇmāṉ eṉṉum peyarālum,
naṉṉaṉ utiyaṉ aruṅkaṭip pāḻit
toṉmutir vēḷir ōmpiṉar vaitta
poṉ 	(akam. 258)
eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. pāḻip poṉ kavara aṭikkaṭi pōr nikaḻntataṉāl annakar ceruppāḻi eṉap peyar peṟṟatu pōlum!
ēḻilmalai eṉpataip pirāmaṇar catta cailam (sapta caila) eṉa moḻipeyarttaṉar. piṉṉar ēḻilmalai eṉpatu elimalai eṉat tirintapōtu, ēḻilmalai nāṭṭai avar mūcikanāṭu eṉṟaṉar. mūcikam (mūṣika) eṉpatu eliyaik kuṟikkum vaṭacol. pāḻiccilampu eṉpatu pāḻikkal eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. atu iṉṟu pāṭkal eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. viyalūr eṉpatu, iṉṟu kaṉṉaṭamoḻi yiyalpiṟkēṟpap peyilūr (Bailur) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
koṇkāṉattiṉ kaṭalōra vaṭapakuti tuḷunāṭu eṉappaṭum. aṅkut tōkaikkā eṉṟa ūruḷḷatu. mayilkaḷ niṟainta cōlaiyiṉāl avvūr appeyar peṟṟatu.
..................... paṟaikkaṇ pīlit
tōkaik kāviṉ tuḷu nāṭ ṭaṉṉa  (akam. 15).
pāḻic cilampum mayilukkup peyar peṟṟateṉpatu,
ēḻil neṭuvaraip pāḻic cilampiṟ
kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa  (akam. 152)
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. tōkaikkā eṉpatu tōkkā eṉa maruvi, iṉṟu cōkku (jōk) eṉat tirintuḷḷatākap pērā. auvai. cu. turaiccāmip piḷḷaiyār kūṟuvār.
koṇkāṉam eṉpatu kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattilēyē koṅ kaṇam eṉat tirintu viṭṭatu. kokkeṉṟu niṉaittaṉaiyō koṅkaṇavā? eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar maṉaiviyār kūṟiyatākak kataiyuṇmaiyum, koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅ karunāṭar eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟuvataiyum (25: 156), kāṇka.
veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āy eyiṉaṉ
koṇkāṉattiṉ teṉkīḻp pakuti puṉṉāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. atai āṇṭa veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉar marapil vantavaṉ veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āy eyiṉaṉ. avaṉ talainakar vākai.
 vaṇkai eyiṉaṉ vākai yaṉṉa   (puṟam. 351).
 yāma viraviṉ neṭuṅkaṭai niṉṟu
 tēmutir cimaiyak kuṉṟam pāṭum
 nuṇkō lakavunar vēṇṭiṉ veṇkōṭ
 ṭaṇṇal yāṉai yīyum vaṇmakiḻ
 veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āay eyiṉaṉ  (akam. 208)
eṉpataṉāl, avaṉ koṭaittiṟam viḷaṅkum.
patiṟṟuppattiṉ 2-ām patikam, imayavarampaṉ neṭuñ cēralātaṉai, utiyañ cēraṟku veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāḷ nalliṉi īṉṟa makaṉ eṉkiṉṟatu. avvēṇmāḷ veḷiyaṉ marapiṉaḷ pōlum.
piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ
koṇkāṉattiṟkuk kiḻakkil, kuṭamalait toṭariluḷḷa kuvaṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟu kutirai malai. ammalaināṭṭai yāṇṭavaṉ piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ.
aruvi yārkkuṅ kaḻaipayil naṉantalaik
kaṟivaḷa raṭukkattu malarnta kāntaṭ
koḻuṅkiḻaṅku miḷirak kiṇṭik kiḷaiyoṭu
kaṭuṅkaṭ kēḻa luḻuta pūmi
naṉṉāḷ varupata nōkkik kuṟavar
uḻāatu vittiya parūukkuraṟ ciṟutiṉai
muntuviḷai yāṇar nāṭputi tuṇmār
maraiyāṉ kaṟanta nuraikoḷ tīmpāl
māṉṟaṭi puḻukkiya pulavunāṟu kuḻici
vāṉkē ḻirumpuṭai kaḻāa tēṟṟic
cānta viṟakiṉ uvitta puṉkam
kūtaḷaṅ kaviṉiya kuḷavi muṉṟiṟ
ceḻuṅkōḷ vāḻai yakalilaip pakukkum
ūrāk kutiraik kiḻava kūrvēl
naṟainārt toṭutta vēṅkaiyaṅ kaṇṇi
vaṭinavil ampiṉ villōr peruma
kaivaḷ ḷīkaik kaṭumāṉ koṟṟa
vaiyaka varaippil tamiḻakaṅ kēṭpap
poyyāc cennā neḷiya ēttip
pāṭupa eṉpa paricilar nāḷum
īyā maṉṉar nāṇa
vīyātu parantaniṉ vacaiyilvāṉ pukaḻē.  (puṟam. 168),
eṉṉum karuvūrk kantap piḷḷai cāttaṉār pāṭṭum
oḷitikaḻ muttam viṟaliyark kīttu
nārpiḻik koṇṭa veṅkaṭ ṭēṟal
paṇṇamai nalyāḻp pāṇkaṭum parutti  (puṟam. 170)
 eṉṉum uṟaiyūr maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār pāṭṭum,
iṉṟu celiṉun tarumē ciṟuvarai
niṉṟu celiṉun tarumē piṉṉum
muṉṉē tantaṉeṉ eṉṉātu tuṉṉi
vaikaluñ celiṉum poyyala ṉāki
yāmvēṇṭi yāṅkem vaṟuṅkalam niṟaippōṉ
tāṉvēṇṭi yāṅkut taṉṉiṟai yuvappa
aruntoḻil muṭiyarō tiruntuvēṟ koṟṟaṉ
iṉamali kataccēk kaḷaṉoṭu vēṇṭiṉum
kaḷamali nelliṉ kuppai vēṇṭiṉum
aruṅkalam kaḷiṟṟoṭu vēṇṭiṉum peruntakai
piṟarkkum aṉṉa aṟattakai yaṉṉē   (mēṟpaṭi 170)
eṉṉum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār pāṭṭum, kutiraimalaik kuṟavar vāḻkkaiyaiyum koṟṟaṉ koṭait tiṟattaiyum teḷiyak kāṭṭum.
kutiraimalaip pakutiyiluḷḷa vaṭakarai mēṟkarai eṉṉum iṭappeyarkaḷ, iṉṟu paṭakarā markkarā eṉṟu urumāṟi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kutiraimalai nāṭṭut talainakarp peyarāṉa koṟṟaṉ karuvūr eṉpatu, iṉṟum kotta kaṉavūr eṉṟu vaḻaṅki varuvatākap pērā. auvai. cu.tu. kūṟuvar.
iruṅkōvēḷ
koṇkāṉattiṉ vaṭapāliruntatu kaṭampariṉ paṅkaḷanāṭu. avviru nāṭukaṭkum kiḻakkil kuṭamalait toṭariṉ kīḻpāl iruntatu vēḷiratu vēṇāṭu. annāṭṭut tuvarai nakarait talainakarākak koṇṭu iruṅkōvēḷ āṇṭu vantāṉ.
uvarā vīkait tuvarai yāṇṭu
nāṟpat toṉpatu vaḻimuṟai vanta
vēḷiruḷ vēḷē  (puṟam 201)
 eṉṟu kapilar pāṭutal kāṇka.
tuvarai (JthurK¤âu«) iṉṟu erumaiyūr nāṭṭaic cērntatu.
vēmpāyp (Bombay) paitiram (piratēcam) muḻutum muṉ kālattil vēṇāṭā yiruntatu. muṉṉar naicām araiyattaic cērntirunta ellōrā (Ellora), paḻaiya paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷil (cāsaṉaṅkaḷil) vēḷūr eṉṟum vēḷūrakam eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēmpāyp paitirattuc cōḻāpuram (Sholapur) māvaṭṭattil oru nakar vēḷāpuram (Velapur) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭuḷḷatu. akamatu nakar vaṭṭattil vēḷāpuram eṉṟum, pūṉā māvaṭṭattil vēḷakam eṉṟum, pala nakaraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. appaitirattaic cērnta vēḷakam (vēḷ kirāmam (?)) eṉṉum māvaṭṭat talainakar pelkām (Belgaum) eṉṟum, vēḷpaṭṭi eṉṉum ūr pēlhūṭṭi (Bel hutti) eṉṟum, vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. oycaḷa maṉṉariṉ paṭṭappeyarāṉa pellāḷa eṉpatu vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripākavē karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
toṭakkak kālattil, vēṇāṭu marāṭṭiya nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi kūrccarattil paraviyiruntatu. kattiyavār kaccup (Cutch) paitiraṅkaḷil, iṉṟum palavūrkaḷ vēḷā eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa pēlā (Bela) eṉṉum peyar koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. akattiyar tuvārapatip pōntu patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷiruḷ ḷiṭṭāraik koṇṭuvantār eṉṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟum, vētakkālattil marāṭamum kuccaramum pañca tiraviṭattuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭatum, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.
piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya caḷukkiyar vēṇāṭṭai āṇṭata ṉālēyē, vēḷpula varacar caḷukku vēntar. eṉṟu tivākaramum piṅkalamum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṅkar
iṟṟai erumaiyūr (maicūr) nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyai, atāvatu, paṇṭaik kuṇakoṅkiṉ vaṭapākattil oru pakutiyai, kaṅkar eṉṉum marapiṉar kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattilēyē āṇṭu vantaṉar.
tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭuntiṟaṟ kaṅkaṉ kaṭṭi
eṉṟu akappāṭṭum (44),

paṅkaḷar kaṅkar palvēṟ kaṭṭiyar
 eṉṟu cilappatikāramum (25: 157), kūṟutal kāṇka.
koṅkaṉ eṉṉum peyarē kaṅkaṉ eṉṟu tirintirukkalām.
kaṭṭiyar
kaṅkanāṭṭiṟkuk kiḻakkiliruntu paṇṭaik kuṇakoṅkup pakutiyaik kaṅkar āṇṭuvantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
kullaik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉaiyatu
palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum
eṉṉuṅ kuṟuntokaip pāṭṭai (11) nōkkuka.
vaṭakkiṟ pallavar talaiyeṭutta piṉpum, teṟkil atiyamāṉar kai tāḻnta piṉpumē, kaṅkanāṭum kaṭṭināṭum teṟkē taḷḷi vantiruttal vēṇṭum.

eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, akkālattilēyē aiṅkammum cempum veṇkalamumākiya iru kaṉṉum naṭaipeṟṟamai peṟappaṭum. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,

eṉṟu, iru kaṉṉaiyum vēṟākap pirittu, veṇkalakkaṉṉai vaṭacollāṟ kuṟittār.
pārppār eṉṉum tamiḻccol, kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil illaṟattārāṉa pirāmaṇarkkē varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. ciṟappākat tuṟaviyaraik kuṟikkum antaṇar eṉṉum tamiḻc collum, mutaṟkaṇ pirāmaṇap pōlit tuṟaviyarkku vaḻaṅki, piṉṉar ilvāḻ pirāmaṇarkkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālap pārppaṉar nilai
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭup pārppaṉar nilai pūcāriyamākum.
cāli yorumīṉ takaiyāḷaik kōvalaṉ
māmutu pārppāṉ maṟaivaḻi kāṭṭiṭat
tīvalañ ceyvatu....  (cilap. 1: 51-3)
eṉṟu kūṟatal kāṇka.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭut talaimaipūṇṭa cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukku, aḻumpilvēḷ, villavaṉ kōtai eṉṉum iru tamiḻar muṟaiyē, amaiccaṉum, paṭait talaivaṉumā yiruntaṉar. uḻavu, kaittoḻil, vāṇikam, aracu, kalvi mutaliya pala toḻilum, tamiḻarālēyē (tamiḻil) naṭattappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ākaiyāl, pārppaṉar tuṇai aṟṟait tamiḻarkkuc ciṟitum vēṇṭiyatāyillai.
āvum āṉiyaṟ pārppaṉa mākkaḷum
peṇṭirum piṇiyuṭai yīrum pēṇit
teṉpula vāḻnark karuṅkaṭa ṉiṟukkum
poṉpōṟ putalvarp peṟāa tīrum
emmampu kaṭiviṭutu nummaraṇ cērmiṉ 	 (puṟam. 9: 1-5)
eṉṉum aṭikaḷ, pārppaṉar maṟamum (vīramum) valimaiyum aṟṟavar eṉṟum, kaḷaṅkamaṟṟavareṉṟum iraṅkattakka vareṉṟum, cilarāl karutappaṭṭataik kuṟikkumēyaṉṟit tūyareṉṟu ellārālum karutappaṭṭatākak kuṟiyātu. pārppaṉar tūyareṉṟu ellārālum karutappaṭṭiruppiṉ. māṇikkavācakarai arimarttaṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ ciṟaiyiṭṭuk kaṭuntaṇṭam ceytirukkamāṭṭāṉ. 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, vaṭamalaiyappap piḷḷaiyiṉ kīḻatikāriyāl nārāyaṇa tīṭci tarum ciṟai ceyyappaṭṭirukkamāṭṭār.
āṉmulai yaṟutta aṟaṉi lōrkkum
māṇiḻai makaḷir karuccitait tōrkkum
pārppārt tappiya koṭumai yōrkkum
vaḻuvāy maruṅkiṟ kaḻuvāyu muḷa   (puṟam. 34: 1-4)
eṉavarum aṭikaḷum mēṟkūṟiya karuttaiyē terivikkum. pārppārt tappiṉavarkkum kaḻuvāyuḷatu eṉṟu kūṟiyirut talaik kavaṉikka. muraṭṭuk kuṇamiṉṟi amaitiyāyirukkum ellā uyirp poruḷkaḷaiyum tamiḻar orutiṟamā yeṇṇik kāppar. pūṉaiyaiyum kaḻutaiyaiyum kolvatu peruntīviṉai eṉṟu karutappaṭuvatai nōkkuka. pārppaṉar akkālattil mikac ciṟiya tokaiyiṉarāyum, eḷiyavarāyum, āṭciyil iṭam peṟātavarāyum iruntatiṉāl, tammaik kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa cāṉṟōrākak kāṭṭik koṇṭaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ palvēḷvi(yāka)c cālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti yiṉ ceyalālum, palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ ceyalā lum, āriya vēḷvikaḷai vēṭkum vaḻakkam kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattilēyē tamiḻnāṭṭil vērūṉṟiviṭṭatai yaṟiyalām.
pālaik kautamaṉār eṉṉum pārppaṉar, palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ utaviyāl, tāmum tam maṉaiviyum tuṟakkam (cuvarkkam) pukavēṇṭumeṉṟu, oṉpatu peruvēḷvi vēṭṭup pattām vēḷviyil tam maṉaiviyuṭaṉ maṟaintu pōṉār eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattuc ceyti ārāyattakkatu.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kāla vāṇika vaḷarcci
nilavaṇikamum nīrvaṇikamum kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattiṟ peru vaḷarcci yaṭaintiruntatu.
caṭṭaiyaṇintavarum pātakkūṭu (boots) māṭṭiyavarumāṉa mēlaiyāciyarum mēṉāṭṭārum, miḷakup potikaḷ koṇṭu cellum kōvēṟu kaḻutaiccāttoṭu kūṭi, malaipaṭu celvamuṅ kaṭalpaṭucelvamumāṉa pala arum poruḷkaḷai, aracaṉ niṟuttiya viṟpaṭaiñar iravum pakaluṅ kāttirukkum cuṅkap peruvaḻikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu, viṟṟuttirintaṉar.

villuṭai vaippiṉ viyaṉkāṭ ṭiyaviṉ  (perumpāṇ: 67 - 82).

kaṭāram (parmā) malaiyā mutaliya kīḻaināṭukaṭkum cumaturai cāli (cāvakam) mutaliya kīḻaittīvukaṭkum, nīrvāṇikar ceṉṟu vaṇikam ceytu vantaṉar. avar ikkālattu nāṭṭuk kōṭṭaic ceṭṭimār pōl taṅkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷai iṅkēyē viṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉar. ivvaḻakkam ki.mu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉarē iruntateṉpatu,
munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūuvō ṭillai.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (980) aṟiyappaṭum.
kīḻkaraiyilirunta koṟkai toṇṭi pukār mutaliya tuṟai nakaraṅkaḷuḷ, pukār mikap peritākavum ulakilēyē talaiciṟanta tākavum iruntatu. atu kāvirik kayavāyil amainta aḻakiya tuṟai nakaramātalāl kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉṟum, avvāṟu kaṭaliṟpukum iṭattiliruntataṉāl pukār eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟatu. tuṟai nakaraip paṭṭiṉam eṉpatu paṇṭai vaḻakku. pati - pataṉam - pattaṉam - paṭṭaṉam (paṭṭaṇam) -paṭṭiṉam.
kayavāy maruṅkiṟ kāṇpōrt taṭukkum
payaṉaṟa vaṟiyā yavaṉar irukkaiyum
kalantaru tiruviṟ pulampeyar mākkaḷ
kalantirun tuṟaiyum ilaṅkunīr varaippum  (cilap. 5: 9-12)
moḻipeyar tēttōr oḻiyā viḷakkamum  (mēṟpaṭi 6: 143)
eṉpavaṟṟāl, ulakeṅkaṇumuḷḷa palvēṟu nāṭṭumakkaḷ, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil, taṅkaḷ nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ taṅkiyiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum. yavaṉareṉpār kirēkkarum urōmarum.
mēlkaraiyilirunta muciṟi toṇṭi māntai mutaliya tuṟai nakarkaḷuḷ, cuḷḷiyam pēriyāṟṟuk kayavāyilirunta muciṟi ciṟantatāyiruntatu.
cuḷḷiyam pēriyāṟṟu veṇṇurai kalaṅka
yavaṉar tanta viṉaimāṇ naṉkalam
poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarum
vaḷaṅkeḻu muciṟi   (akam. 149).
kaṭaikkaḻakat toṭakkam
(ki.mu. 5 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
maturai mānakar amaikkappaṭṭavuṭaṉ, mūṉṟām pulavar kaḻaka mum tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu. nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar uṟuppiṉa rāyiṉar. mukkaḻakattilum uṟuppiṉar tokaikku varaiyaṟai illai. avvakkālattil talaiyāya pulavar allatu ciṟanta pulavar ettaṉaivarō attaṉaivarum iṭam peṟṟaṉar. putitāka yārēṉum ciṟanta pulavar variṉum cērkkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉaiyē, takunta pulavar variṉ kaḻakappalakai tāṉē oru muḻam nīṇṭu iṭantarum, eṉṉum marapurai kuṟikkum. nāṟpat toṉpatu eṉṉun tokai ēḻē ḻeṉṟu vakukkappaṭuvatāl, moḻip paṟṟum mūppum pulamaittiṟamum nōkkātu, matam paṟṟiyō kulam paṟṟiyō nilam paṟṟiyō ēḻeḻuvar tokukkap paṭṭaṉar eṉak karutaṟka. ettaṉaivar variṉum ēṟṟup pōṟṟaṟ kēṟṟa celvam pāṇṭiyaṉiṭattiruntatu. mukkaḻakattilum pulavar tokaiyai oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇil muṭittatu, ōriṭattai iṟaivaṉukku otukkavēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ koḷkai paṟṟiyē.
 ciṟaivāṉ puṉaṟṟillaic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cintai yuḷḷum
 uṟaivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭali lāyntavoṇṭīntamiḻ
eṉṟu māṇikka vācakar pāṭiyatu ikkaruttup paṟṟiyē. akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum kaṭaik kaḻaka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷāyiruntaṉa.
iṭaik kaḻakattiṟkum kaṭaikkaḻakattiṟkum īrāyirattain nūṟāṇṭiṟku mēl iṭaiyīṭupaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, iṭaik kaḻakat tiṟutip pāṇṭiyaṉāṉa muṭattiru māṟaṉē kaṭaikkaḻaka mutaṟ pāṇṭiyaṉāṉāṉ eṉpatu poruntātu. kaḻaka irukkaiyai iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai uttara maturai yeṉṟu kūṟiyatu, teṉmaturai yoṭu oppunōkkiyeṉ ṟaṟika.
kaḻakattiṟkut tokai yeṉṟum kūṭal eṉṟum peyarkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ataṉpiṉ, iṭavaṉāku peyarāka, maturaiyum kūṭaleṉap paṭṭu, māṭac ciṟappāl māṭakkūṭal, nāṉmāṭak kūṭal eṉṉum vaḻakkeḻuntatu. māṭaṅkaḷ mukilpaṭiyumāṟu vāṉaḷāva vuyarntiruntataṉāl, nāṉ mukiṉ māṭak kūṭal eṉṟum pulavar pukaḻntu pāṭiṉar. attoṭarait tolkatai yāḷar payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭu, maturaimēṟ kaṭumaḻai poḻinta nāṉmukilkaḷai nāṉmāṭaṅkaḷ kūṭittaṭuttaṉa veṉṟu katai puṉaintuviṭṭaṉar.
maturaik kōṭṭai vāyil oṉṟaṉ muṉ, oru mūtāla maram paṭarntōṅkiyiruntataṉāl, avviṭam ālavāy eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntirukkalām. appeyaraiyum poruḷ tirittu, pāmpiṉāl ellai kāṭṭappaṭṭaviṭameṉṟu katai kaṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. ālavāy eṉpatu mutalil nāṉmāṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ peyarākavē yiruntatu. nāṉmāṭap peyarkaḷai, avai tiruvālavāy tirunaḷḷāṟu tirumuṭaṅkai tirunaṭuvūr. iṉik kaṉṉikariyamāl kāḷi ālavāy eṉṟumām eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka. (kali. 92 urai).
kaṭaikkaḻaka muṭivu
ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ukkirap peruvaḻuti kālattil, pāṇṭiya varacum kaṭaik kaḻakamum kulaintaṉa.
10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa paṭṭiṉattu aṭikaḷ kaḻakattaik kuṟikkum caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭacollum periya caṅkaik kuṟikkum caṅkam eṉṉum teṉcollum vaṭivottirup patāl, caṅkaip payaṉpaṭuttum kālam nōkki atai mūṉṟākki,
mutaṟcaṅ kamutūṭṭum moykuḻalā rācai
naṭuccaṅkam nalvilaṅku pūṭṭum  kaṭaiccaṅkam
āmpō tatuvūtum ammaṭṭō immaṭṭō
nāmpūmi vāḻnta nalam.  (potu)
eṉṉum veṇpāvil, paṇṭait tamiḻk kaḻakam mūṉṟeṉ pataik kuṟippāka vuṇarttutal kāṇka.
nalvilaṅku pūṭṭum eṉṟatu tirumaṇattaṉṟu ūtappaṭṭatai. akkālattil maṅkala viṉaikkum caṅkūtappaṭṭatu. āmpōtu eṉṟatu iṟantapiṉ ūtappaṭum vēḷaiyai. mūviṭattum caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭivē amaiyumāṟu, mutaṟcaṅkam pālūṭṭum eṉṟu pāṭiyirukkalām. āyiṉ, pālūṭṭuvatu ciṟu caṅkātalāl, ataic caṅku eṉṟu kuṟittār.

kāṇ eṉṉuñ col āṅkilattilum ataṉ iṉa moḻi kaḷilum kaṉ (cun), kāṉ (con), keṉ (ken) ke aṉ (can) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. kīṉ (keen) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatākavum kīṟṟu (Skeat) kūṟṟiṉiṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
kāṇutal eṉṉuñ col, tamiḻil, puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇutalai yum akak kaṇṇāṟa kāṇutalaiyum, kuṟikkum. muṉṉatu pārttal; piṉṉatu aṟital, tiyūttāṉiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇ eṉpataṉ tiricoṟkaḷellām piṉṉataiyē uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
Cun: AS. cunnan, to know, ME. cunnen, to know, ON. cunna, to know, Ice. kunna, to know, Sw. kunna, to know, Dan. kunde, to know, M Goth. kunnan, to know.
ceyvāṉ (ceyya) eṉṉum etirkāla viṉaiyeccattiṉ (muṟṟeccattiṉ) vāṉ īṟu malaiyāḷattil āṉ īṟākat tiriyum. atuvē aṉ eṉṟu kuṟukiyum piṉṉar eṉ eṉṟu tirintum paḻaiya āṅkilattil nikaḻkāla viṉaiyecca (Infinite) īṟāka vaḻaṅkiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. āṅkila iṉa moḻikaḷil avviraṇṭum a, e eṉak kaṭaikkuṟaintuḷḷaṉa.
E cunning, 1. Knowing, skilful, ME. cunning, conning, Northern form cunnand, Ice. kunnandi.
2. Knowledge. knowledge, skill. ME. conninge, Ice. cunnandi, AS cunnung, temptation, trial.
E. con, A secondary verb, formed from AS cunnan, to know; it signifies accordingly ‘to try to know’ and may be regarded as the desiderative of ‘to know’ - Skeal’s. Etymological Dictionary of the English Language, p. 126. con, 1. to learn, learn to know, study. 2. to commit to memory. - The Random House Dictionary, p. 303. To con (the passage) (Shakespeare) = to learn by heart.

kāṇ eṉpataṉ tiri coṟkaḷ, ilattīṉa kirēkkattil eṭup poliyoṭum tiyūttāṉiyattil eṭuppilā voliyoṭum iruttal, muṉṉavaṟṟiṉum piṉṉavai tamiḻukku nerukka māyiruttalai yuṇarttutal kāṇka.
kālṭuvelār, tolkāppiyamum pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭut tokaikaḷum tamiḻarkkum teriyātu maṟaintu kiṭanta kālattil, vaḻi kāṭṭiyumiṉṟit taṉṉan taṉimaiyāy ārāynta pōtum, tamiḻiṉ toṉmai muṉmaikaḷaiyum mūlat taṉmai yaiyum uṇarntu, tam tiraviṭa oppiyalilakkaṇa nūliṉ iṟuti yil, colliyal uṟavukaḷ (Glossarial Affinities) eṉṉum pakuti yil, kīḻ varumāṟu varaintuḷḷār.
“kan, the eye; kan (in the preterite kandu), to see; also secondarily, to mark, to consider, to think. In the latter sense it comes kannu in Tamil, but the base remains unchanged. In (kanu, kannu) Telugu, the ordinary n, he nasal of the dental row, is used instead of n, the cerebral nasal. Comp. the Welsh ceniaw, to see; English ken, view, power of reach of vision, to ken, to know by sight. In Webster’s “English - Dictionary” kanna was said to be `an eye’ in Sanskrit; whereas it is exclusively a Dravidian word. This error  may be compared with Klaproth’s representing kuruta, blind, as a Sanskrit word, instead of referring it to the Dravidian languages, to which alone it belongs. There is a curious word in Sanskrit kana, one-eyed, which seems to have some Dravidian relationship. It becomes in Bengali kana, blind which, in form at least, is identical with the Dravidian negative kana, that sees not. Possibly the Dravidian kan, to see, kannu, to consider, may have some ulterior connection with the Gothic kunn-an, to know: Greek gno-nai; Sans. na; Latin gna (gnarus); Old High German chann. The different shades of meaning which are attributed to Greek to gno-nai and eide-nai; seem to corroborate this supposition; for the latter is represented as meaning to know by reflection, to know absolutely, whereas the former means to perceive, to mark, and may therefore have an ulterior connection with the Dravidian root.”  (Caldwell’s Dravidian Comparative Grammar, University Edition, p. 591.)
tamiḻ vaḷarcci taṭukkappaṭṭirunta kaṭanta mūvāyiramāṇṭuk kālattil, āriyap pūcārikaḷāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. jñā eṉṉun tiri colliṉiṉṟu piṉvarumāṟu ērāḷamāṉa coṟ kaḷai ākkik koṇṭaṉar.

ivai oru parukkai allatu ōraricip patam. ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟu ēṉaic coṟperukkattaiyum uyttuṇarntu koḷka. iṅṅaṉam vaṭamoḻiyai vaḷarttuk koḷḷutaṟku ūṇuṭai mutaliya vāḻkkaip poruḷkaḷellām varaiyātu vaḻaṅkiya vaḷḷalkaḷ, mēlaiyāriyat tiripākiya vētamoḻiyaiyum ataṉoṭu tamiḻaic cērttu vaḷartta camaṟkirutattaiyum tēva moḻiyeṉṟum, ataṉaip pōṟṟuvatāl maṇṇulakamē vīṭṭulakamāy māṟi viṭumeṉṟum, kuruṭṭuttaṉamāy nampiya pētai mūvēntarē.
tamiḻakattiṟku vētamoḻiyō camaṟkirutamō eḷḷaḷavun tēvaiyillai. āyiṉum camaṟkirutattai vaḷarttatu. tamiḻai vīḻttaṟkum nāḷaṭaivil ataṉai oḻittaṟkumē eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum. tamiḻakam civa matamum tirumāl matamum tōṉṟiya nāṭāyiruntum, iṉṉum tirukkōyil vaḻipāṭu vaṭamoḻiyilēyē naṭaipeṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟiruppatu, tamiḻariṉ maṭamaiyaiyum aṭimait taṉattaiyumē valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭum.
karaṇam allatu kāṭci yeṉṉum mūlaccol ciṟavātu, ñāṉam eṉṉum ataṉ tiricollē ciṟantu tōṉṟuvatu, uṇmaiyāṉa oḷikkalliṉum pōlikkallē polintu tōṉṟuvatu pōlum! eṉiṉum, camaṟkirutattiṟku ñāṉattait tantatu tamiḻē eṉṉum pukaḻ nilaittu niṟkum.  (ce.ce.)

kaṇṇāmpotti viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar: oruvar oru piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottik koṇṭirukkumpōtu, piṟa piḷḷaikaḷ ōṭi oḷiyum viḷaiyāṭṭu kaṇṇāmpotti eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟai: mutiyār oruvar, āṭa virumpum piḷḷaikaḷai yellām oruṅkē iruttikkoṇṭu, ovvoruvaraiyum cuṭṭi oru maraput toṭaraic colli, at toṭariṉ iṟutic collāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottuvar.  iṉi, mutiyār oruvar mītu ellāp piḷḷaikaḷum paṭapaṭaveṉṟu kaiyālaṭikkumpōtu, ammutiyavariṉ kaiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottuvatu muṇṭu. pottum pōtu, maṟṟap piḷḷaikaḷellām ōṭi oḷintu koḷvar.
mutiyār akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottik koṇṭirukkumpōtu, avviruvarukkum piṉ varumāṟu viḷaiyāṭṭu nikaḻum.

kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar iyalpu
kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum ōruyirum īruṭalumāka oruvarkkoru var urimai pūṇṭiruppataṉāl, iruvarkkum muṟaiyē kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉṟu peyar. kiḻamai pūṇṭavaṉ kiḻavaṉ; kiḻamai - urimai. kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar kātal urimaiyeṉap paṭṭamaik kum ituvē kāraṇam. iruvarum oruvarkkoruvar kaṇpōṟ ciṟantavarātaliṉ kaṇṇāḷaṉ kaṇṇāṭṭi eṉavuṅ kūṟappaṭuvar.
maṉaivikku vāḻkkait tuṇai eṉṟum, il illāḷ maṉai maṉaivi kuṭi eṉṟum peyariruppatāl, maṉaivi kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ ottuḻaittu avaṉukku aṭaṅki naṭattal vēṇṭumeṉpatum, āṭavaṉ peṇ tuṇaiyiṉṟi vāḻkkai naṭattutal iyalā teṉpatum, āṭavaṉ veḷiyēṟip poruḷīṭṭik koṇarntu koṭukka maṉaivi illattiliruntu koṇṭē taṉṉaiyum taṉ kaṇavaṉaiyum taṉ makkaḷaiyum pēṇa vēṇṭumeṉpatum peṟappaṭum.  (ta.ti.)

kaṇi - kaṇitam = kaṇippu, palvakaik kaṇakku.

kuḻampiṟkuk kaṇicamāy uppuppōṭu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
kaṇitam - kaṇitaṉ = kaṇiyaṉ, kaṇakkaṟiñaṉ. (va.va: 106 - 107)
kattari
kattari - karttari
kattutal = veṭṭutal. ivviṉai piṟkālattu vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
kattu - katti = aṟukkum allatu veṭṭuṅkaruvi.
o. nō: kottu - kotti, veṭṭu - veṭṭi.
kattu + ari = kattari. kattarittal = veṭṭi naṟukkutal.
arital = ciṟitāy naṟukkutal. arittal = arāvittēyttal.
katakkuk katakkeṉṟu veṭṭutal eṉpatu ulakavaḻakku.

Fr. saveur, L. sapor. - sapio, to taste. tū - tuppu, uḷaṟu, kuḻaṟu, (ōtai) - ōtu, paṭi, ūm - ūṅ koṭṭu, mecca(k koṭṭu), kutappu - utappu.
oliyaṭip peyarkaḷ: ēppam, kulavai, cīḻkkai, cīttai, lā - nā - nāvu - nākku. lālāṭṭu - rārāṭṭu - rōrāṭṭu. lālam - tālam  tālāṭṭu. E. Lull, Scand - Sw. lulla, Ger. lallen, Gr. laleo. E. Lullaby, a song to lull children.

kampa viḷaiyāṭṭu
nālvar, oru maṇṭapattiṉ allatu catura iṭattiṉ nāṉku mūlaik kampattilum, kampattiṟkoruvarāka niṉṟukoṇṭu, etirum valamum allatu etirum iṭamumāka, māṟi māṟi allatu cuṟṟiccuṟṟi vēṟuvēṟu kampattiṟku iyaṅkik koṇṭirukka, iṉṉoruvar avarait toṭalvēṇṭum. muṉṉarē ētēṉu moruvakaiyil tōṟṟavar allatu tavaṟiyavar toṭa vēṇṭiyavarāvar.
oruvar kampattaiviṭṭu vilakiyirukkumpōtum, ēṟkaṉavē āḷuḷḷa iṉṉoru kampattaic cērntirukkumpōtum, avarait toṭalām. toṭappaṭṭavar toṭṭavar viṉaiyai yum, toṭṭavar toṭappaṭṭavar viṉaiyaiyum, mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
toṭukiṟavar, pāl mōr tayir viṟpavarpōl, pālō pāl! allatu mōrō mōr! allatu tayirō tayir! eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu tirivatu, cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakkam, uccantalaiyaik kaiyāl taṭṭikkoṇṭu toṇṭāṉ toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟu collit tirivatu, pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakkam ākum. pāṇṭināṭṭil iv viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkut toṇṭāṉ toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟē peyar.
tīṭṭuḷḷa allatu tīṇṭappaṭāta oruvar tammiṉiṉṟu vilaki yōṭiya piṟarai, viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkō kuṟumpiṟkō toṭṭa ceyalai, naṭittuk kāṭṭuvatākavuḷḷatu iv viḷaiyāṭṭu.
karaṇam
tirumaṇattiṟkuriya oppanta allatu tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅku karaṇam eṉappaṭum. karaṇam ceykai. atu āṭci paṟṟi caṭaṅkai uṇarttiṟṟu. karaṇattōṭu kūṭiya tirumaṇattai vatuvai maṇam eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakku. (ta.ti. muṉ V)
maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷait taṉ nilaiyāṉa vāḻkkait tuṇai yākak koḷvatāka, palaraṟiyak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ cūḷ (āṇai) iṭuvatē karaṇamām. (ta.ti. muṉ V)

karuppum kaṟuppum
karumai kuṟitta colliṉ karuppu, kaṟuppu eṉṉum iruvaṭivu kaḷuḷ. muṉṉatē muṉṉatām. im muṭiviṟku ētukkaḷ mūṉṟu, avaiyāvaṉa:
 1.	kaḷ eṉum vērccolliṉiṉṟu karu eṉṉum vaṭivē muntit tōṉṟal.
 kaḷ - kar - karu - kaṟu.
 o.nō: kuḷ - kur - kuru - kuṟu:
 muḷ - mur - muru - muṟu (vaḷai)
teḷ - teṟu. veḷ - veṟu, eṉpavaṟṟil ṟakaravaṭivu nēraṭiyākat tōṉṟiyiruppiṉum, avai rakara vaṭivākiya iṭainilai yillātaṉa. kaṟu eṉpatō av iṭainilaiyai uṭaiyatu. rakarattiṉ vaṉmaiyē ṟakaramātalāl, rakaramē muntiyatām. neṭuṅkaṇakkilum rakaram muṉṉum ṟakaram piṉṉum vaikkap paṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
oḷir - oḷiṟu, muri - muṟi (vaḷai) eṉpaṉavum ṟakarattiṉ piṉmaiyaik kāṭṭum.
2.	karumai kuṟitta coṟkaḷuḷ, māperumpālaṉavum iru vakaip paṇṭai vaḻakkum rakarattaiyē koṇṭiruttal.

 3.	ṟakaraṅ koṇṭa vaṭivu vaḻipporuṭkē ēṟṟamai. iṟṟait tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ mutalatāṉa tolkāppiyattil, kaṟuppum civappum vekuḷip poruḷa (855) eṉṟu, kaṟuppu eṉṉum col ciṉapporuḷaik kuṟippatākavē kūṟuppaṭṭuḷḷatu. kaṟuttavarum civantavarumāka iruvakai niṟattār tamiḻakattut toṉṟutoṭṭuḷar. civappu eṉṟatu poṉṉiṟattaiyum cempoṉṉiṟattaiyum, ciṉattiṉāl, kariyar mukam mikak karukkum: civappar mukam mikaccivakkum: kaṇ iruniṟattārk kum civakkum.
 ceṟuvar nōkkiya kaṇtaṉ
 ciṟuvaṉai nōkkiyuñ civappā ṉāvē  (puṟam: 100)
 eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭiyaik kāṇka.
poruḷ māṟumpōtu colvaṭivam māṟavēṇṭumeṉpatu collākka neṟimuṟaiyātalāl. karuppu eṉṉum col ciṉattaik kuṟikkum pōtu kaṟuppu eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
vacaiyunark kaṟutta pakaivar  (patiṟṟup. 32: 15)
kaṟuttōr = pakaivar.
kaṟuttō ruṟumuraṇ tāṅkiya.  (patiṟṟup. 66: 9)
kaṟu - kaṟuvu = nīḷciṉam, maṉavayiram.
kaṟuvi vekuṇṭuraippāṉ (tirikaṭu. 46)
kaṟuttal = maṅkic ciṟitu karuttal. e.ṭu: kaṟukkaṇ veḷḷi.
kaṟuppu = kaṟai, kuṟṟam, taḻumpu, karumēkanōy.
kaṟu - kaṟai = karutta kaḷaṅkam. kaṟaimiṭaṟu (puṟam. 1:5)
kaṟai = kaḷaṅkam, mācu, kuṟṟam, uṟaintu karutta arattam.
kaṟuppuk kaṭṭutal = payirmutirntu iruṇṭa paccainiṟaṅ koḷḷutal.
kaṟuttal = muṟṟutal. keḷavai kaṟuppa (maturaik. 371)
kaṟuppuveṟṟilai = iruṇṭa paccai veṟṟilai.
 paccai, nīlam, karuppu mūṉṟum ōriṉa niṟaṅkaḷātalāl, payir muṟṟi iruṇṭapacumai yaṭaital kaṟuttal eṉappaṭṭatu.
 iṅṅaṉam vaḻipporuḷkaṭkē kaṟuppu eṉṉum vaṭivam uriyatām. āyiṉum, 10ām allatu 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa piṅkalantaiyil kaṟuppu eṉṉuñ coṟkuk karumaip poruḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyattil atu am mutaṟporuḷilum vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
kamparāmāyaṇam, nakar nīṅku paṭalam 58ām ceyyuḷil, kaṟuttāy eṉṉum vaṭivam, poṟuttāy, iṟuttāy, veṟut tāy eṉṉum ēṉaiyaṭi mutaṟcīrkaṭku etukaiyāka vantatākak karutalām. atē vaṉappil, ilaṅkai kāṇpaṭalam. 47ām ceyyuḷil, nāḷ eṉṉum col, yāḻ, vāḻ, pāḻ eṉpaṉavaṟ ṟiṟku etukaiyāka nāḻ eṉṟu vantiruttal kāṇka. ōr kaṟuppu millāta eṉṉum paṭikkācup pulavar pāṭṭilum. ār kaṟuppaṉ pērkaṟuppaṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiluḷḷa ṟakaram etukai paṟṟiyatē.
potuvāka, piṟkālattilakkiyattil coṟkaḷ ciṟappup poruḷ karutātu mōṉaiyetukait toṭai yoṉṟēpaṟṟi āḷap paṭṭuḷa, ūṭal, pulavi, tuṉi eṉṉum mūṉṟum mūvēṟu nilaiyaik kuṟippaṉavāyiṉum, orē poruḷil āḷappaṭṭirut talaik kāṇka.
ātalāl, kaṟukaṟuttal (mikunta karuniṟamaṭaital, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam, 325. urai), kaṟutta kākkaṭṭāṉ (karuṅkākkaṇam, yāḻppāṇa akarāti), kaṟuttakār (kuṟuvai nelvakai), kaṟutta vaṉ (karuniṟamuṭaiyavaṉ) kaṟuttavuppu, kaṟuppaṉ (kariyavaṉ), kaṟuppuk kaṭṭutal, kaṟuppuk kaṉṉimār, kaṟuppuk kāñcoṟi, kaṟuppuk kuṅkiliyam, kaṟuppuk koḷ, kaṟupput tāmar, (oru vakai maram). kaṟupputtēyilai, kaṟuppuppayaṟu, kaṟuppuppuḷḷi, kaṟuppuppūlā, kaṟuppu maṭṭivāy, kaṟuppu maṇittakkāḷi, kaṟuppuvarāl, kaṟuppu vavvāl, kaṟuppu vīram (viḷakkukkari) mutaliya piṟkāla ilakkiya vaḻakkum akara mutali vaḻakkum koḷḷattakkaṉavalla.
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyil, varaccūlai, varaṭci, varaṭcuṇṭi, varaṭcūlai, varaṭcoṟi, varaṭṭaṭaip pāṉ, varaṭṭi, varaṭṭutal, varaṭṭu, varaṭṭuc cōkai, varaṭṭup pacu, varaḷ, varaḷvāyu eṉa ṟakaram varavēṇṭiya coṟkaḷaiyel lām oḻuṅkāy rakaramiṭṭuk kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ittakaiya akaramutalikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟātu. tūya karuniṟattaik kuṟikkum collellām rakarattaik koṇṭavaiyeṉṟum, ciṉam, kaṟai, mutircci mutaliya vaḻipporuḷaik kuṟippaṉavellām ṟakarattaik ko ṇṭavaiyeṉṟum, terintukoḷka. mara vayiram karuttum civantum irukkumātalāl, karuttataik karuppu eṉṟum civantataic cēku eṉṟum collal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, karuppu eṉṉum col pañcam eṉṉum poruṭkēyuriya tākac cilar karutuvar. karuppu iruḷaiyum, iruḷ tuṉpattaiyum nāṭṭut tuṉpaṅkaḷuṭ koṭiya pañcattaiyum kuṟikkum. ataṉālēyē. attamikkum pōtil eṉṉum kāḷamēkam pāṭṭuc coṟṟoṭarkku. pañcakālattil eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam aṇivakaip poruḷkaḷ ellā niṟap peyarkaṭkumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu:
karuppu = pēy, vayiram, cārāyam.
veḷḷai = veḷutta āṭai, cuṇṇāmpu, veḷḷāṭu, veṇpā, kaḷḷamiṉmai, teḷivu.
paccai = iḻavu vīṭṭiṟ koṭukkum payaṟu. iṭakkar, paccai maiyiṟ kuttiya toyyil, moḻiyiṉ iyalpu nilai.
civappu = māṇikkam (civappukkal), ciṉam.
mañcaḷ = kāmālai.
iṉi, potumakkaḷ eṉṉum col putiyateṉṟum, poruntāta teṉṟum, cila pulavar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

carukilai yiṇaitta kallai  (periyapu. kaṇṇappa. 118) (va.va. 110)
 kalvi (Culture)
makkaḷ nākarikamillāta māṇmutu paḻaimaiyiṟ kuṟuntokai yarāyk kuṟiñci nilattu vāḻntapōtu kāykaṉi kiḻaṅku mutaliya iyaṟkai viḷaiporuḷkaḷaiyē uṇṭu vantaṉar. uṇpatum uṟaṅkuvatumē avark kiruperuntoḻil. makkaṭṭokai mika mika, iyaṟkai viḷaivu pōtātāyiṟṟu. vitaikaḷtāvara varkkat tiṉiṉṟum kīḻē viḻuntu nilattil muḷaippatai muṉṉamē uṟṟu nōkki ūkittiruntaṉar. aḵtaṉṟi vaḷḷik kiḻaṅkaip paṉṟikaḷ uḻutaviṭattu viḻunta vitaikaḷ viraivil muḷaittu, aṭarntōṅki, viḻumiya palaṉṟantamaiyuṅ kaṇṭiruntaṉar. ātalāl avarē attakai yiṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceyaṟkaiyiṟ payir paccaikaḷai viḷaikkat toṭaṅkiṉar.
 paṉṟiyuḻutaviṭattup payirviḷaippatai.
aruvi yārkkuṅ kaḻaipayi ṉaṉantalaik
kaṟivaḷa raṭukkattu malarnta kāntaṭ
koḻuṅkiḻaṅku miḷirak kiṇṭik kiḷaiyoṭu
kaṭuṅkaṭ kēḻa luḻuta pūḻi
naṉṉāḷ varupata nōkkik kuṟava
ruḻā atu vittiya parūukkuraṟ ciṟutiṉai  (puṟam. 168)
eṉpatāṟ kāṇka.
ākavē, mutaṉmutal makkaḷ kaṟṟa kalvi uḻavuttoḻil eṉpatē pulaṉākiṉṟatu. uyir vāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṇavu ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭutaliṉ, kalvi eṉṉum collum uḻavut toḻilaiyē mutaṉ mutaṟ kuṟittatu. kal, pakuti; vi, vikuti. kallal tōṇṭal. kalleṉṉuñ collē illeṉṟu tiriyum. payirttoḻil viṉaikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷum uḻavē mutalatum mutaṉmaiyatu mātaliṉ, av vuḻaveṉ peyarē oruvakai ākupeyarāyp payirttoḻil muḻumaikkumāyiṟṟu. aṉṟiyum vāṉāvārip payirkaṭku uḻavoṉṟē yamaiyum. uḻavāvatu nilattai akaḻtalum nilaipeyarttalum pūḻiyākka lum, ērumerutuṅkoṇṭuḻavaṟiyāta paḻaṅkālattuk kalluṅ kaḻiyuṅ karuviyāka nilattai akaḻtalē uḻavāyiṟṟu. akkālat tum kuṟiñci nilattum maḻaikkuk kuṟaiyiṉmaiyiṉ nīrpāycca vum vēṇṭātāyiṟṟu.
kallal, tōṇṭal, uḻutal eṉpaṉa oruviṉai kuṟittaliṉ oru poruṭ kiḷavi. ākavē, kalvi eṉpatu mutaṉmutal uḻavu kuṟitta kiḷaviyē yeṉpatu peṟṟām. uḻaviṟkup piṉ kaṟkappaṭṭatu kaittoḻil. kaittoḻiṟkup piṉṉatu kalaiyaṟivu allatu nūṟkalvi. ākavē, toḻiṟkalvi, nūṟkalvi yeṉak kalvi iru vakaittāyiṟṟu. uḻavum kaittoḻilum toḻiṟkalvi, kalai yaṟivu nūṟkalvi. ivviraṇṭaṉuṭ ṭalaimaiyum iṉṟiyamai yāmaiyum paṟṟi nūlkalviyē kalviyeṉa vitantōtappaṭṭatu.
vaṭanūṉ muṟaiyil vaḻaṅkum aṟupāṉāṟ ṟoḻilkaḷum toḻiṟ kalviyākum; ēṉai nāṉmaṟai yaṟukalaikaḷ nūṟkalvi yākum.
iruvakaik kalviyilum ennūlaiyum maṟai yeṉpatu paṇṭai vaḻakku. icainūlai narampiṉ maṟai eṉṟār tolkāppiyar. maruttu nūlai āyurvētameṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. kaṇkaruvi yākak kaikaṟkum toḻiṟkalviyum ciṟupāṉmai nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattai nūlvāyilāyuṇarttu meṉka.
ovvoru nūlum oru cār maṟaiporuḷai yuṭaimaiyiṉ maṟai yeṉappaṭṭatu. iṉi, ellā nūlkaṭkum muṭivu vīṭupēṟu kūṟum kaṭavuṇmaṟaiyātaliṉ, am maṟaiyuṇarccikku orucār karuviyākum piṟa nūlkaḷum av viyaipāṉē maṟaiyeṉap paṭṭaṉa veṉṉalu moṉṟu.
nūṟkalviyum ulakanūl, aṟivunūl eṉa iruvakaittām. aṟivu nūl vīṭṭunūleṉavum paṭum. kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭum vīṭupēṟum kūṟum maṟaiyākamak kataikaḷum, ilakkaṇa tarukka tattuva nūl kaḷum vīṭṭu nūlkaḷākum. ulaka vāḻkkaikkuriya vellām ulaka nūlkaḷākum. āṉmavuṇarcci ciṟantakālattu vīṭṭunūlē nūleṉappaṭṭatu. tamiḻilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅ kaḷellām vīṭupēṟṟiṟkē vaḻikāṭṭuvaṉa.
aṟamporu ḷiṉpam vīṭaṭaita ṉūṟpayaṉē
........ tolaivillāc
cattamuñ cōtiṭamu meṉṟāṅ kivaipitaṟṟum
pittariṟ pētaiyā ril
alakucāl kaṟpiṉ aṟivunūl kallā
tulakanū lōtuva tellāṅ - kalakala
kūun tuṇaiyallāṟ koṇṭu taṭumāṟṟam
pōon tuṇaiyaṟivā ril
 eṉṟār periyōr.
ikkālattuk kaṟkappaṭum āṅkilak kalvi tamiḻmuṟaippaṭi ulaka nūliṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē.
eḻuttaṟiyumpōtē ari ōm nama: eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatum, eḻuttukaḷē mantiravaṭivāyiruppatum, eḻuttukkūṭṭi vācikkap puku māttiraiyē aṟañceya virumpu, ālayan toḻuvatu cālavu naṉṟu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa aṟivuraikaḷai aṟivippatum, ilakkaṇa muḻumaiyum meyyuṇarcci yilakuvatum, ilakkiyamellām vīṭupēṟṟai yilakkāk koḷvatum tamiḻukkē ciṟanta takaimai kaḷām.
ākavē, kalviyeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭu tamiḻil mutaṉmutal uḻaviṟ kiyaṟpeyarā yiruntu, piṉṉark kaittoḻiṟkum nūṟkalvikkum iṉa vilakkaṇamāy iṟutiyil.... nūlukkē ciṟappāka varaiyaṟuk kappaṭṭateṉka.
nilattaik kalluvatiṉāl, āḻattiṉ maṟaintirukkum kaṉi (Mine)íª tāvaramumākiya iruvakai viḷaiporuḷkaḷu mārāyappaṭum: vitaikaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ kiḷarnteḻum payirpaccai kaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭaiyum: kāykatirkaḷummuṟṟi makkaṭku nukarcciyuṇṭākum. atupēlak kalviyiṉālum aimpūtap pakuti yāṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷu mārāyappaṭum ; maṉattiṉ āṟṟal kaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭaiyum. (cf. educate - to draw out the powers of mind); āṉma nukarcciyākiya vīṭupēṟum cittikkum.
kalviyeṉṉun tamiḻccoṟkuc camamāṉa āṅkilaccol Culture eṉpatākum. atu kal eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyāyp piṟanta viṉaip peyarē. kalvi eṉpatil vi vikuti: Culture eṉpatil taram (ture) vikuti. kalviyeṉṉuñ coṟkuṟikkum poruḷkaḷaiyē Culture eṉpatuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟatu. avaiyāvaṉa:
1. Culture - n. Cultivation
L. colo - to till. kal = tōṇṭu, uḻu. payiriṭu.
iḵtaṉṟi āṅkilattil uḻavu kuṟikkum ēṉaic coṟkaḷil iṉṟiyamaiyāta cilavum tamiḻēyāta laṟiyappaṭum.
toḷ - till; toḷ - toṭu - tōṇṭu. toḷḷal = tōṇṭal allatu uḻutal.
ēr - n. E. ear, L. aro, GK. aroo. adj. E. arable.
akaram - n. E. acre, Gk. agros, L. ager, a field.
akaram marutanilattūr - cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu. marutam = vayal. kāṟu (koḻu) - share.
Culture eṉṉuñ col kalviyālāṉa oḻukkattai allatu tiruttattai uṇarttiya piṉṉar, uḻavu āṅkilattil Agriculture eṉa aṭaikoṭukkappaṭṭatu.
2. Culture-n. refinement
kalviyālaṟivum aṟivā loḻukkamum payaṉātaliṉ kalvi yeṉpatu kāraṇavākupeyarāyt tiruttattaiyuṅ kuṟikku meṉka.
kalvi toṭarpāṉa coṟkaḷ
kaḻakam - college, n. (orig.) any collection or community of men with certain privileges or a common pursuit as a college of heralds or the college of cardinals: a seminary of learning; a literary, political, or religious institution. - Chambers Etymological Dictionary
N.B. orig. ‘g’ was pronounced as ‘g’ in god and the final ‘e’ was not silent.
 kal +akam = kallakam - kaḻakam.
 kallakam viṉaittokai
 Pedagogue - n. a teacher.
L. - Gk. paidos (paital), a boy; ago - (ukai) to lead.
pedantry - n. vain display of learning (same)
Cyclopeadia - n. the circle of human knowledge.
Gk. kyklos (cakkaram), a circle; paideia (from the root - go) learning.
3. Cult - n. worship
L. colo - to worship.
kaṟṟataṉā lāya payaṉeṉkol vālaṟivaṉ
naṟṟā ṭoḻāa reṉiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 21)
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl uḻavē ulaka mutaṟkalvi yeṉṟum kalvi yeṉṉuñ col mutaṉmutal uḻavu kuṟitta collē yeṉṟum, piṟkālattu nūṟkalviyai varaiyaṟuttamaiyiṉ uḻavupporuḷil vaḻakkaṟṟateṉṟum, ulakil mutaṉmutal nākarikameyti uḻavuṅ kalviyu maṟintār tamiḻmakkaḷēyeṉ ṟum avarāṉēyē av virutoḻilum mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ paraviya teṉṟum. iv virutoḻilaiyuṅ kuṟikkum mēṉāṭṭuc coṟ kaḷil iṉṟiyamaiyāta vellām centamiḻt tiripē yeṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka. (centamiḻc celvi cuṟavam 1933)
kalakam
 kalakam - kalaha
kalattal = poruntutal, poruntip pōr ceytal.
o.nō: poru - pōr. camam - camar.
kai kalattal = caṇṭaiyiṭutal. kala - kalām = pōr, kalakam.
kala - kalavu - kalaku - kalakam = kūṭṭac caṇṭai. kalaku - kalaki. kalakittal = kalakañceytal.
vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamillai. (va.va: 109)
kalampakam
kalampakam - katampam.
pariyakam nūpuram pāṭakañ cataṅkai (cilap. 6: 84, urai). (va.va.)
kalavam
kalavam - kalāpa
kala - kalavu. kalavutal = kalattal.
kalavu - kalavam = 1. kaṟṟaiyāṉa mayil tōkai.
kalavam viritta maññai (poruna. 212). 2. mayil (ciṉaiyāku peyar).
kalavañcēr kaḻikkāṉal  (tēvā. 532: 4)
kalavu - kalāvu. kalāvutal = kalattal. kalavam - kalāvam = mayil tōkai.
kalimayiṟ kalāvam  (puṟam. 146: 8)
mayil teṉṉāṭṭuk kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya paṟavai; kuṟiñcit teyvamākiya murukaṉ ūrti.
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalā (ciṟupakuti) + āp (koḷ, to obtain) eṉṟu pirittu, palapakutikaḷai oṉṟucērppatu kaṟṟai (“that which holds single parts together, abundle” eṉṟu kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatu, vaṭṭañcuṟṟi valintum nalintum poruḷ koḷḷuvatā yiruttal kāṇka. (va.va: 109 - 110.)
kalittal
kalittal eṉpatu piṟappuk karuttu vērccol.
ul - ullari = taḷir. (aka. ni.)
ullari - va. vallari.
ul - kul - kuḷ - kuḷa - kuḷavu - kuḷaku = iḷantaḻai. maṟikuḷa kuṇṭaṉṉa maṉṉā makiḻcci (nālaṭi. 16).
kuḷavu - kaḷavu - kaḷavam - kaḷapam = yāṉaik kaṉṟu. matakarik kaḷapamum (cilap. 25: 49).
o.nō: OS calf, AS cealf, OE coelf, ME kalf, calf, E. calf, OHG chalb, ON kalfr, Du. kalf, Icel kalfr, Swed, kalf, Dan, kabr, Goth kalbo, G. kalb Skt. kalabha.
kuḷa - kuḻa - kuḻam = iḷamai.
kuḻa = iḷamaiyāṉa. maḻavuṅ kuḻavum iḷamaip poruḷa (tol. uri. 14). kuḻamakaṉ = iḷantalaivaṉ. kuḻamakaṉaik kali veṇpāk koṇṭu...... viḷakkavuraittāṅku (ilak. vi. 858).
kuḻa - va. kuṭa.
kuḻa - kuḻavu = iḷamai. kuḻavu - kuḻaku = 1. kuḻantai. kuḻakeṉa veṭuttukanta vumai (tiruppu. 106). 2. iḷamaic cevvi. kommaik kuḻakāṭuṅ kōlavurai mārpar (cīvaka. 2790). 3. aḻaku - koṉṟai cūṭik kuḻakāka .... viḷaiyāṭum (tēvā. 468:7).
kuḻaku - kuḻakaṉ = 1. iḷaiyōṉ. niṉmaṇak kuḻakaṉ (tiruviḷai. tirumaṇa. 44). 2. murukaṉ. (piṅ.). 3. aḻakaṉ. koṭkap peyarkkuṅ kuḻakaṉ (tiruvāca. 3: 12).
kuḻakaṉ - ka. huṭuka.
kuḻam - kuḻantu - kuḻantai = kaippiḷḷai. kuḻantaiyaiyuyirtta malaṭikku (kamparā. urukkā. 65). 2. iḷamaip paruvam. kuḻantai veṇmati (kamparā. ūrtēṭu. 209).
 ō.nō: ME, E. child, OE cild, Gothr kilthei, womb (karuppai).
kuḻantu - koḻuntu = 1. iḷantaḷir. 2. iḷamaiyāṉatu.                                  3. meṉmaiyāṉatu. 4. marukkoḻuntu. 5. paṭaiyiṉ muṉṉaṇi. cēṉaiyiṉ koḻuntu pōyk koṭimatiṉ mitilaiyi ṉellai kūṭiṟṟē (kamparā. eḻucci. 24). 6. eri neruppiṉ nāvu. 7. kavarinuṉi. 8. karumpunuṉi, koḻuntāṭai. ma. koḻuntu.
kuḻavu - o.nō: L. dim. suf. culus, culum, cula, cule - cle - cel, cillus - cil.
Ex. fasciculus, vasculum, Auricula, arimalcule, particle, parcel, L. codicillus - E. codicil.
kuṭṭiyappaṉ, kuṭṭikkiḻaṅku, kuṭṭit tolkāppiyam, kuṭṭit takkāḷi, kuṭṭippal, kuṭṭiviral mutaliya coṟkaḷil, kuṭṭi eṉpatu ciṟumaip poruḷ muṉṉoṭṭāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. kuḻavit tiṅkaḷ eṉpataiyum nōkkuka.
kuḻavu - kuḻavi = 1. kaikkuḻantai. īṉṟa kuḻavi mukaṅkaṇṭiraṅki ( maṇi. 11: 114). 2. cila vilaṅkukaḷiṉ kuṭṭip peyar. (tol. mara. 19-23). 3. ōraṟivuyiriṉ iḷamaip peyar. (tol. mara. 24). vīḻil tāḻaik kuḻavi (tol. mā. 24, urai).
kuḻam - kuḻaṉ - kuḻal - kuḻalai - kutalai.
o.nō: maḻalai - matalai.
kutalai = 1. kuḻantai. 2. kuḻantaiyiṉ maḻalaic col. itaḻ kuvittup paṇitta kutalaiyun teriyātu (kallā. 5). 3. iṉiya col. kutalaic cevvāyk kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir (cilap. 30:114).
kuḻal - kutal - katal - katali = ciṟuvāḻai. montaṉ eṉpatu peruvāḻai.
katali - kacali - kacaḷi = ciṟu keṇṭaimīṉ. keṇṭaik kacaḷi eṉpatu vaḻakku.
katal - kacal - kaccal = 1. vāḻaip piñcu, iḷampiñcu, ka, kacca. 2. olli. āḷ kaccalāyiruppāṉ (u.va.)
katali - va. katalī = vāḻaip potu.
ciṟu vāḻaiyaik kuṟikkum katali yeṉṉum teṉcol, vaṭamoḻiyil taṉ ciṟappup poruḷai yiḻantu, vāḻaip potuvaik kuṟittalai nōkkuka.
pērā. parō (Burrow) itai aṟiyātu, tam camaṟkiruta moḻi (The Sanskrit Language) eṉṉum nūliṉ iṟutiyatikārattil.
“kadale, `banana’: cf. Sakai telui, kelui, Nicobar talui, Khmer tut taloi, Palaong kloa `plantain’; Savara kin-ten `banana’ eṉṟu, katali yeṉṉum colliṟku mūlaṅkāṭṭiyuḷḷamai ciṟitēṉum poruntumā veṉṟu aṟiñar kaṇṭu koḷka.
kuḷ - kuṭṭu - kuṭṭaṉ = 1. ciṟuvaṉ, ciṟupiḷḷai. eṉ ciṟuk kuṭṭaṉ (tiv. periyāḻ. 1: 4: 2), kuyileṉap pēcumeṉ kuṭṭaṉeṅ kuṟṟatu (tirukkō. 224). 2. āṭṭuk kuṭṭi. (tivā).
ma. kuṭṭaṉ, ka. kuṭṭa (gudda).
kuṭṭu - kuṭṭi = 1. mūṅkā, veruku, eli, aṇil, nāy, paṉṟi, puli, muyal ākiyavaṟṟiṉ iḷamaip peyar. (tol. mara. 6, 8). 2. vilaṅkiṉ iḷamaip potup peyar. 3. ciṟu peṇ atu nallakuṭṭi. 4. kaṭaicip piḷḷai. ivaṉ eṉ kaṭaikkuṭṭi. 5. ciṟumaip peyar e-ṭu. kuṭṭit tolkāppiyam.
ma. kuṭṭi, ka. kuṭṭi (guddi). ta. piḷḷaikuṭṭi = piḷḷaikaḷ.
kuṭṭi - E. kid, a young goat, kid, kiddy (sl), child; ME kid, kidde, Dan. kid, Swed, kid, Norw. kid, Icel, kid, ON kith, OHG kizzi, chizzi, MHG., G. kitze.
kid - fox, a young fox.
kuḷ - kuy - kuyñcu - kuñcu = 1. paṟavaik kuñcu 2. aṇil eli mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kuṭṭi.
ma. kuññu. ka. kūcu = kuḻavi.
kuñcu - kuñci = 1. paṟavaikkuñcu. 2. ciṟiyatu. e-ṭu. kuñciyappaṉ, kuñcip peṭṭi.
kul - kur - kuru. kuruttal = tōṉṟutal. atiṉiṉṟu moru puruṭaṉ kuruttāṉ (vināyakapu. 72: 4).
kuru = 1. kuḻantai. 2. tōṉṟuṅ koppuḷam, vērk kuru veppu nōyuṅ kuruvun toṭara (cilap. uraipeṟu kaṭṭurai). 3. mayir cilirppu. 4. koṭṭai, palākkuru. 5. pulliyatu. kurunakai = puṉcirippu. LG. gor, child.
kuru - kuruttu = 1. ōlaik koḻuntu, paṉaṅkuruttu. 2. koḻun tāṭai, karumpunuṉi. kuruttiṟ karumputiṉṟaṟṟē (nālaṭi. 211). 3. iḷamai. 4. melliya uṭpuṟam, kātuk kuruttu. 5. melliya uṭporuḷ. maruppu (tantam) mūḷaiyivaṟṟiṉ kuruttu. āḷi naṉmāṉ..... vēḻattu veṇkōṭu vāṅkik kuruttaruntum (akanā. 381).
ma. karuttu.
kuru - kuruku = 1. iḷamai kurukuk kiḻaṅku. 2. vilaṅkiṉ kuṭṭi. ciṅkak kuruku (tiv. tiruppā. 1, vyā.) 3. kuruttōlai. kuruku paṟiyā nīḷirum paṉaimicai (paripā. 2: 43).
 k., bj., tu. kuru.
kuru = kurumpai = teṅku paṉaikaḷiṉ iḷaṅkāy. kuṉṟuṅ kurumpaiyum veṟuttaniṉ ṉiḷamulai (kallā. 52).
kuru - kuruḷ - kuruḷai = 1. kuḻantai. aruṭ kuruvāṅ kuruḷai (ci.ci. parapak. pāyi. 4). 2. cila vilaṅkiṉ kuṭṭi. (tol. mara. 8, 9). 3. pāmpiṉ kuṭṭi. ciṟuveḷḷaraviṉavvarik kuruḷai (kuṟun. 119).
“Girl, a female child, young woman, (E.) ME. girle, girle, gyrle, formerly used of either sex and signifying either a boy or girl..... N Fries. gor, a girl; Pomeran. goer, a child; O Low G. gor, a child.... Swiss gurre, gurrli, a depreciatory term for a girl.... Norw. gorre, a small child; Swed. dial. garra, gurre (the same). Root uncertain.” - E.D.E.L. by Skeat, p. 240.
“girl, n. ME. gurle, gurl, gerle, gerle, gerl, boy, girl, rel to LG. gore, gor a child; of uncertain origin.” - Klein’s C.E.D.E.L., p. 312.
