kalaiccollākka neṟimuṟaikaḷ
taṟkālak kalvik kalaikaḷiṟ pala. mēṉāṭṭiṉiṉṟum namakku van tamaiyāl, avaṟṟaippaṟṟiya perunūlkaḷum, kuṟiyīṭukaḷum iṉṟum perumpālum mēlai moḻikaḷilēyē uḷḷaṉa. taṟpōtu kuṭiyaracukkāṉa vaḻituṟaikaḷai vakukkuṅkāl, tāymoḻiyiṟ kalvi kaṟpikka vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ tamiḻnāṭṭuk kalvikkut tamiḻiṟ kalaiccoṟkaḷaiyum, kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum moḻi peyarttuk koḷḷutalum ākkik koḷḷutalum iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. itaṟkākap pala cārpil pala muṟaikaḷ pala kuḻukkaḷ palaviṭattiṟ kūṭiyārāyntum ōraḷavu vēlai ceytum vantirukkiṉṟaṉa; varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, kalaic coṟ kaḷai ākkikkoḷḷum neṟimuṟaikaḷai iṉṉum ak kuḻukkaḷ cariyāy uṇarātiruppatu peritum varuntattakkatu.
tamiḻiṟ kalaiccoṟkaḷai ākkuvār āṅkilam tamiḻ iraṇ ṭaiyum naṉṟāyaṟintavarāyum collārāycciyuṭaiyavarāyum tamiḻppaṟṟu nirampiyavarāyu miruttal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻaṟiyāta perumāḷar kalaiccollākkuvatu kuruṭar vaḻikāṭṭuvataiyum, collārāycciyillātār ākkum kalaiccol iḷañciṟār ōviyattaiyumē okkum. tamiḻppaṟ ṟillātavariṭam kalaiccollākkattai oppuvippatō peṟṟa tāyaip paṟṟalariṭam oppuvippatēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. mēṟkūṟiya mūviyalpukaḷ uḷḷavar kuḻumiya piṉpum āttirappaṭalākātu. palanūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāy mēlai moḻikaḷil tōṉṟiya kuṟiyīṭu kaḷukku ōriru nāḷil allatu mātattil nērcol kāṇa muṭiyātu. pataṟiya kāriyam citaṟik keṭum; pataṟāta kāriyam citaṟātu. viraintu ceyvatu maṟaintupōm. nīṭittuc ceyvatu nilaittu niṟkum.
aitarāpāttiluḷḷa ucumāṉiyāp palkalaikkaḻakattār. paṭṭat tērvukaṭkuriya kallūrik kalviyai urutumoḻiyiṟ pukuttu muṉ, nālāṇṭākap poṟumaiyuṭaṉ uḻaittuk kalaic coṟkaḷaiyellām am moḻiyil ākkik koṇṭaṉar. nāmum avarkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum.
tamiḻiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām iṉṉum tokukkappaṭa villai. nūlvaḻakkiluḷḷa coṟkaḷē palkalaikkaḻaka akarāti 
yil (University Lexicon) muṟṟum iṭampeṟavillaiyāyiṉ, ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷaip paṟṟic collavēṇṭuvatē yillai. uḻavu, kaittoḻil, ulakiyal mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya pala ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ kalaiccollākkattiṟkup peritum payaṉ paṭuvaṉa. ākaiyāl, mutalāvatu nūlvaḻakku, ulaka vaḻakku, kalveṭṭu im mūṉṟiṉiṉṟum itukāṟum akarātiyiṟ pukāta coṟkaḷai yellām tokuttuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum. itu ceyyāmaṟ kalaiccollākkuvatu karuviyillāmaṟ karumañ ceyvatē pōlum.
araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭāṭi yaṟṟē nirampiya
nūliṉṟik kōṭṭi koḷal.   (kuṟaḷ. 401)
iraṇṭāvatu, centamiḻc colliyal neṟimuṟaikaḷil, kalaic collākkattiṟku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai yellām terintukoḷ ḷal vēṇṭum. atāvatu, muṉṉoṭṭukaḷ, piṉṉoṭṭukaḷ, iṭaiyoṭṭukaḷ, īṟukaḷ eṉṟa nāṟṟiṟappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉ allatu kuṟaiccoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ payaṉkaḷellā vaṟṟaiyum terintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
mūṉṟāvatu, avvak kalaiyil tērccipeṟṟa āṅkila aṟiñaraik koṇṭu moḻipeyarkkavēṇṭum. āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷai yellām kalaivāriyāka eḻutuvittuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
nālāvatu, muṟkūṟiya mūvakait tiṟaṉum oruṅkamainta tiṟavōr, oruṅkē yamarntu tīra āyntu, kalaivāriyāka moḻi peyark kavō coṟpuṉaiyavō vēṇṭum.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ acainilai, puṇarnilai, pakucoṉṉilai, tokainilai, paltokainilai, pirinilai eṉa aṟu nilaikaḷaiyaṭaiyumēṉum, avaiyāvum taṉinilai, puṇarnilai eṉa iraṇṭā yaṭaṅkum. taṉinilaiyāvatu coṟka ḷellām taṉittaṉiyāka amaintu kiṭakkum nilai: puṇar nilaiyāvatu ac coṟkaḷ iraṇṭum palavum kūṭip pakucoṟ kaḷāyum toṭar moḻikaḷāyum puṇarntu niṟkum nilai: taṉinilaiyākkam perumpālum paṇṭaik kālattilēyē muṟṟup peṟṟatu. puṇarnilaiyākkam ekkālattiṟkum uriyatu. ātalāl, coṟkaḷaip pakuttāyntu collākka muṟaikaḷaik kaṇṭu piṭittu avaṟṟaik kaiyāḷiṉ ekkālattum iyaṟkai muṟaippaṭiyē vēṇṭumaḷavu putuccoṟ puṉaintu koḷḷa lām.
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattai aṟiyāta cilar, tamiḻiṟ pōtiya col illaiyeṉṟum, atil moḻipeyarttaṟku āṅkilam allatu vaṭamoḻi pōṉṟa ayaṉmoḻit tuṇai iṉṟi yamaiyātateṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivar, kirēkku ilattīṉ mutaliya āriya moḻikaḷil centamiḻ vērkaḷ ceṟintu kiṭappataiyum, āṅkilak kalaiccoṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷ eḷimaiyaiyum, collamaippu neṟimuṟaikaḷaiyum, tamiḻ nikaṇṭukaḷaiyum aṟintiruntāl iṅṅaṉam kūṟār.
āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ amaippeḷimaiyai viḷakkutaṟku, cēmparār (Chambers) āṅkilac colliyal akarāti yiṉiṉṟum cila col viḷakkaṅkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟēṉ.
	(1)	Alum: a mineral salt (cf. aḷam = c¥ò)
		L. alumen
 		Alumina: one of the earth. L. alumen, alum.
 		Aluminium: the metalic base of alumina.
	(2) 	Candidate: so called because at Rome, the applicant used to dress in white.
 		L. candidus, white-candeo (cf. fhªJ), to shine.
	 (3)	Chancellor: Low L. cancellarius, orig. an officer that had charge of records, and stood near the cancelli (L.) the crossbars that surrounded the judgement-seat.
	(4)	Electric: having the property of attracting and repelling light bodies when rubbed.
		L. electram-Gk. electron, amber (cf. m«g®) in which the above property was first observed.
	(5)	Protein: the common radical of the most essential articles of food.
 		Gk. protos, first, and suffix-in.
kalaiccoṟkaḷait taṉittamiḻilēyē yāttal vēṇṭum. av yāppukkūṭāta orō vaḻi maṭṭum ayaṉmoḻit tuṇai vēṇṭappaṭum. itu ellā moḻikkum potu.
moḻipeyarpput tiṟaṉil tamiḻ emmoḻikkum iḷaitta taṉṟu. taṉittamiḻil kuṟiyīṭṭukkākap puṉaitaṟku, vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷai vaḻakkiṟkuk koṇartal vēṇṭum. vaḻaṅkāmalē coṟkaḷ vaḻakkaṟukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattil vaḻaṅkāta tuppuravu (=cuttam) eṉṉum teṉcol teṉpākat tilum, teṉpākattil vaḻaṅkāta nūkku (=taḷ) eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭapākattilum, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkāta neyttōr eṉṉum teṉcol teluṅkil netturu eṉṟum vaḻaṅku vatai nōkkuka. aṉṟiyum tamiḻccollirukka ayaṟcol nāṭal. kaiyil veṇṇeyirukka neyk kalai vatum, ētilār ārat tamar pacikka viṭutalumākum.
tamiḻiṟ kalaiccoṟkaḷākkumpōtu, ēṟkeṉavē āṅkila nēr teṉcoṟkaḷirukkumāyiṉ eḷitāy moḻipeyarttuk koḷḷalām. iṅṅaṉa millāviṭattumaṭṭum putuccoṟkaḷ puṉaiyappaṭum. appuṉaivukkāṉa neṟimuṟaikaḷāvaṉa:
 	1. 	oru poruḷukku ētēṉum oru kāraṇampaṟṟip peyariṭa lām. ak kāraṇam, oru ciṟitē poruntumēṉum amaiyum. atē kāraṇam piṟa poruḷukku ēṟkumā eṉṟu kavaṉikka vēṇṭuvatillai.
		alviyaṉmai (avviyāpti), mikuviyaṉmai (ativiyāpti) eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷ varaiyaṟaikkē (definition) yaṉṟic coṟkillai.
	2.	pala poruḷukku orē kāraṇam ēṟpiṉ avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ vevvēṟā yamaiyumpaṭi, oru poruṭ palacoṟkaḷ āḷavēṇṭum.
	e-ṭu: vaḷaiyal = vaḷainta aṇi.
	koṭukku = vaḷainta uṟuppu.
	puricai = vaḷainta matil.
	kuṉivu = mutuku vaḷaital.
	pariti = vaṭṭamāṉa cūriyaṉ.
	3.		orē collaip pala poruṭku vaḻaṅkiṉ, poruḷtoṟum tirittukkoḷḷavēṇṭum.
	e-ṭu: kaḷ - kar (vēr) = karuppu, maṟaivu.
kara, karampu, karaṭi, karantai (pū), kari, karical, kariccāṉ, karicu, karu, karuku, karukku, karuttai, karuppai (eli), karumpu, karuval, kār, kāri mutaliya coṟkaḷellām karumai eṉṉum orē karuttai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu vikuti vēṟupāṭṭāl vevvēṟu poruḷaik kuṟippaṉa.
4. 	orē poruḷiṉ allatu karuttiṉ nuṭpa vēṟupāṭṭaik kuṟikkavum collait tirittukkoḷḷalām.
	e-ṭu: paricu = ikali (pōṭṭiyiṭṭu)p peṟuvatu.
 	paricil = ikalātu peṟuvatu.
 	paricam = peṇṇuk kaḷippatu.
5. 	coṟṟirippup pala vakaippaṭum.
oṉpāṉ tiripu, mukkuṟai, mummikai, pōli, ilakkaṇappōli, tiripākupeyar (tattitāntam), marūu mutaliya vellām tiripiṉ vakaikaḷām. ivai taṉittum kalantum varum.
e-ṭu: 
kaḷ-(=karuppu)-kaḷi (=karumaṇ, kaḷimaṇ pōṉṟa uṇavu) -
kaḷimpu (=kaḷipōṉṟa maruntu - (īṟṟumikai.)
nantu - nattai - (valittalum īṟṟumikaiyum)
ir-irā, irum, iruḷ, irumpu
iṟaṭi, iṟuṅku palvakait tiripu
ēṉal, ēṉam.
yāṉai, (ēṉai)
6. īṟukaḷaip poruṇmarapaṟintu puṇarkkavēṇṭum.
ovvōr īṟum ovvoru allatu ciṟcila poruḷkaḷai marapākak kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu: al.
cemmal, karuval, vaḷḷal - uṭaiyōṉai uṇarttiṟṟu.
vaṟuval, noṟuval - ceyporuḷai uṇarttiṟṟu.
āṭal - toḻilai uṇarttiṟṟu.
tōṉṟal - ceyvōṉai uṇarttiṟṟu.
orē poruṭkup pala īṟum ēṟkum. īṟu peṟuñ colliṉ iṟutikkēṟpa ōr īṟṟaic cērkka vēṇṭum.
eccam, tōṉṟal, ōtuvāṉ, viṟakuveṭṭi, ventai eṉpavaṟṟi luḷḷa am, al, āṉ, i, ai eṉṉum īṟukaḷ ceyvōṉaiyē kuṟittaṉa.
kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟḷellām ceyvōṉ poruḷil ikaravīṟṟai ēṟkum.
 e-ṭu: tōṉṟi, ōti, veṭṭi, iṭukki.
7.	oru poruḷ iṉṉoṉṟiṉ perumaippāṭāyiruntāl, perumaiyaṭaikaḷaiyum īṟukaḷaiyum potumaiyāṉa poruṭpeyarōṭu cērttukkoḷḷalām.
e-ṭu: neruñcil - āṉaineruñcil 
nāval - perunāval aṭai
kuṉṟu - kuṉṟam - īṟu.
perumaippāṭṭup peyarkaḷaip perumaipporuḷ vērkaḷi ṉiṉṟum tirikkalām.
e-ṭu: kaṭal, kaṭappāṉ paṭākai.
8.	oru poruḷ iṉṉoṉṟiṉ kuṟumaippāṭāyiruntāl, kuṟumaiyaṭaikaḷaiyum īṟukaḷaiyum potumaiyāṉa poruṭpeyarōṭu cērttukkoḷḷalām.
e-ṭu: aṟai - kaṇṇaṟai
akatti - ciṟṟakatti aṭai
muṟam - (muṟṟil) - muccil - īṟu.
kuṟumaippāṭṭup peyarkaḷaik kuṟumaipporuḷ vērkaḷi ṉiṉṟum tirikkalām.
e-ṭu: kuṇil, kukkal, kuṉṟi, ciṟukkaṉ.
9. 	oppumaipaṟṟic cila peyarkaḷai aṭaicērttum cērā malum uvamaiyākupeyarākavum tirittum vaḻaṅkalām.
e-ṭu: īppuli, karaṭikai, peruccāḷi, maṇippavaḷam.
cf. Lens, so called from its likeness to a lentil seed.
oppumai ētēṉum ōr iyalpupaṟṟi irukkalām.
e-ṭu: tōṇippālam, āmaivaṭai.
ivaṟṟil, tōṇi āmai eṉṉum peyarkaḷ muṟaiyē concave, convex, eṉṉum poruḷpaṭal kāṇka.
10.	ōriṉap poruḷkaḷai, avaṟṟiṉ naṉmaiyum tīmaiyum piṟataṉmaiyum kuṟitta āṭaikaḷaip potuppeyaruṭaṉ cērttup pirittuk kūṟalām.
e-ṭu: naltuḷaci, nāyttuḷaci.
naṉcey, puṉcey.
karumpu, pēykkarumpu.
kaṭalai - koṇṭaik kaṭalai, vērkkaṭalai. paṭṭāṇik kaṭalai.
 11. 	ōriṉap poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikka oruporuṭ coṟkaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām.
 	e-ṭu: kāṟṟu = wind, vaḷi = gas.
	āṅkilac coṟkaḷai yotta tamiḻp pōliyolikaḷ, āṅkilattōṭotta poruḷtariṉ koḷḷappaṭalām.
 	e-ṭu: 	parliament - pārāḷumaṉṟu.
 			bracker - piṟaikkōṭu.
 12. āṅkilac coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷai yaṟintu ataṟkēṟpa moḻipeyarkkalām.
 e-ṭu: colemn - piḻampu.
 colonel, (the leader of a column of soldiers) piḻampar.
 phenamenon (an appearance) - tōṟṟaravu.
am īṟupeṟṟa tōṟṟam eṉṉum col potuvāṉa tōṟṟattaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkataliṉ, aravu īṟupeṟṟa tōṟ ṟaravu eṉṉum col oru vitapput tōṟṟattaik kuṟittaṟ kēṟṟa tātal kāṇka.
13. 	cila āṅkilac coṟkaḷukkuc cemmoḻipeyarppuc (literal) ceytukoḷḷalām.
 	e-ṭu: inspect - uṇṇōkku
 	inspection: uṇṇōkkam.
 	inspector: uṇṇōkkāḷar.
14. cila coṟkaḷ āṅkilattukkum tamiḻukkum potuvā yiruppatāl, avaṟṟai aṅṅaṉamē vaittukkoḷḷalām.
 	e-ṭu: note - nōṭṭu.
 	nōṭu = pār, kavaṉi, nōṭuvatu nōṭṭu, nōṭṭam, nōṭṭam eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar tamiḻilum, nōṭu eṉṉum pakuti allatu ēval kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
15. ōr āṅkilac coṟku nērāṉa tamiḻccollai av vāṅkilac col kuṟikkum pala poruḷilum vaḻaṅkalām.
 	e-ṭu: article = uruppaṭi = 
	1. poruḷ (cāmāṉ)
 	2. paṇṭam
 	3. ceytip pakuti.
16.	oru poruḷiṉ pala kūṟukaḷaik kuṟittaṟku oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷai āḷalām.
 e-ṭu: pork - paṉṟikkaṟi. L. porcus, a pig.
 tumpikkai = yāṉaikkai, tumpi = yāṉai.
17.	cila āṭpeyarkaḷaiyum, av vāḷkaḷaip paṟṟiya vitappuc ceytikaḷaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷāka vaḻaṅkalām.
 	e-ṭu: Boycott, from Captain Boycott who was excommunicated by his neighbours in Ireland in 1881.
18.	coṟkaḷai iyaṉṟavarai poruḷ mayakkamiṉṟi amaikka vēṇṭum.
19.	ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷukku, ataṉ ayaṉ moḻip peyarē amaiyavēṇṭumeṉpatu yāppuṟaviṉṟu.
 	e-ṭu: ostrich = tīkkōḻi.
 	cycle = mitivaṇṭi.
20.	tamiḻukkillātu piṟamoḻikaṭkuḷḷa collākka muṟai kaḷil, centamiḻ iyalpiṟ kēṟṟavaṟṟait tamiḻilum taḻuvik koḷḷalām.
āṅkilattil peyarccoṟkaḷai viṉaiccoṟkaḷākap payaṉ paṭuttuvatu perumpāṉmai, tamiḻilum, karumpu eṉṉum col, karumpai allatu atu pōṉṟatait tiṉpataik kuṟikkum viṉaic collākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
 toṭarpiṉāl, vaḻivaḻi vevvēṟu poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikkum ākupeyarpōṉṟē, vevvēṟu viṉaikaḷai allatu karuttu kaḷaik kuṟikkum ākuviṉaikaḷumuṇṭu. āṅkilattiluḷḷa focus ṉṉum col ilattīṉil aṭuppu eṉṉum poruḷatu. atu āṅkilattil neruppaic celuttal, oru poruḷait tiruppal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil viṉaiyākavum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. im muṟaiyil tamiḻilum coṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkik koḷḷalām.  (ce.ce. 1941)
kalaiyuṅ kalviyum
paṇṭait tamiḻakattil kalvi ikkālattiṟpōl araciyal tiṇaikkaḷaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iruntatillai. āciriyariṉ muyaṟciyiṉālum potumakkaḷiṉ pōṟṟaraviṉālumē, potuk kalvi nāṭṭiṟ parappappeṟṟu vantatu. kaṟṟuvalla pulavaraiyē aracar ōraḷavu pōṟṟivantaṉar.
potukkalvi: eḻuttum eṇṇum kaṟpitta tuvakkakkalvi yāciriyarkku iḷampālāciriyar eṉṟum, ciṟṟilakkaṇamum nikaṇṭum kaṇakkuṅ kaṟpitta naṭuttarak kalviyāciriyarkkuk kaṇakkāyar eṉṟum, aintilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṟkuriya ilakki yamuṅ kaṟpitta mēṟṟarak kalviyāciriyarkku āciriyar eṉṟum, peyar. āciriyar, nūlāciriyar nuvalāciriyar (pōtakāciriyar) uraiyāciriyar eṉa mūvakaiyar.
tuvakkakkalvi māṇavarkkum naṭuttarak kalvi māṇavarkkum, paḷḷippiḷḷaikaḷ eṉṟum, māṇiyar eṉṟum māṇavar (māṇavakar, māṇākkar) eṉṟum, caṭṭar eṉṟum, peyar. avaruḷ talaimaiyāṉavaṉ caṭṭanampi allatu caṭṭa nampip piḷḷai (caṭṭām piḷḷai) eṉappaṭṭāṉ. mēṟṟarak kalvi māṇavarkku māṇavar eṉṟum maḻapulavar eṉṟum, kaṟṟuc colliyar eṉṟum, peyar.
tuvakkakkalvi nilaiyilum naṭuttarakkalvi nilaiyilum māṇa variṉ peṟṟōrum, mēṟṟarakkalvi nilaiyil māṇavarum, āciriyarkkuc campaḷam iṟuttu avaraippōṟṟi vantaṉar. ēḻaiyāyiruntu āciriyaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyyum māṇa vaṉukku muttarak kalviyum ilavacamāyk kaṟpikkappaṭṭatu.
ciṟappuk kalvi: māṇavar potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, icai nāṭakam kaṇiyam maruttuvam mutaliya ciṟappuk kalaikaḷai, avvakkalaiyāciriyar vāyilāyk kaṟṟaṉar. ak kālattu nūlkaḷellām ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntamaiyiṉ, evvakai nūṟkalvikkum ciṟanta ilakkaṇakkalvi iṉṟiyamaiyātatā yiruntatu.
poruḷ koṭuppōrkkum paṇiviṭai ceyvōrkkum kalaippayiṟci yaḷikkappaṭṭatu.
meynnūṟkalvi: potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, allatu potuk kalviyuñ ciṟappuk kalviyuṅ kaṟṟapiṉ, meyp poruḷaṟivu peṟa virumpiṉavar, kāṭṭakattuḷḷa muṉivariṭam allatu yōkiyariṭam ceṉṟu mūvāṇṭu meynnūṟkalvi kaṟṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
vēṇṭiya kalviyāṇṭumūṉ ṟiṟavātu 
 eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (1134).
ceyyuḷiyaṟṟum tiṟam: eṇṇum eḻuttum tavira evvakaik kalviyum ceyyuḷ vāyilākavē kaṟṟamaiyālum, iḷamaiyi liruntu ceyyuḷ ceytum ceyyuḷiṟ pēciyum payiṉṟamai yālum, paṇṭaip pulavarellām ceyyuḷiyaṟṟun tiṟam ciṟakkappeṟṟiruntaṉar.
aracar tamiḻai vaḷarttalum pulavaraip pōṟṟalum: pāṇṭiyar toṉṟu toṭṭuk ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai kaḻakam (caṅkam) irutti, nūl nilaiyam pōṟṟi, (iṭaiccaṅka nūl nilaiyattil maṭṭum eṇṇāyiratteccam nūliruntatāka vaḻimuṟaic ceyti kūṟum.) mutunūlārāycciyum putu nūlākkamum nikaḻvittu, muttamiḻai vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kaḻakaṅ kulaintapiṉ, muttamiḻaracarum tattam avaiyil ōriru ciṟanta pulavarai yamarttip pōṟṟivantaṉar. avaikkaḷap pulavarkkum, nūlaraṅkēṟiyavarkkum, aracaraip pukaḻntu pāṭiyavarkkum, muṟṟūṭṭum ciṟantaparicum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. āṭal pāṭal vallārum araṅkēṟi āyiratteṇ kaḻañcupoṉ peṟṟaṉar. araṅkēṟṟam aracaṉ muṉṉilaiyilum avvattamiḻiṟ ciṟanta atikāriyiṉ talaimaiyilum naṭaipeṟṟatu. araṅkēṟiya nūlāciriyarai veṇpaṭṭaṇivittu yāṉaimēlēṟṟi nakarvalam varuvippatu vaḻakkam.
ilakkiyap pulavarum camayanūṟ pulavarum coṟpōr nikaḻtti uṇmai nāṭṭaṟkut, talainakartoṟum paṭṭimaṇṭapam eṉṉum tarukkamaṇṭapam iruntatu. pulavariṭait teyvap pulamaiyaiyum piṟaruḷḷak karuttaṟiyum āṟṟalaiyum vaḷarttaṟ poruṭṭu, aracar avvappōtu tam uḷḷattiluḷḷa kiḷarcci mikka karuttaip paṟṟi avaraip pāṭaccolvatuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam pāṭappaṭuvatu kaṇṭacitti eṉappaṭṭatu. araca ruṭaiya uḷḷakkaruttai aṟivippatum aiyuṟavait teḷivippatumāṉa pāṭaṟkup poṟkiḻiyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. ittakaiya camaiyaṅ kaḷil, iṅṅaṉam pāṭiṉārkkituveṉṟu viḷampi muṉṉarē poṟ kiḻiyaic caṅkamaṇṭapattil tūkkiviṭuvatu pāṇṭiyar vaḻakkam.
kuṟikkai (camaciyai) pāṭalum, arikaṇṭam emakaṇṭam pōṉṟa kaṭumpā (ācukavi) pāṭalum, aracar avvappōtu nallicaip pulavarkkum kaṭum pāvalarkkum naṭattivanta ceyyuṭ pōṭṭi vakaikaḷākum.
takka pulavar talaimai yamaiccarākavum, tūtarākavum amarttap peṟṟaṉar. avaikkaḷat talaimaip pulavarkkum, araṅkēṟiya pulavarkkum, paṭṭimaṇṭapattil veṉṟa pulavarkkum, civikai kuṭai koṭi mutaliya koṟṟac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. pulavariṉ parinturaikkum vēṇṭukōṭkum iṇaṅkuvatum, avaratu aṟivuraiyaiyum aṟavuraiyaiyum iṭitturaiyaiyum ēṟṟukkoḷvatum, avaratu pulanturaiyaiyum paḻippuraiyai yum poṟuttuk koḷvatum, cevviyaṟi yātum viṭai peṟātum eccamayattilum ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuḷ avaraip pukaviṭuvatum, avarukkup paṇiviṭai ceyvatum, avarai vaḻiviṭukkumpōtu ēḻaṭi piṉpōy mīḷvatum, akkālat taracar palar ceyalkaḷākum.
paricaḷiyāviṭattum tammai avamatittapōtum pulavar aracaraic cāvippatumuṇṭu.
kalvit taravuyarvu: akkālap pulavar kaṭuttup pāṭum āṟṟaluṭaiyarāyiruntu ceyyuḷilēyē tammuḷ uraiyāṭi vantaṉar. nikaṇṭu eṉṉum akarātiyuṭpaṭa aṉaittilakkiya mum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē yiruntaṉa. maturaic caṅkattilum piṟa pulavaravaiyilum araṅkēṟiya piṉṉarē nūlkaḷ nāṭṭil ulavalāyiṉa.
 kalvi vaḷarcci: talaiccaṅkattil irunta pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟapattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaraip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar eḻuva reṉṟum; iṭaiccaṅkattil irunta pulavar aimpattoṉpatiṉ mar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar mūvāyiratteḻu nūṟṟu var eṉṟum, avaraip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiya var aivar eṉṟum; kaṭaic caṅkattil irunta pulavar nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaraip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭi yaruḷ kaviyaraṅ kēṟiyavar mūvar eṉṟum, muccaṅka varalāṟu kūṟum.
itupōtuḷḷa kaṭaiccaṅka ilakkiyattiliruntu aṟiyappaṭum pulavar ēṟattāḻa eḻunūṟṟuvar āvar. avaruḷ makaḷir irupattiruvarum aracar paṉṉiruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarum āvar.
aṟuvaivāṇikaṉ, ōlaikkaṭaikkāraṉ, kaṇiyaṉ, kūttaṉ, kūlavāṇikaṉ, pāṇaṉ, porunaṉ, maruttuvaṉ, vaṇṇakkaṉ mutaliya palvakait toḻilāḷarum; kāvaṟpeṇṭu, araci, kuṟa makaḷ, pēymakaḷ mutuliya palatara makaḷirum; pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
kaṭaic caṅkattiṟkup piṟkālattilum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉārum kulacēkarāḻvārum kaṇṭarātittarum ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaṉum pōlum aracarum, tiruveṇkāṭarpōlum vaṇikarum, 2 ām auvaiyārum kāraikkālammaiyārum pōlum makaḷirum, palar pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta pulavar, caṅkakālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum ettuṇaiyarō aṟiyōm.
ācukavikaḷ kācu eṉṟu piṟkālattil vāṅkappaṭṭa vari, araca voppam peṟṟu avar vāḻkkaic celaviṟku vāṅkappaṭṭatu pōlum!
aracar ilakkiyat toṇṭu: aracar pulavarai nūliyaṟṟumāṟu ūkkiyum tāmum nūliyaṟṟiyum vantatuṭaṉ, pala taṉippāṭal kaḷaiyum pāvum poruḷum aḷavum paṟṟit tokuttum tokuppittum uḷḷaṉar. naṟṟiṇai tokuppittōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ paṉṉāṭu tanta māṟaṉvaḻuti. kuṟuntokai tokuttōṉ pūrikkō. aiṅkuṟunūṟu tokuppittōṉ yāṉaikkaṭcēy māntarañ cēralirum poṟai, akanāṉūṟu tokuppittōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirapperuvaḻuti.

 paṭittu cēṭeṟiyuñ ceṅkaṭ kavariyum  (kallāṭam, 59: 90)

kaḻakam
kaḻakam eṉpatu ciṟappākavum perumpāṉmaiyākavum kaṟṟōr kūṭṭattaiyē kuṟikkumēṉum, ciṟupāṉmai cūtāṭu miṭattaiyum kuṟikkumāṟu ilakkiyattil āḷappaṭṭuḷatu.
kaḻakam eṉṉum colliṉ vērum varalāṟum teriyiṉ. (poruḷ teḷivām)
kul - kula - kulavu. kulavutal = kūṭutal.
kula - kala.
kala - kalavi.
kala - kalavai, kalappu - kalampu - kalampum - katampam.
kalampu - kalampakam
kala - kalaṅku - kalakku - kalakkam
kala - kalakam, kalām, kalāpam, kalāpaṉai.
iruvar allatu iru kaṭciyār kalantē poruvatāl, kalattaṟ karuttiṟ pōrkkaruttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. kai kalattal eṉṉum vaḻakkaiyum, poru, camar eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷaiyum nōkkuka.
mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟṟirivup paṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu, kalattaṟcoṟku, irupāṟ kūṭṭam, kalappu, kalakkam, kalakam eṉṉum nāṟ poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷamai kāṇka. nīrum maṇṇum pōlap pala poruḷkaḷ allatu karuttukaḷ kalappatē kalakkam.
kul - kuḷ - kaḷ. kaḷḷutal = kūṭutal. poruntutal, ottal.
kaḷ - kaḷam = ērkkaḷamum pōrkkaḷamumpōlap palar kūṭumiṭam.
kaḷam - kaḷaṉ = avaikkaḷam
kaḷaṉ - kaḻaṉi = ērkkaḷam uḷḷa vayal.
kaḷam - kaḷar = avai.
kaḷar - kaḷari = avai, kalvi nāṭakam mal vil payilum araṅku, vaḻakkumaṉṟam, toḻil ceyyum iṭam.
kaḷḷa = pōla.
kaḷḷa matippa vella vīḻa  (tol. 1235)
kuḷ - kuḻu - kuḻampu - kuḻappu - kuḻappam.
kuḻavi = tiraṇṭa arai kal.
kūṭutaṟ karuttil tiraṭcik karuttut tōṉṟum.
cērē tiraṭci   (tol. 846)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka.
kuḻu - kuḻūu
kuḻu - kuḻām.
kuḻu - kuḻampu.
kuḻu - kuḻumu - kuḻuvu. kuḻumu - kuḻumal, kuḻumam.
kuḻuvu - kuḻuval, kuḻumutal = kūṭutal, kuḻuvutal = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal.
kuḻumu - kaḻumu - kaḻumal = mayakkam, niṟaivu, mikuti.
kuḻu - keḻu - keḻumu - keḻuvu.
keḻumutal = poruntutal, keḻuvutal = poruntutal, mayaṅkutal.
keḻuvu = naṭpu, keḻuva ōr uvamavurupu
keḻu - keḻi = naṭpu
kuḻu - kuḻai = tiraṇṭa kātaṇi.
kuḻu - (kuḻaku) - kaḻaku - kaḻakam = ōlakkam (Durbar), pulavaravai, kalai payiliṭam, paṭaikkalam, mal payiliṭam, cūtāṭumiṭam.
kuḷ - (kūḷ) - kūṭu.
kaḻakam eṉṉum col. kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷkoṇṭa kuḻu eṉṉum vēraṭiyākap piṟantiruttalāl, potuvākak kūṭṭam eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvatākum. atu, mutaṟkaṇ, ciṟanta kūṭṭa mākiya pulavaravaikkup peyarāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntami ḻēṉai
maṇṇi ṭaiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭaippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō.
eṉṉum parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇac ceyyuḷil , kaḻakam pulavaravaiyaik kuṟittatu. caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟku nēr teṉcol kaḻakam eṉpatē.
paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muttamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷum tamakku muntiya tamiḻilakkiyattai ārāyntu vantatiṉāl, kaṟṟōr payilum iṭattaikkuṟitta kaḻakam eṉṉumcol, nāḷaṭaivil, kaṟpōr payilum iṭattaiyum kuṟikkat talaippaṭṭatu. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa maturai yeṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapurat teṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu uttara maturai yeṉpa. eṉṟu iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai muṟaiyē muttamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷaiyum paṟṟik kūṟutal kāṇka. itaṉāl, iḷaiñar kalvi payilumiṭattai.
kantaṉai yaṉaiyavar kalaiteri kaḻakam  (kamparā. nāṭṭup. 48)
eṉṟār kampar.
iṉi, īṇṭaik kaḻakam eṉṉum coṟku, viṟkalai maṟkalai mutali ya pōrttuṟai payilum iṭam eṉavum poruḷuraippar. atuvē kampar karuttāyiṉ pōrkkalaiyaiyum kalvit tuṟai kaḷuḷ oṉṟāka aṭakkik koḷka. tivākaram, paṭaikkalam, maṟpōr mutaliyaṉa payilum iṭattaiyum kaḻakam eṉak kuṟikkum.
ērkkaḷattilum avaikkaḷattilumpōl pōrkkaḷattilum makkaḷ kūṭuvatāl, kaḷam eṉṉum col am mūṉṟiṭattaiyum kuṟittatu. cūtāṭutaṟkum makkaḷ kūṭuva tālum, cūtum oruvakaip pōrātalālum kaḻakam eṉṉum col mūṉṟāvatāka allatu iṟutiyākac cūtāṭumiṭat taiyum kuṟittatu. cūtum oruvakaip pōr eṉpataic cūtu porutal eṉṉum vaḻakkiṉālum, cūtu pōrc carukkam eṉṉum villi pāratac carukkap peyarālum, uttiyālum, aṟiyalām, vēttavaiyiluḷḷārellām ovvoru tuṟaiyil tērcci peṟṟirupparātaliṉ, ōlakkamum pulavaravaiyiṉ aṭaṅkum. ākavē, pulavaravai, kalvi payiliṭam, cūtāṭumiṭam ākiya mūvēṟu poruḷaik kaḻakam eṉṉum col kuṟikkum eṉpatu teḷivām.
paḻakiya celvamum paṇpum keṭukkum
kaḻakattuk kālai pukiṉ.  (937)
 eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, cūtāṭumiṭattaiyum,
kaḻakat tiyaluṅ kavaṟṟi ṉilaiyum  (358)
eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālait toṭaril, cūtāṭṭaiyum, kaḻakam eṉṉum col kuṟittatu.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟippiṉum itu arukiya vaḻakkē. iṟṟai ulaka vaḻakkilum, nāṭṭāṇmaik kaḻakam, centamiḻk kaḻakam, caiva cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, māṇavar kaḻakam, eṉak kaḻakam eṉṉum col cūtāṭukaḷamallāta piṟa kaḷarikaḷaiyē kuṟittal kāṇka.
kuḻu eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟē kuḻām, kuḻumam mutaliya palvēṟu tokutip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruppiṉum, avai yāvum parupporuḷilaṉṟi nuṇporuḷil ottaṉa valla.
kuḻu = ciṟukūṭṭam (committee).
kuḻūu = maṟaiporuṭ kuṟiyīṭu vaḻaṅkum toḻiṟ kulam allatu vakuppu.
kuḻām = kuḻuviṉum caṟṟup periya kūṭṭam (party).
kōṣṭi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku nērāṉa teṉcol kuḻām.
pajaṉaik kōṣṭi eṉpatait tiruppāṭṭuk kuḻām eṉṉalām.
kuḻumal, kuḻuval = kuḻāttiṉum periya kūṭṭam (gathering).
 kuḻumam = vaṇikar caṅkamum toḻilāḷikaḷ caṅkamum pōṉṟa kūṭṭam (guild).
kuḻumpu = yāṉai nirai, tōḻamaik kūṭṭam (herd, company).
kaḷiṟṟuk kuḻumpiṉ  (maturaik. 24).
kaḻakam = caṅkam (society, association).
ākavē, kuḻu, kuḻūu, kuḻām, kuḻumam eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu periya tokutikaḷām. kaḻakam eṉpatu uyartiṇaikkē yuriyatāyp perumpālum kalvipaṟṟiyatum nilaiyāṉatum kuḻumattiṟkuc camamāṉatumāṉa kūṭṭattaiyē kuṟikkum. itaṉālēyē, paṇṭait tamiḻc caṅkattaik kaḻakam eṉṟār parañcōti muṉivar. talaikkaḻakap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar: iṭaikkaḻakap pulavar aimpattoṉ patiṉmar: kaṭaik kaḻakap pulavar nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar. ākavē, kaḻakam eṉpatu ciṟu kuḻuvaik kuṟippataṉṟu.
iṉi cūtāṭu karuvikkuk kaḻaṅku, kaḻaṅkam eṉap peyariruppa tāl, kaḻaṅkam allatu kaḻaṅkakam eṉṉuñ col kaḻakam eṉat tokkuc cūtāṭu miṭattaik kuṟittatōveṉa aiyuṟavum iṭa muṇṭu. āyiṉum, āyntu nōkkuvārkku, mēṟkāṭṭiya varalāṟē uṇmaiyāṉa teṉpatu pulaṉām.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, kaḻakam eṉpatu kuḻu eṉṉum vēraṭi yāyp piṟantateṉṟum, kūṭṭam allatu kūṭumiṭam eṉpataiyē aṭippaṭaip poruḷākak koṇṭateṉṟum, mutaṟkaṇ kaṟṟōravaiyai allatu kaṟpōr kūṭṭattaik kuṟittē piṉṉarc cūtāṭumiṭattaik kuṟittateṉṟum, caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟ poruḷil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya tūya paḻan tamiḻc colleṉṟum, paṇṭaiyilakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantu paṭṭa ikkālattil tirukkuṟaḷaik koṇṭu coṟ poruḷ varicaiyaik kāṇamuṭiyāteṉṟum collārāycci yiṉ tuṇaiyiṉālēyē atait tuṇital kūṭumeṉṟum, teṟ ṟeṉat terintukoḷka. (teṉmoḻi)

kaḻiyal viḷaiyāṭṭu
oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokaiyiṉar, kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil neruṅki niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoru kaiyilum ovvoru kuṟuṅkaḻi ēnti avaṟṟaip piṟar kaḻikaḷōṭu tākkiyaṭittu, piṉṉip piṉṉiyum cuṟṟic cuṟṟiyum varum āṭṭu, kaḻiyal eṉappaṭum.
itu pakalilum āṭaṟkuriyatāyiṉum, potuvāka iravilēyē āṭappeṟum. itaṟkum kummikkup pōl oru taṉivakaip pāṭṭuṇṭu. atu,

kaḷ
kaḷ eṉṉum mutaṉilai muṟkālattil kaḷavu ceytalaik kuṟittatē. kaḷḷam, kaḷḷattaṉam, kaḷḷaṉ, kaḷavu eṉṉum coṟkaḷai nōkkuka. piṟkālattil kaḷ eṉṉum mutaṉilai taṉ poruḷai iḻantapiṉ, toḻiṟ peyaroṭu tuṇaiviṉai cērtta kaḷavu cey eṉṉum kūṭṭuc col mutaṉilai tōṉ ṟiṟṟu. kaṭkuvāṉ parikkil ñēluvāṉ parikkēṇam eṉṉum malaiyāḷap paḻamoḻiyaiyum nōkkuka. (ti.ma. 166.)
kaḷ uṇṇāmai
nīrākavō kaṭṭiyākavō pukaiyākavō iruntu, veṟiyiṉāl uṇarvai maṟaikkum poruḷkaḷai uṇṇāmai.
kaḷḷutal maṟaittal. ivviṉai iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
kaḷ - kaḷḷam = maṟaippu. kaḷ - kaḷavu = maṟaippu.
kaḷ eṉṉum peyar munnilaip poruṭkum (nīr, kaṭṭi, pukai) potuvēṉum, peruvaḻakkup paṟṟi nīrvaṭivāṉa poruḷaiyē kuṟikkum. atu iyaṟkaiyum ceyaṟkaiyum eṉa iruvakaittu muṉṉatu paṉai teṉṉai mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu iṟakkuvatu, piṉṉatu arici kāykaṉi mutaliyavaṟṟaip puḷikka vaittuk kāycciyum eṭuppatu. (ti.ma. ati. 93.)
kaḷmayakku
aimpulaṉum aṭaṅki aṭiyōṭu uṇarvu iḻattalum, vāykā vātu maṟaiveḷippaṭuttalum, pittar pōṟpitaṟṟalum, āṭai vilakalum, aṟṟam maṟaiyāmaiyum, tī nāṟṟam vīcutalum, vāy nurai taḷḷutalum, vaḻiyil kiṭattalum, vaḻip pōkkar paḻitta lum, īmoyttalum, iḷañciṟār cirittalum (kaḷmayakkam ākkum). (ti.ma. 923)

kal - kaḷ = karumai. kaḷ - kaḷam = karumai. kaḷam - kaḷaṅku = karu mai, kuṟṟam. kaḷaṅku - kaḷaṅkam = karumai, kaṟai, maṟu, kuṟṟam. kaḷaṅkaṉ = maṟuvuḷḷa mati.
mā.vi.a. mūlam aiyuṟaviṟkiṭamāṉatu (“etym. doubtful”) eṉṟu, kuṟittiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. (va.va: 111)
kaḷac ceyal
kaṇṇait tōṇṭi viṭuvēṉ mūḷaiyai uṟiñci viṭuvēṉ
 tōlai urittu viṭuvēṉ īralait tiṉṟuviṭuvēṉ
kuṭalai mālaiyākap pōṭṭuviṭuvēṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa accuṟuttukkaḷ, anākarikak kālap pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷil ceyalaḷa vāy nikaḻntavaiyē. (col. 25.)
kaḷappāḷar (kaḷappirar)
(tōrā. ki.pi. 300 - 590)
kaṭaikkaḻaka muṭiviṟkuppiṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭai munnūṟṟāṇṭu āṇṭa, kaḷappāḷar vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavareṉṟum, pal vakuppiṉareṉṟum, palavāṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. avar palvēḷviccālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti pirāmaṇarkku aḷitta vēḷvikkuṭip paṭṭaiyattai maṟuttataṉālum, muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṉar eṉṟu collappaṭuvataṉālum, tamiḻarākavum iruntirukkalām. kaḷapputal kāṭu veṭṭit tiruttutal, nellai māvaṭṭattiṟ caṅkaranayiṉār kōvil vaṭṭattil, kaḷappāḷar kuḷam eṉṟu ōr ūr uḷḷatu. kaḷappilār eṉpatu kaḷḷar vakuppāriṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka, paṇṭitar na.mu. vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭār kuṟittuḷḷār.
kaḷam
kaḷam1 - kala (kh) - i.vē.
kaḷḷutal = kūṭutal. kaḷ - kaḷam = uḻavar kūṭi vēlai ceyyumiṭam .
kaḷam - kaḷamar = uḻavar. ērkkaḷam, pōrkkaḷam (pōraṭikku miṭam) eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷai nōkkuka. kaḷam - kaḷaṉ - kaḻaṉi = vayal.
kaḷam2 - kaḷa (g)
kaḷḷutal = kalattal, poruntutal. kaḷ - kaḷam = talaiyai uṭaluṭaṉ poruttum kaḻuttu, toṇṭai.
pāṭukaḷa makaḷirum  (cilap. 6: 157).
kaḷam - (kaḷattu) - kaḻuttu.
vaṭavar kal eṉṉum ceyaṟkaiyaṭiyai kru (viḻuṅku) eṉṉum colliṉ tiripākak koṇṭu, kala eṉpataṟku viḻuṅkum uṟuppu eṉṟu poruṭkāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvar. (va.va: 111 - 112)

kaṟkālam  (Stong Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 5,00,000 - 50,000.)
muntiyal tamiḻarāṉa anākarika māntar kallāṟ palvēṟu karuvi kaḷaic ceytu koṇṭa kālam kaṟkālamākum. atu paḻaṅkaṟ kālam, putukkaṟkālam eṉa iru piriviṉatu.
(1) paḻaṅ kaṟkālam (Old Stone Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 5,00,000 - 1,00,000.)
tamiḻariṉ mutaṟkāla muṉṉōrāṉa kumarināṭṭu māntar, poṉṉam (metal) oṉṟum kaṇṭupiṭikku muṉ, killi (kalli), veṭṭi, kutti, katti, uḷi, cutti (cuttiyal), camaṭṭi (cammaṭṭi), kuntam, kūntālam, kōṭari, kuttuk kōṭari mutaliya karuvi kaḷaik kallāl muraṭṭu vēlaippāṭākac ceytu, payaṉpaṭutti vanta kālam paḻaṅkaṟkālamākum.
avar vāḻnta iṭam kumarimalait toṭariṉ aṭivāramāṉa kuṟiñci nilam.
avar ceyta toḻil kāykaṉi paṟittal, kiḻaṅkakaḻtal, tēṉeṭuttal, vēṭṭaiyāṭal eṉpaṉa. kiḻaṅku tōṇṭaṟkuk kōṇalillāta kompaiyum kūrākac cetukkiya vaṉkuccai 
yum, vēṭṭaiyāṭaṟkuk kallaiyum kuṟuntaṭiyaiyum neṭuntaṭiyaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar uṇṭavuṇavu paccaiyum cuṭṭaṉavumāṉa iyaṟkai viḷaivu kaḷum vēṭṭaik kaṟiyum ākum. vēṉiṟ kālattil mūṅkilum maraṅkaḷum urāyntu paṟṟiya neruppil akappaṭṭuc cetta vilaṅku paṟavaiyūṉ, cuvaiyākavum metuvākavum iruntatu kaṇṭu, vēṭṭaikkaṟiyaic cuṭṭut tiṉṉum paḻakkattai mēṟkoṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar uṭuttiya uṭai, kōrai taḻait toṭaiyum taiyilai yum, marappaṭṭaiyum vilaṅkuttōlumākum. āppirikka anākarika māntar, innūṟṟāṇṭu muṟpakutiyilum, cila marappaṭṭaikaḷai ūṟavaittut taṭṭi virivākki āṭaiyāka aṇintaṉar. kumarināṭu teṉṉāppirikkāvuṭaṉ iṇain tiruntataṉāl, kumarināṭṭu māntarum anākarika nilaiyil attakaiya maravuriyai aṇintiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar uṟaiyuḷ (taṅkumiṭam) malaikkukai, palkavar marakkavaṭu, paraṇ, kallāl amaitta vaḷimaṟai (kāṟṟai maṟaikkum ciṟu kuṭil) ākiyavai.
avar aṇinta aṇikaḷ, marukkoḻuntu pōlum naṟuntaḻai, maṇamuḷḷa allatu aḻakiya malar, mayiṟpīli, cēvaliṟaku, pulippal tāli mutaliyaṉa.
avar puḻaṅkiya nīrkkalam mūṅkil nāḻiyum curaik kuṭukkai pōṉṟa neṟṟuk kūṭum.
avar maṇamuṟai, paruvam vantapiṉ, perumpāṉmai āṇ pālār, orōviṭattup peṇpālār, iṇaiviḻaiccu vēṭkai nērnta pōtellām etirp pālārai icaivittō icaiyumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyō valintō puṇarum potumaṇam (Promiscuity) ākum. kūṭivāḻum kuṭumpa vāḻvu avariṭai yillai. ataṉāl, oru talaivaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭa vāḻvum (Community Life) avariṭaiyillai. vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavaikaḷum pōl, pakalil uṇavutēṭiyuṇpatum, uṟaiviṭattil irāttaṅkuvatumē avar iyalpāyiruntatu.
koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷ etirppaṭṭapōtu allatu iruppiṭam vantu tākkiyapōtu, avar tammiṭamuḷḷa kaṟkaruviyun kanta maramuṅ koṇṭu cilavaṟṟai etirttuk koṉṟiruttal vēṇ ṭum; kollamuṭiyāta valiyavaṟṟiṟku marattiṉ mītēṟiyō putaruḷḷum potumparuḷḷum maṟaintō tappiyiruttal vēṇṭum; arimāvum yāṉaiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟai iravuk kālattil tī vaḷarttu viraṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum. vilaṅkuka ḷoṭum pakaivaroṭum pōriṭa nākaraip pōl talaiyiluṅ kompukaḷai aṇintirukkalām.
mutaṟkālattil tīyaik kaṇṭu mikamika añciṉārēṉum, piṉpu mella mella ataṉ palvakaip payaṉpāṭṭaik kaṇṭu, vilaṅ kāṇṭi māntarum ataip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉar. aṇukātum akalātu miruntu, kuḷirkāytal, iṟaicci cuṭutal, iruḷ nīkkal, kuḷaviyaik kalaittut tēṉeṭuttal, koṭu vilaṅku veruṭṭal mutaliyaṉa tīyiṉ payaṉkaḷ. nimirnta kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecanthropus Erectus), eṉṉum cāli (cāvaka) māntaṉiṉum muntiyavaṉāṉa pīkkiṉ māntaṉ eṉṉum cīṉa māntaṉ (Sinanthropus Pekinensis), neruppait taṉ kukaiyil vaittup payaṉpaṭuttiyatākac collappaṭuvatāl, cālimān taṉai yotta kumarimāntaṉ neruppaip payaṉpaṭuttiyatil viyappoṉṟumillai.
naṉmaiyō tīmaiyō iraṇṭumō ceytavaṟṟaiyum ceyva tākak karutappaṭṭavaṟṟaiyum, mutaṟkāla māntar teyvamāka vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. avai tī, katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, iṟantōr āvi pēy, nākam mutaliyaṉa. teyvam eṉṉum peyar tīyaik kuṟikkuñ colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatē. maraṅkaḷiṉ urāyvu, iṭi, miṉṉal ākiya mūvakaikaḷil iyaṟkait tī uṇṭā yiruttal vēṇṭum. tīppaṟṟi eriyuṅ kiḷaikaḷiṉiṉṟu, koḷḷik kaṭṭaikaḷaip piritteṭuttuk koṇṭupōyp paḻaṅkaṟ kāla māntar payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
marantoṟum, malaitoṟum, nīrnilaitoṟum āvi allatu pēy kuṭikoṇṭiruntatākavum, paḻaṅkāla māntar nampiṉar.
iṟantavaruṭampaik kuṭiyiruppiṟkuc caṟṟut tolaivāṉa iṭattiluḷḷa kuḻiyiliṭṭu, kākaṅkaḻukum nariyōriyum tiṉṉā vāṟu maṇṇāl mūṭiviṭuvatu avar vaḻakkam.
moḻittuṟaiyil, iyaṟkai moḻi (Natural Language) allatu muḻaittal moḻi (Inarticulate Speech) eṉṉum muntu moḻik kuriya.
(1) uṇarcci yolikaḷ (Emotional Sounds)
(2) viḷiyolikaḷ (Vocative Sounds)
(3) oppolikaḷ (Imitative Sounds)
(4) kuṟippolikaḷ (Symbolic Sounds)
(5) vāycceykai yolikaḷ (Sounds produced with oral gestures)
(6) cuṭṭolikaḷ (Deictic Sounds)
ākiya aṟuvakai yolikaḷum, paḻaṅkaṟkālattilēyē muṟaiyē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉ iṟutik kālattil āttirēliyā nōkkiyum teṉṉāppirikkā nōkkiyum kumari māntar paṭarntiruttal vēṇṭum. nālvakai yeḻuttil mutalatāṉa paṭaveḻuttu (Picture writing or Pictograph) paḻaṅkaṟ kālattil tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
iyaṟkaiyuṇavu tēṭat terintatum, eḷiya uṟaiyuḷ amaitta tum, aṟṟam maṟaittatum, aḻakuṇarcci tōṉṟiyatum, tīyaip payaṉpaṭuttiyatum, iyaṟkaimoḻi vaḷarttatum, paṭaveḻut taippayaṉ paṭuttiyatum paḻaṅkaṟkāla māntariṉ aṟivunilai yeṉṉalām.
(2) putuk kaṟkālam (New Stone Age)
tōrā. ki.mu. 1,00,000 - 50,000
muntiyal kumari māntar, paḻaṅ kaṟkālak karuvikaḷait tiṇkaṉak karuṅkallāl, vaḻavaḻappākavum mikak kūriyaṉavākavum ceytu koṇṭa kālamputuk kaṟkālamākum.
avar vāḻnta iṭam kuṟiñciyum ataiyaṭutta mullaiyumākum. vāḻkkait tiruttattālum māntar tokaip perukkālum, iyaṟkaiyākavē avar mullai nilattilum paraviyiruttal vēṇṭum.
avar ceyta toḻil, perumpāṉmai kālnaṭai vaḷarppum vāṉāvārip payir viḷaippum; ciṟupāṉmai paḻaṅkaṟkāla māntar ceytaṉa.
malaiyaṭi vāraṅkaḷilum malaimēlum vāḻum āṭu māṭu erumai ākiya mūvilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷaiyum, avar piṭittup paḻakki vīṭṭu vilaṅkākkiṉar. mūṉṟum pāl tantaṉa. āṭu pāl taruva toṭu ūṉuṇavumāyiṟṟu. kāḷaimāṭum kaṭāverumaiyum ēruḻaviṟkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. māṉaiyum āmāṉaiyum kāṭṭup paṉṟiyaiyum muyalaiyum uṭumpaiyum piṭittu ūṉuṇaviṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iṉṟu paṉṟiyiṟaicci yuṇpavar orucārārē. avariṉuñ ciṟutokaiyiṉar māṭṭiṟaicci yuṇpavar. paṟavai kaḷuḷ, kōḻi, puṟā, kuyil, kāṭai, katuvāli ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ūṉai virumpiyuṇṭaṉar. kāṭṭukkōḻiyaip paḻakki vīṭṭuk kōḻiyumākkiṉar.
vāṉāvārip payirkaḷ eṉpaṉa, ērkkāṭum kottukkāṭu mākiya nilattil maḻaiyiṉālēyē viḷainta, tiṉai varaku pōṉṟa ciṟutavacaṅkaḷum avarai tuvarai pōṉṟa payaṟu vakai kaḷumākum. pulveḷikaḷāyuḷḷavaṟṟaik kālnaṭai mēycca lukku viṭṭuviṭṭu, kuṟuṅkāṭum peruṅkāṭumā yuḷḷavaṟṟait tīyiṉāṟ cuṭṭerittuk koṉṟu viḷai nilamākkiya viṭam, piṟkālattiṟ kollai yeṉappaṭṭatu. aṭarnta marañceṭi koṭikaḷāl iruṇṭu kiṭavātu veṭṭaveḷiyāṉa nilamel lām, kaṇṇiṟkup pulaṉāṉa tiṉāl, pulam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pulantoṟum parappiya tēriṉi rāyiṉum  (puṟam, 10, mēṟpaṭi ; 12)
pulaṅkeṭa viṟukkum varampil tāṉai  (mēṟpaṭi 16-9)
 eṉṉum aṭikaḷai nōkkuka.
viḷai nilattilum vīṭṭilumuḷḷa kūlaṅkaḷai elikaḷ tiṉṟu keṭuttataṉāl, avaṟṟaik kollak kāṭṭup pūṉaiyaiyum; āṭukaḷai narikaḷ piṭittut tiṉṟataṉāl, avaṟṟai viraṭṭi mantaiyaik kākkak kāṭṭu nāyaiyum; vīṭṭiṟ paḻakkiṉar.
nilattiṟ pūcippūci metteṉṟu naṭappatāl, pūṉai pūcaiyeṉap paṭṭatu. pūcu - pūcai - pūṉai - pūñai. nāykku āṭpaṟṟuppōl pūṉaikku iṭappaṟṟu mikuntiruppatāl, tolvaravuṇarcci mikka āṇpūṉai paruttuk koḻutta nilaiyil iṉṟum kāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, kaṇṭār añcattakka verukāki viṭukiṉṟatu.
vevvāy verukiṉaip pūcai eṉṟalum  (tol. mara. 69)
verukku viṭaiyaṉṉa veruṇōkku  (puṟam. 324)
piḷḷai verukiṉ muḷḷeyiṟu puraiya  (mēṟpaṭi 117)
eṉpavaṟṟāl, tamiḻakattiṟ kāṭṭuppūṉai toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntuvaruvatai aṟiyalām.
iṉi, mēlaiyāriyap pūṉaip peyarkaḷ tamiḻccolliṉ tiripāyiruttalāl, tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟē pūṉai airōppāviṟkuc ceṉṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
pūcai - E. puss - pussy. MLG. pus, Norw. puse, Du. pces itaṉ mūlam teriyavillai yeṉṟum, orukāl mutaṟkālattil itu oru pūṉaiviḷic collāyiruntirukkalāmeṉṟum, erutan tuṟai (Oxford) āṅkila akaramutali kūṟukiṉṟatu. nellai vaṭṭārat til iṉṟum pūṉaiyaip pūcupūcu eṉṟu aḻaippataik kāṇalām.
kotti (ka.) - E. cat, OE. catte, ME, catt(e), LL. cattus, catta, ONF, cat, F. chat, ON. kottr, OHG. kazza. kottai = kuruṭu kuruṭaṉ. pūṉaikkup pakaliṟ cariyākak kaṇ teriyāmaiyāṟ kotti yeṉappaṭṭatu.
mullai nilattai uḻak kalappaiyaiyum, vēṭṭaiyāṭaṟku vaḷari - vaṇari (vaḷai taṭi), kavaṇ, vil mutaliya karuvikaḷaiyuṅ kaiyāṇṭaṉar. kallōṭu, maram, kompu, maruppu (tantam), elumpu mutaliyaṉavum mutaṟkaruviyākappayaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. māṭṭuk kompum erumaik kompum yāṉai maruppumē mutaṟkaṇ vaḷaitaṭiyākap payaṉpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. avai kiṭaiyāviṭattē avai pōṉṟa vaḷaitaṭikaḷaip payaṉ paṭuttiyiruppar.
mullai nilap pāṟaikaḷiluḷḷa paḷḷaṅkaḷilum kuḻikaḷilu muḷḷa nīr, katiravaṉ vemmaiyālum kāṭṭut tīyālum kāynta pōtu, atiṟkiṭanta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ cuṭṭavuṇa viṉum paruttum meṉmaiyākiyum cuvaimikkum iruntataik kaṇṭa māntar, kaṟkalaṅkaḷil varaku tiṉai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ariciyaic cōṟākkavum, avarai tuvarai mutaliya payaṟukaḷai avikkavum, kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. paṭṭa maraṅkaḷ urāyntu aṭikkaṭi neruppeḻak kaṇṭatiṉāl, iyaṟkai neruppillāta pōtu ñelikōlāṟ kaṭaintu ceyaṟkai neruppaiyum uṇṭākkik koṇṭaṉar. vīṭutoṟum nāḷtoṟum vēḷai toṟum tīkkaṭaiyat tēvaiyillāvāṟu, ūr muḻuvataṟkum potuvāka ōr iṭattil iravum pakalum kaṭṭaiyeriya viṭṭu aviyā neruppaip pēṇiyiruttalum vēṇṭum.
uṭukka maravuri pōṉṟa nārāṭaiyum, pōrttikkoḷḷa āṭṭu mayirk kampaḷiyum, mutaṟkaṇ kaippiṉṉalākavum piṉṉart taṟinecavākavum, avar ceytu koṇṭaṉar. paṭukka mūṅkiṟ pāyum ōlaippāyum muṭaintu koṇṭaṉar.
cantaṉac cēṟṟālum vaṇṇaccāntālum, irupālārum, ciṟappākap peṇṭir, mēṉimuḻutum palvēṟu ōviyam varaintu koḷvatum, nilaiyāyirukkumāṟu palvakai yuruvaṅkaḷaip paccai kuttik koḷvatum, peruvaḻakkāyiruntatu.
kuṭiyirukka vaṭṭamāṉa kūrai vīṭukaḷaik kaṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. mutalil marattilum marattaṭiyilum vatintataṉāl, vaṭṭamākak kiḷaikaḷ paṭarntum nilattiṟpaṭintumuḷḷa marattiṉ pōṅkaip piṉpaṟṟi, vīṭṭiṉ vaṭivai amaittatākat terikiṉṟatu. cuṟṟuc cuvaraik kalluḷḷa viṭattil maṭcāntupūcik kallālum, aḵtillāviṭattil maṇṇālum, amaittaṉar.
kūlaṅkaḷum kāykaṟikaḷum pōṉṟa keṭṭip poruḷkaḷai iṭṭuvaippataṟku, paṉainārp peṭṭikaḷum mūṅkiṟ kūṭaikaḷum muṭaiyappaṭṭaṉa. nīraiyum nīrpporuḷkaḷaiyum vārttu vaippataṟku, mūṅkil nāḻiyum marattiṟ kaṭaintu koṇṭa kaṭaikālum āṭṭut tōṟpaiyum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. camaippataṟkuk kaṟkalam utaviṟṟu. kallutal tōṇṭu tal, allatu kuṭaital. kallappaṭṭa ēṉam kalam eṉṉap paṭṭatu.
āṭumāṭu uṭumpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tōlai marattilum kalattiṉ vāyilum kaṭṭi ularttiyapōtu, kuccuṅkaiyum paṭṭu iṉṉōcai yeḻuntataik kaṇṭu tuṭitoṇṭakam mutaliya tōlicaik karuvikaḷum; vaṇṭiṉāl tuḷaikkappaṭṭa mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyilum nāṇal taṭṭaiyilum kāṟṟuppukuntapōtu, iṉitāy olittataik kaṇṭu pullāṅkuḻalum; muṟukakkaṭṭiya villiṉ nāṇ teṟittapōtu, iṉṉicai piṟantataik kaṇṭu vil yāḻ eṉṉum narappicaik karuviyum; nāḷaṭaiviṟ putuk kaṟkāla māntar puṉaintu koṇṭaṉar.
iṉpattiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi, vēḷaikkuvēḷai uṇṭi camaikka vum, toḻilukkut tuṇaiyāyirukkavum, uṭaimaikaḷaip pātukākkavum, nōynilaiyil nalam pēṇavum, oru peṇ nilaiyāka vīṭṭilirukka vēṇṭiyiruntatāl; vīṭṭu vāḻkkai ēṟpaṭṭapōtē, ōr āṭavaṉum peṇṭum kūṭivāḻum kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyum ēṟpaṭṭatu. atu ilvāḻkkaiyeṉṟu potu vākac collappaṭiṉum, kūṭṭu vāḻkkaikkum kumukāya vāḻkkaikkum uriya iṉṟiyamaiyāta aṟaṅkaḷait taḻuviya tāl, illaṟam eṉac ciṟappittuc collappeṟum.
akkālam ilvāḻkkait toṭakkak kālamātalāl, paṉmaṉai maṇamum (polygamy) tīrvai (divorce) muṟaiyum peruvaḻakkā yiruntaṉa. peṟṟōrum peṇṇum icaiyāta viṭattu vaṉkavar vum kaḷḷakkaṭattamum, cūḷuraittup peṇṇoṭu kaḷavākak kūṭiyapiṉ cūḷai maṟuttalum niṟaivēṟṟāmaiyum, palaraṟiyak kūṭiyapiṉ oru peṇṇaik kaiviṭutalum, aṭikkaṭi nikaḻntaṉa.
kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyuṅ kūṭiyē makappeṟiṉum, veḷippaṭai yākac cūlkoṇṭu pattu mātam iṭarppaṭṭuc cumantu perunōvoṭu piḷḷai peṟuvatāl, piḷḷaikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa urimaiyum atikāramum, neṭuṅkālam tāykkē iruntu vantatu. itil tantaimār tamakkum paṅkukōṭaṟku, īṉiyaṟpaṭukkai (Couvade) eṉṉum oru valakkārattaik kaiyāṇṭaṉar. atāvatu, maṉaivi piḷḷai peṟṟavuṭaṉ, kaṇavaṉum avaḷaip pōl nōvuṟṟatāka naṭittuppaṭuttukkoṇṭu. taṉakku makappēṟṟu maruttuvam pārkkac colvatu. kuṟattipiḷḷai peṟa, kuṟavaṉ kāyan tiṉṟāṉām. eṉṉum tamiḻp paḻamoḻi, iṉṟum, paṇṭai āppirikka māntariṉ vaḻakkattai yotta viṉai kumari nāṭṭilum iruntataik kuṟippāka uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
ilvāḻkkaiyum nilaiyāṉa kūṭṭukkuṭiyiruppum ēṟpaṭṭa taṉāl, āṅkāṅkup paṟpala vūrkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiṉa. ovvōr ūrilum kuṭivāṇar perumpālum paltalaimuṟaip paṭṭa orē māperuṅ kuṭumpa vuṟaviṉarāyiruntataṉāl, akkuṭumpa mutiyōṉē talaivaṉāyiruntu, kuṟṟavaḻakkut tīrttut taṇṭittum muṟai ceytum vantāṉ. akkālattil makkaḷ vāḻvu nīṇṭiruntataṉāl, makaṉ, tantai, pāṭṭaṉ, pūṭṭaṉ, ōṭṭaṉ (cēyāṉ) eṉṉum aintalaimuṟaiyiṉarum orē kālattil vāḻntiruntaṉar.
āṭumāṭukaṭkup pulviḷaiyavum vāṉāvārip payirkaḷai viḷaivikkavum maḻai iṉṟiyamaiyātatā yiruntataṉāl, maḻait teyvam allatu mukil teyvam paḻaṅkaṟkālat teyvaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ putitākavum ciṟappākavum vaṇaṅkappeṟṟatu. kumari moḻi, muḻaittalmoḻi nilaiyiṉiṉṟu iḻaittal moḻi yil (Articulate Speech),
 (1) acainilai (Isolating or Monosyllabic Stage)
 (2) puṇarnilai (Compounding Stage)
 (3) koḷuvunilai (Agglutinative Stage)
 (4) pakucoṉṉilai (Inflexional Stage)
eṉṉum nālnilaikaḷaip putukkaṟkālam muṭiyumuṉ kaṭan tiruttal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu karutteḻuttut (Ideograph) tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
acainilaikkālattiṟ cīṉariṉ muṉṉōrum, koḷuvu nilaik kālattiṟ cittiyariṉ muṉṉōrum, pakucoṉṉilaik kālat toṭakkattiṟ cumēriyariṉ muṉṉōrum, kumari nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu pirintu pōyiruttal vēṇṭum.
cumēriyar pirintupōṉatai irāmaccantira tīṭcitar eḻutiya tamiḻariṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum (Origin and Spread of the Tamils) eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu teḷika, nakar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ūr eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya nakarp peyar tamiḻāyiruppataiyum, appaṉ eṉṉum muṟaip peyar kaltēyar (Chaldees) allatu pāpilōṉiyar moḻiyil āp eṉṟu tirintiruppataiyum, nōkkuka.
tīyuṇṭākkal, camaittal, vāṉāvārip payir viḷaittal, kāl naṭai vaḷarppu, necavu, ilvāḻkkai, ūrāṭci, iḻaittal moḻiyamaittal, karutteḻuttaip payaṉpaṭuttal eṉpaṉa putukkaṟkāla māntariṉ aṟivu nilaiyaik kāṭṭum.
kaṟkāla māntariṭai vakuppu vēṟupāṭiṉmai
kaṟkāla māntar paḻaṅkaṟkālattiṟ kuṟiñci nilattilum putukkaṟ kālattiṟ kuṟiñciyilum ataiyaṭutta mullai nilattilum vatintārēṉum, tiṇai nilam paṟṟiyō toḻil paṟṟiyō teyva vaṇakkam paṟṟiyō, avariṭai evvakai vakuppu vēṟu pāṭum iruntatillai. ellārum ellāt toḻilum ceytu orē vakaiyāy vāḻntu orē vakuppāyiruntaṉar. paḻaṅkaṟkālat tāḻvu nilaiyum putukkaṟkāla uyarvu nilaiyum ellārkkum potuvākum.
nīlamalaiyiluḷḷa kōttar pal toḻilum terintavarāy orē vakuppārāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṭavar ovvoruvarum uḻavu, kālnaṭaivaḷarppu, necavu, vaṇikam, taccu, kol, taṭṭāra vēlai, calavai, mañcikam (muṭitiruttam) ākiya vāḻkkaip paṇikaḷ aṉaiyavum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. tokai paṟṟiyaṉṟit toḻil paṟṟi oruvarkkum iṉṉoruvar utavi vēṇṭi yatillai. iṅṅaṉamē kaṟkāla māntarum vāḻntiruttal vēṇṭum.
kaṟkālaṅ kaḻintu maruta nila vāḻvu toṭaṅkiya piṉṉarē, toḻiṟ pirivum atupaṟṟiya vakuppu vēṟupāṭun tōṉṟiṉa.
kaṟpu
kaṟpu eṉpatu kaṟpōl uṟutiyāṉa irupāliṭaik kātaṟ paṇpu. kal - kaṟpu. atu maṇapparuvam varai tōṉṟātiruntu piṉpu oruvaraiyē kātalippatu. irupāṟkum potuvāṉatu. ātaliṉ maṉaiviyaiyaṉṟi aṇaṅkaiyum nōkkāta āṇ kaṟpum, kaṇavaṉaiyaṉṟik kāvalaṉaiyum nōkkāta peṇkaṟpum eṉak kaṟpu iru tiṟappaṭṭatām.
kaṟpuṭai maṉaiviyiṉ kaṇṇiyam
ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyirvarai ellā vuyirkaḷum perum pālum āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupāliṉa. iv virupāluḷ etu ciṟantatu eṉṉum viṉāviṟku viṭaiyiṟuppatu aritākum. āṇum peṇṇumākap paṭaikkappaṭṭa ellā vuyirkaḷum irupāluṅ kūṭivāḻvatē iṟaivaṉ tirunōkka mātalāṉum, ak kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyāṉēyē ulakam iṭai yaṟātu iyaṅki varutalāṉum, av vāḻkkaiyil irupālum oṉṟukkoṉṟu tuṇaiyāy otta urimaiya vātalāṉum, ovvoṉṟum ēṉaiya tāṟ ceyyappaṭāta orucār kaṭamaiyai mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷamai yāṉum, irupālum camam eṉpatē koḷḷattakkatām. ik karuttup paṟṟiyē, mutaṟṟāyāṉa ēvāḷ, mutaṟṟantaiyāṉa ātāmiṉ (aṭiyu muṭiyu mallātu iṭaippaṭṭa) vilāvelumpi ṉiṉṟu uṇṭākkappaṭṭatākak kiṟittavamaṟai kūṟum.
perumaiyum uraṉum āṭuu mēṉa
accamum nāṇum maṭaṉumun tuṟutta
niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriya veṉpa  (tol. kaḷavu. 7, 8)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatu. kaḷavoḻukkattil talaivaṉ talaiyiṭattuc ciṟantutōṉṟum kuṇaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, irupālukkumuḷḷa ēṟṟat tāḻvaṉṟu. ik kuṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟumuḷḷaṉavāyiṉ, kaḷaviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭātu irukai kōḷukkum potuvāṉa akat tiṇai yiyal allatu poruḷiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum: aṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭāmai kāṇka.
kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭu maṇanikaḻu maṭṭum pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi ākiya iruvakaik kuṟiyilum talaivaṉ ciṟitum añcātu perumaiyōṭum uraṉōṭum ceṉṟu mīḷvatum, talaivikkut talaivaṉ mītuḷḷa aṉpu kāraṇamāka āṟṟarumaipaṟṟi accan tōṉṟuvatum, kāmakkuṟippu allatu kaḷavu veḷippāṭupaṟṟi nāṇam piṟattalum, ceviliyar koḷuttakkoṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmaiyākiya aṟiyāmaṭamum, kūṭṭattai nikaḻttiyiruntum aṟiyātatu pōṉṟa aṟimaṭamum nikaḻvatu muṇṭu. talaivaṉukku makkaḷum vilaṅkum naccuyirum ākiyavaṟṟāl nērakkūṭiya cētattuṭaṉ. avaṉ teyvattai nōkkiyiṭṭa cūḷuraip poyppāl varakkūṭiya ūṟṟukkum talaivi añciyiruntamai.
antarat teḻutiya eḻuttiṉ māṉa
vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa oḻukalum  (kaṟpiyal. 5)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya aṭikaḷāl uṇaralām.
mēṟkūṟiya irupāṟ kuṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iraṇṭoṉṟu kūṭṭi, aṟivu niṟai ōrppu kaṭaippiṭi eṉṉum nāṉkum āṇpāṟ kuṇameṉṟum, accam maṭam nāṇam payirppu eṉṉum nāṉkum peṇpāṟ kuṇameṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar piṉṉōr.
aṟivoṭu niṟaiyē yōrppuk
kaṭaippiṭi kuṇanāṉ kāmē
nāṇamē maṭamē yacca nāṭṭiya
payirppu nāṉkum
māṇiḻai maṭanallārkku vaitta
nāṟkuṇaṅkaḷākum  (cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu. 12: 16, 17)
avvaiyum kākkaipāṭiṉiyumpōlum pulaviyarum, taṭātakai yum maṅkaiyumpōlum araciyarum, kaṇṇakiyum tilakavatiyum pōlum kaṟpuṭaiyārum. kavuntiyum maṇimēkalaiyum pōlum tuṟaviyarum innāṭṭilum iruntirukka, aṟivu mutaliya naṟ kuṇaṅkaḷai āṇpāṟkē ciṟappākak kūṟiyatu, innāṭṭup peṇṭirkku iṟceṟippu kāraṇamāka aṟivuk kuṟaivum peṇṭirkkellām iyalpākavuḷḷa meṉmaiyum paṟṟiyē yām.
iṟceṟippu peṇṭiriṉ oḻukkakkāppum uyirkkāppum nōkkiyatu. perumpālum veḷiyēṟātu vīṭṭukkuḷḷēyē yiruppavarkku. allatu, ayaliṭañ cellātu uḷḷūrilēyē vatipavarkku aṟivu maṭṭāyiruttal mikuti. namnāṭṭiṟ peṇṭir ciṟṟiḷam paruvattilēyē illaṟattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭu vantamaiyiṉ, avarkku uyartarak kalvikkup pōtiya vāyppu miruntatillai. itaṉāl, oppunōkkiya muṟaiyil, perumpāṉ maipaṟṟi maṭattaip peṇpāṟkurittākkip pētai maṭantai maṭavaral mutaliya peyarkaḷai vaḻaṅkiṉar.
potuvākap peṇṭirkkup pūppaṭaintavuṭaṉ maṇa nikaḻnta maiyiṉ perumpālum patiṉeṭṭu allatu irupatāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kaṇavaṉ, taṉ iḷamaṉaiviyaip pētai allatu maṭantai yeṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟṟamillai. atu. añcaṉ maṭavaṉamē eṉṟāṟ pōla iḷamai nōkkiya arumaiviḷiyēyaṉṟi, aṟiyāmai nōkkiya iḻippu viḷiyaṉṟu. mēlum, namnāṭṭil āṭavariṉ maṇapparuvattiṟku varaiyēyillai; maṇat tokaikkum ellaiyillai. peṇṭirkkō kaimpeṇ ṇāyiṉ maṟumaṇa millai yeṉṟum, vāḻnāḷ muḻutum oruvaṉaiyē maṇakka vēṇṭum eṉṟum varampuḷatu. itaṉāl kaṇavaṉiṉ mūppum maṉaiviyiṉ ciṟṟiḷamaiyum peruvaḻakkām, maṭam eṉṉuñ collukku iḷamai yeṉṟum poruḷ. iḷamaiyil aṟivu mutirāmaiyiṉ, eḻutiṟa iḷampeṇ paruvaṅkaḷil mutalatāṉa pētaip paruvam aintu mutal ēḻāṇṭu varaippaṭṭatātalaiyum nōkkuka. itaṉāl, iḷamaiyum iṟceṟippum aṟimaṭamum paṟṟiyatē peṇṭir maṭam eṉavum. atu ettuṇaiyum iḻivu kuṟitta taṉṟeṉavum, āṭavarum aṟivillākkāl pētaip peyarkkuriyar eṉavum. āṉmāvai āṇavamalam piṇittiruppatupōlap peṇ pālaiyē pētaimaikkuṇam iyalpāyp paṟṟik koṇṭiruppataṉ ṟeṉavum aṟintu koḷka.
ōti yuṇarntum piṟarkkuraittun tāṉaṭaṅkāp
pētaiyiṟ pētaiyā ril  (kuṟaḷ. 834)
eṉa irupāṟ potuvāyum.
aṟaṉkaṭai niṉṟāru ḷellām piṟaṉkaṭai
niṉṟāriṟ pētaiyā ril  (kuṟaḷ. 142)
eṉa āṭavaraiyē cuṭṭiyum tiruvaḷḷuvar pētaiyeṉṉum peyarai vaḻaṅkiyamai kāṇka. iṉi peṇṭirkku meṉmait taṉmai ciṟakka, āṭavarkku iṉpañ ciṟappatām. ammeṉmait taṉmaikku aṇikalamāyiruppatu pētaimaiyām. pētaimai yeṉpatu mātarkkaṇikalam. peṇṭirkku iyalpākavuḷḷa meṉmait taṉmaiyāl irakkam ciṟantu. tuṉpaṅ kaṇṭa viṭattup piṟarporuṭṭu accan tōṉṟum ēṉai nāṇamum payirppum ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷeṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭā.
iṉi, āṭavarkku vaṉmaiyum peṇṭirkku meṉmaiyum ciṟappiyalātalāṉum, meṉmaiyilum vaṉmaiyē ciṟantatā talāṉum, āṭavarē ciṟantavar eṉiṉ. av vaṉmai meṉmai yākiya iraṇṭum oṉṟaiyoṉṟu iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyāṉum, payaṉpāṭṭaḷavil iraṇṭum ottiruttalāṉum meṉmaiyait taḻuvit tāṅkuvatē vaṉmaiyiṉ payaṉātalāṉum, meṉmaiyē iṉpan taruvatā yiruttalāṉum. iru pālarum orupālarē yeṉak kūṟi viṭukka.
 iṅṅaṉam irupālarum orupāla rallareṉiṉ.
 oṉṟē vēṟē yeṉṟiru pālvayiṉ
 oṉṟi uyarnta pāla tāṇaiyiṉ
 otta kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyum kāṇpa.  (tol. kaḷavu. 2)
 atuvē, tāṉē yavaḷē tamiyar kāṇak
 kāmap puṇarcci yiruvayi ṉottal.  (iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ . 2)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷ poruḷaṟṟaṉa veṉka.
mēṟkūṟiya accam mutaliya nāṟkuṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ maṇāḷaṉ allatu kaṇavaṉ mītuḷḷa aṉpuñ cērntatu kātal. kātal vēṟu. kaṟpu vēṟu. kātalil kaṟpuṇṭu; kaṟpil kātaliruppatu yāppuṟaviṉṟu. vāḻnāḷ muḻutum maṇam virumpātavaḷukkum tuṟaviyāyiruppavaḷukkum kaṟpirukkalām; āṉāl kātalirātu. acca mutaliya nāṟkuṇaṅkaḷuḷ. nāṇam payirppu eṉṉum iraṇṭum kaṟpā yamaiyum; āṉāl kātalāyamaivatu oṉṟu millai. ākavē, acca mutaliya nāṉkum (oḻukkamuḷḷa) peṇ pāṟ potukkuṇamum, kātaloṉṟum (aṉpuḷḷa) maṉaivi yiṉ ciṟappuk kuṇamumākum.
kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyākiya iruvarkkum kātal oru paṭittāy iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. atu illaṟa vāḻkkaikkuriya naṟkuṇaṅka ḷellān tiraṇṭu muḻuniṟaivāṉatu. atu iru kaikōḷukkum potu. cilarkkuk kaḷavilēyē toṭaṅkalām; cilarkkuk kaṟpil maṭṭum toṭaṅkalām. kaṟpellām kātalaṟṟateṉṟu koḷvatu tavaṟu. kaḷavil veḷippaṭa vāyppillāta kātal, kaṟpiltāṉ veḷippaṭum. attakaik kātal, karaṇamum vāyilum peṟṟatēṉum, kākkai uṭkārap paṉampaḻam viḻuntatu pōṉṟatē. kaḷavilum cūḷuṟavākiya karaṇamum, pāṅkaṉum pāṅkiyumākiya vāyilkaḷumuṇṭē! kaḷaviṟku allatu kātalukku meyyuṟu puṇarcci iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭuva tiṉṟu; uḷḷap puṇarcciyē pōtum.
 uḷḷap puṇarcciyu meyyuṟu puṇarcciyuṅ
 kaḷḷap puṇarcciyum kātalark kuriya.  (nampiyakapporuḷ. 34)
uḷḷap puṇarcciyaiyum meyyuṟupuṇarcciyō ṭoppak koṇṭamaiyāṉēyē, tilakavatiyār pōṉṟa paṇṭaik kaṟpuṭai mātar, maṇappēccu nikaḻntu maṇam nikaḻātavaḻiyum kaṇava ṉiṟantaviṭattu uyir nīkkavum kaimmai nōṟkavum talaippaṭṭaṉar. uyarntōr uḷḷap puṇarcciyālum iṉpam nukarvar; tāḻntōrē meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṉāya iṉpamē vēṇṭuvar.
iṉi maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum maṇappēcciṟku muṉ oruvarai yoruvar oruvakaiyālum aṟiyātavarā yiruppiṉum, maṇap pēccuvaḻi aṟintavarēyāvar. avariṭaik kātal tōṉṟu māyiṉ irumpaik kāntamiḻuppatu pōlum, kaṭṭaiyil tīppaṟṟuvatupōlum oruvar maṉattai oruvar maṉam iḻuttup paṟṟikkoḷḷum. vēṭṭañceṉṟa talai makaṉum puṉaṅkātta talaimakaḷum kāṭṭil oruvarai yoruvar kaṇṭatu pōṉṟē. maṇappēcciṟkuriya maṇamakkaḷum vīṭṭil oruvaraiyoruvar kāṇkiṉṟaṉar; kaṇṇāṟ kāṇum vāyppiṉṟēl karuttā lēṉuṅ kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. kātalukkuk kāṇuvatuṅ kaṇṇuvatum oṉṟē. uḷḷattāl maṭṭum kalakkum naṭpiṟkē puṇarcciyum paḻakutalum vēṇṭātu uṇarcciyē pōtumeṉiṉ, uḷḷat tālum uṭalālum iraṇṭaṟak kalakkum kātalukku uṇarcci pōtumeṉac collavum vēṇṭumō?
kātal vāḻkkaikku allatu uṇmaiyāṉa maṇavāḻkkaikku, vāyilum karaṇamum iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭupa valla.
kaṇṇōṭu kaṇṇiṉai nōkkokkiṉ vāyccoṟkaḷ
eṉṉa payaṉu mila  (kuṟaḷ. 1100)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kōvaiyil mutalāvatu kūṟappaṭum iyaṟkaip puṇarcci, talaivaṉun talaiviyum koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟit tāmē tamiyarāyk kūṭip puṇarum teyvap puṇarcciyē. ataṉpiṉ attakaip puṇarccikku iṭamiṉmaiyāṉēyē, pāṅkaṉum pāṅkiyumākiya vāyilkaḷ vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vāyil kaḷālum iyalātavaḻi kaṟpu allatu karaṇam vēṇṭap paṭukiṉṟatu.
uṇmaiyāṉa kaṟpāvatu kaḷavu veḷippāṭē yaṉṟik karaṇa maṉṟu. mutaṟkālattil karaṇamillāmalē makkaḷ illaṟam naṭātti vantaṉar. makappēṟu kātalai veḷippaṭuttiviṭu mātaliṉ, vāḻnāḷ muḻutum kaḷavoḻukkam iyaluvataṉṟu. ākaiyāl, muṉpu kaḷavum piṉpu kaṟpumāka maṇavāḻkkai iru pakutippaṭṭatu.
paruva makaṉum makaḷum mutalāvatu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam, kāmap puṇarcci, kātaṟ puṇarcci eṉa iru vakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu kāmattāl maṭṭum nikaḻntu piṉpu tīrvatu; piṉṉatu kāmattōṭu kūṭiya aṉpāl nikaḻntu kālamellām nīṭuvatu. itu eṇṇātu (teyvattāl) nikaḻvatum eṇṇi nikaḻvatum eṉa iruvakaittu, iv viruvakaiyilum kātal karai puraṇṭōṭi ettuṇait taṭaikaḷaiyun takartteṟintu iṟutivarai iṉpañ ciṟappatē. mutaṟkālattil naṭanta kaḷavellām kātaṟ puṇarcciyākavēyiruntatu. piṉpu, iṭaikkālattil cila pala kaḷavukaḷ kāmap puṇarcciyā yoḻiyavē. muṉivarāl karaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatu.
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇa meṉpa  (tol. poruḷ. kaṟ. 4)
āyiṉum, karaṇattāl maṭṭum poyyum vaḻuvum tīrntuviṭātu. uṇmaiyāṉa kātal vāḻkkai karaṇamaṟṟatēṉum paḻikkap paṭātu. alaṅkāramum makkaṭ kūṭṭamum iṉṉicaiyum uṇṭāṭṭum ūrvalamum varicaiyaḷippum ākiya āravāramē karaṇattaic ciṟappippatu, iv vāravāram caṟṟum aṟṟatāyiṉ karaṇamum potumakkaḷāl ciṟitu paḻikkappaṭuvatē. karaṇattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa vāḻkkaiyilum poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟumāyiṉ atuvuṅ karaṇamaṟṟatē. karaṇavaḻippaṭṭa pala illaṟa vāḻkkai yil poyyum vaḻuvum iṉṟum kaṇkūṭākak kāṇkiṟōm. karaṇamaṟṟatāyiṉum uṇmaiyāṉa kātal vāḻkkai yāyiṉ iṉṟum paḻikkappaṭātu ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakkatē.
makkaḷ immaiyil aṭaiyakkūṭiya iṉpaṅkaḷuḷ talai ciṟantatu peṇṇāl varuvatu.
kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyum aimpulaṉum
oṇṭoṭi kaṇṇē yuḷa.  (kuṟaḷ. 1111)
atuvuṅ kātalōṭu, kūṭiyatāyiṉ ciṟṟiṉpamēṉum pēriṉ pattiṟ kiṇaiyākak kūṟattakkatām.
tāmvīḻvār meṉṟōṭ ṭuyili ṉiṉitukol
tāmaraik kaṇṇā ṉulaku  (kuṟaḷ. 1103)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum,
ellā vuyirkkum iṉpam eṉpatu
tāṉamarntu varuu mēvaṟ ṟākum  (tol. poruḷiyal. 28)
ik kātaliṉpa vāḻkkaiyil vaṟumaiyillai. nōyillai, tuṉpamillai, kavalaiyillai, oru kuṟaiyumillai. illateṉ illavaḷ māṇpāṉāl eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. ettāl vāḻalām ottāl vāḻalām. kātalaṉum kātaliyum tāṉeṉ-ṟum avaḷeṉṟum vēṟṟumaiyiṉṟi ōruyirum īruṭalumāka oṉṟi nukarum iṉpam iruvarkkum potu vēṉum, kātalaṉē mikutiyāka nukarkiṉṟāṉ.
itaṉālēyē,
vilaṅkalaik kālviṇṭu mēṉmē liṭaviṇṇu maṇṇu muṉnīrk
kalaṅkalaic ceṉṟa vaṉṟuṅ kalaṅkāy  (tirukkō. 24)
tillaiccivaṉṟāḷām poṟṟaṭamalar cūṭum  (tirukkō. 21)
āṟṟalakaṟṟap peṟṟu,
maṭukkō kaṭaliṉ viṭutimi laṉṟi maṟitiraimīṉ
paṭukkō paṇilam palakuḷik kōparaṉ ṟillaimuṉṟiṟ
koṭukkō vaḷaimaṟṟu nummaiyark kāyakuṟṟēval ceykō
toṭukkō paṇiyī raṇiyīr malarnuñ curikuḻaṟkē  (tirukkō. 63)
eṉa eḷivantum iḻivantum kuṟṟēval ceyyavum.
muḻaṅkā rarimuraṇ vāraṇa vēṭṭaicey moyyiruḷvāy
vaḻaṅkā vatariṉ vaḻaṅku  (tirukkō. 157)
maṭalēṟiyum ēṟutaḻuvalum pulippāl kaṟattalum kōḷariyaik kollutalum mutaliya maṟacceyal ceytum maṇakkavum, karaṇattiṉ piṉ putalvaṉaip peṟṟapiṉpum maṉaiviyaik kāluṅ kaiyum piṭittu vēṇṭavum (tirukkō. 390) tuṇivatu.
iṅṅaṉam kātali allatu kaṟpuṭai maṉaivi mutalāvatu taṉ kātalaṉukku iṇaiyaṟṟa, iṉpam payakkiṉṟāḷ.
iraṇṭāvatu, kaṇavaṉ kaḷavil kūṭṭan taṭaippaṭṭavaḻi maṭa lēṟiyum pitaṟṟiyum, niṟaiyaḻintu perumaiyiḻanta viṭattum, kātali niṟai yaḻiyātu aṭakka voṭukkamāyiruk kiṉṟāḷ.
kaṭalaṉṉa kāma muḻantum maṭalēṟāp
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka til.  (kuṟaḷ. 1137)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
ettiṇai maruṅkiṉum makaṭūu maṭalmēl
poṟpuṭai neṟimai iṉmai yāṉa  (akattiṇai iyal. 38)
ceṟivu niṟaivuñ cemmaiyuñ ceppu
maṟivu marumaiyum peṇpā lāṉa  (poruḷiyal. 15)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
ākavē, aṟivu, niṟai, ōrppu, kaṭaippiṭi eṉṉum nāṉkum āṇpāṟkēyuriyavaiyalla eṉpatu veḷiyām. aṟivuḷḷa viṭattil ēṉai mūṉṟum amaiyum. peṇpāṟkum aṟivuṇ ṭeṉpatu iruvakai vaḻakkilum kaṇṭatē. kātalaṉ taṉṉaik kaippaṟṟiyaviṭattuk kattiṉum, tāy vantapōtu avaṉukkut taṇṇīr vikkiyateṉṟu colvatu. aṟivō? aṟiviṉmaiyō? peṇṭiriṭaik kalvi paraviṉ avarum āṭavarpōl aṟivu peṟutal tiṇṇam.
mūṉṟāvatu, karaṇattiṉpiṉ kaṇavaṉ taṉṉaik kaiviṭṭup parattaiyiṟ pirintaviṭattum maṟumaṇañ ceytaviṭattum, kātal maṉaivi avaṉ māṭṭu kaṭukaḷavuṅ kātal kuṉṟātirukkiṉ ṟāḷ. avaṉ āṇai poyttaṉimittam avaṉukkut tīṅku nērātapaṭi teyvattaiyum iravum pakalum vēṇṭukiṉṟāḷ. taṉ peṟṟōraiyum viṭṭuviṭṭu, ettuṇaic ciṟantavaṉā yiṉum vēṟoruvaṉaiyum kaṉavilum karutātu. taṉṉaiyē teyvamāka iṟukap paṟṟik koṇṭirukkum melliyalai maṟantu avaḷ uyirōṭirukkumpōtē avaḷ kaṇṇetirē vēṟoruttiyai maṇakkum āṭavaṉ vaṉmaitāṉ eṉṉē!
nāṉkāvatu, talaivaṉ taṉakku ettuṇait tīṅku ceyyiṉum ataip poruṭpaṭuttātum taṉṉalaṅ karutātum aṉṉaiyum aṭiyāḷum amaicciyumpōlap palvakaiyilum avaṉukkup paṇiviṭai ceykiṉṟāḷ. kaṟpuṭai maṉaiyāḷtāṉ kaṇavaṉuk keṉṟē vāḻntu avaṉillātapōtu alaṅkarippatum, ciṟakka uṇṭuṭut tuṟaṅkuvatum oḻikiṉṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ taṉakkuc ceyyuṅ koṭumaiyait tāy kūṟiṉum kaṭintu avaṉ iyaṟpaṭa moḻi kiṉṟāḷ.
aintāvatu, irappōrk kīntum aṭiyārp pēṇiyum viruntōmpiyum piṟarkkut toṇṭu ceykiṉṟāḷ kaṟpuṭai yillāḷ.
āṟāvatu, pañca kālattum maḻai poḻivikkavum, tēvaiyāṉa viṭattu ākkavum aḻikkavum āṟṟaluṭaiyavaḷāyirukkiṉ ṟāḷ kaṟpuṭaippeṇ. iv vāṟṟal, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam maṇa vāḻkkai ākiya munnilaik kaṟpuṭaip peṇṭirkkum potu vām. illaṟattiliruntu koṇṭē teyvattaṉmai peṟavum, tuṟaviyaiyum vekuḷavum valla kaṟpuṭaiyāḷariṉ perumai kaṭṭuraikkun tiṟattatō!
ēḻāvatu kaṟpuṭai maṉaivi taṉ kaṇavaṉiṟantatum uyir nīkkiṟāḷ ; illāviṭiṉ kaimmai nōṟkiṟāḷ. ittakaik kaṟpuṭaip peṇṇai maṉaiviyākap peṟuvatē ulakil   oruvar peṟattakka pēṟukaḷil talaiciṟantatām.
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka yāvuḷa kaṟpeṉṉum
tiṇmai yuṇṭākap peṟiṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 154)
pukaḻpurin tillilōrk killai yikaḻvārmuṉ
ēṟupōṟ pīṭu naṭai  (kuṟaḷ. 59)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
eṉṉoṭu porutu meṉpa avarai
ārama ralaṟat tākkit tēro
ṭavarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṟ ciṟanta
pērama ruṇka ṇivaḷiṉum pirika!  (puṟam. 71)
 eṉpatu ollaiyūr tanta pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ vañciṉam.
kaṟpuṭaip peṇṇai yuṭaimaipaṟṟip palavaracar pulavarāṟ pārāṭṭap peṟṟaṉar.
ceyirtīr kaṟpiṟ cēyiḻai kaṇava  (puṟam. 31)
ōṭuṅkī rōtik koṭuṅkuḻai kaṇava 
āṉṟōḷ kaṇava
puraiyōḷ kaṇava  (patiṟṟuppattu. 14, 55, 70)
kaṟpuṭai maṉaiviyai iṅṅaṉam kaṇavaṉukku viḻuccelva mākap pārāṭṭiyatōṭu nillātu. avaḷai iṟantapiṉ teyva mākavum vaṇaṅkiṉar muṉṉōr.
uraicāl pattiṉik kuyarntō rēttalum  (cilap.)
eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, kumari mutal paṉimalaivarai taṉ āṇaiyaic celuttiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum piṟavaracarum kaṇṇa kikkuk kōyileṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. turōpatai yammaṉ occāṇṭammai mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷum kaṟput teyvaṅkaḷē. maṟacceyal purinta kaṟpuṭaip peṇṇait teyvamāka vaṇaṅ kum vaḻakkamē. kaṭavuḷiṉ āṟṟalaip peṇ pālāka uruvakik kavum pōrt teyvattaip peṇpālākak koḷḷavuṅ kāraṇamāyiruntirukkalām.
pōriliṟanta ciṟanta maṟavarum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉarēṉum, avarait tāḻntōraṉṟi uyarntō rētta itukāṟuṅ kaṇṭilam. ākaiyāl peṇpāl ennilaiyiṉum āṇ pālilun tāḻnta taṉ ṟeṉpatum kaṟpuṭai maṉaiviyē oruvaṉukku immaiyil talaiciṟanta pēṟeṉpatum maṟak koṇāta muṭipukaḷām.
paṭṭiṉattārum civappirakācarum pōṉṟa aṟiñar peṇṭiraip paḻittuk kūṟiyatellām, vilaimakaḷiriṉ tīyoḻukkamum vīṭupēṟṟiṟkut taṭaiyāṉa ulakak kavarcciyumpaṟṟiyē yaṉṟip peṇpāliṉ iḻivupaṟṟi yaṉṟu.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, āṇ peṇṇākiya irupālum ōraṉṉa nilaiyaveṉṟum irupālukkum iyaṟkaiyilēyē perumaiyillai yeṉṟum, peṇpālariṟpōl āṇ pālariṉum iḻintāruṇ ṭeṉṟum, kaṟpuṭai maṉaiviyē maṉaiviyeṉṟum, kātal vāḻkkaiyē vāḻkkaiyeṉṟum, illaṟaviṉpa vāḻkkaiyai naṭāttupavar kaṇavaṉ maṉaivi yākiya iruvarumē yātaliṉ avarkkup piṟar tuṇai iṉṟi yamaiyāta taṉṟeṉṟum, maṉapporuttamē maṇap porutta meṉṟum, uṇmaiyāṉa maṇam kūṭṭamēyaṉṟik karaṇamaṉ ṟeṉṟum, kātal kūṭiyapiṉ iṟappiṉālaṉṟip pirippillai yeṉṟum, kātal vāḻkkai maṇṇulakai viṇṇulakākkuva teṉṟum, ituvē muṉṉait tamiḻarkaṇṭa aṉpu neṟi akapporuḷiṉpa vāḻkkai yeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka!
kuṟippu: kaṟpuḷḷa maṉaiviyum kaṇavaṉiṟantapiṉ maṟumaṇañ ceyyalā meṉpatu ikkālak karuttām. maṉaivi yiṟantapiṉ kaṇavaṉ maṟumaṇañ ceyyum pōtu, maṉaivikku maṭṭum eṉṉō taṭai! nāvalantēyattil āṭavarāṟ peṇṭirkkuc ceyyappaṭuṅ koṭumai aḷavaṟṟatākum. peṇṭiriṉ aṭimaittaṉattaip pōkkiyapiṉpē āṭavar tam aṭimait taṉattaip pōkkaṟpālar. toṉṟutoṭṭu varum peṇṭiriṉ aṭimaittaṉamē peṇpālait tāḻvākak karutak kāraṇa mākum. āṉāl uṇmaiyil irupālarum oṉṟē. meṉmai yālākum iṉpattai yellām nukarntukoṇṭu atai vaṉmaiyāl oṭukkuvatu muṟaiyō? kaṇavaṉiṟantapiṉ kaṟpuṭai maṉaivi uyirait tuṟappatu aḷavukku miñciyatākak karutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. kapilarum, picirāntaiyum poyyāmoḻiyum pōṉṟa vaṉmaipeṟṟa āṭavarē naṭpurimai kāraṇamākat tam naṇpar iṟantavuṭaṉ uyirait tuṟappārāyiṉ, meṉmaiyiṟ ciṟanta kaṇṇakiyum pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ ṟēviyum pōṉṟa melliyalār tam kaṇavariṟantavuṭaṉ uyirait tuṟavātu vēṟēṉ ceyvar? āyiṉum, pulavarellām tam naṇpariṟantāl uyirviṭu vārallar; atupōṉṟu kaṟpuṭaiya maṉaivi yārellām taṉ kaṇavariṟanta piṉ uyirviṭuvā rallar. īriṭattum uyir viṭuva taṟkuk kāraṇam, uṇarcci mikutiyē. aḵtuḷvaḻi pōṟṟap paṭiṉum, ivvaḻi ikaḻappaṭātu.  (cakuntalā niṉaivu malar.)
kaṉṉaṭam
 kaṉṉaṭam eṉṉum peyar
malaiyāḷattiṟku aṭuttut tamiḻōṭu toṭarpuḷḷatu kaṉṉaṭam, kaṉṉaṭam eṉpatu karunāṭam eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripu. itu mutalāvatu kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭaik kuṟittup piṉpu aṅku vaḻaṅkum moḻiyaik kuṟittatu. itaṉ paḻaiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ karunāṭu, karunāṭakam eṉpaṉa. kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭār karunāṭar eṉṟum, karunaṭar eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭār.
karunaṭam allatu karunāṭakam eṉṉum collukku iru poruḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai (1) kariya nāṭu, (2) karuṅkūttu eṉpaṉa.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟ perumpakuti karical nilamāyiruppatāl, kariya nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭaṉar kuṇṭeṭ paṇṭitarum (Dr. Gundert) kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyārum.
kūttukaḷil iḻinta vakaikkuk karuṅkūttu eṉṟu peyar.
 mutupārppāṉ vīḻkkaip peruṅkaruṅ kūttu   (kalit. 65: 29)
 naṭam = kūttu. naṭaṉ = kūttaṉ
vaḷinaṭaṉ melliṇarp pūṅkoṭi mēvara nuṭaṅka (paripā. 22: 42)
 naṭar = kūttar.
viṭarum tūrttarum naṭarum uḷḷiṭṭa  (kuṟaḷ. parimē. urai)
nāṭakam = katai taḻuvivarum kūttu. mikap paḻaimaiyāṉa anākarika allatu kaṇmūṭip paḻakkattaip paḻaiya karu nāṭakam eṉpar; iṅkuk karunāṭakam eṉpatu paḻaimai yāṉa anākarikattaik kuṟikkalām. ākavē, karunaṭam allatu karunāṭakam eṉṉum peyar karuṅkūttu nikaḻum nāṭu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭatāyirukkalām.
cilappatikārattil karunāṭar kuṟikkappaṭum pōtellām tiruntāmaiyaik kuṟikkum koṭu eṉṉum aṭai koṭuttē kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅkaru nāṭar  (cilap. 25: 156)
koṅkaṇak kūttaruṅ koṭuṅkaru nāṭarum  (cilap. 26: 106)
iṉṟum yakṣakāṉam eṉṉum karuṅkūttu kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil naṭikkappaṭṭu varuvatākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āyiṉum karunaṭaraik karunāṭar eṉṉum vaḻakkum uṇmai yāṉum, kūttākiya kāraṇattiṉum nilavakaiyākiya kāraṇam peyarppēṟṟiṟkuc ciṟattalāṉum, karicaṟpāṅkāṉa nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvatē poruttamām. karai nāṭu eṉpatu karunāṭu eṉa maruviṟṟeṉpar cilar.
 kaṉṉaṭanāṭ ṭellai
teṟkē nīlamalai (nīlakiri) yiliruntu vaṭakkē pīṭārp (Bidar) pakuti varai. maicūr kaṉṉaṭam teṉmarāṭṭam (makārāṣṭiram) aitarāpāttiṉ teṉmēṟkup pakuti ākiya cīmaikaḷiṟ perum pālum tāymoḻiyākap pēcappaṭuvatu kaṉṉaṭam.
nīlamalaiyiluḷḷa paṭakar (vaṭakar), eṉṉum malaivāṇar kaṉṉaṭattiṉ tiripāṉa oru moḻiyaip pēcukiṉṟaṉar.
16ām nūṟṟāṇṭil teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vanta makamatiya maṉṉar, aṟṟait tamiḻnāṭṭiṉmēṟ karunaṭa maṉṉar āṇai celutti yamai kāraṇamākat tamiḻnāṭṭaiyum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭōṭu cērttuk karnāṭṭak (karunāṭakam) eṉṟaṉar. atai āṅki lēyar karnāṭṭik (Carnatic) eṉṟu tirittaṉar.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭu varalāṟu
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟ ciṟanta pakuti maicūrc cīmaiyākum. maicūril taṟpōtu haḷepīṭu (Helebid) eṉa vaḻaṅkum tuvarai nakā (tuvāra camuttiram) ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarc centamiḻ nāṭṭaic cērntatāyiruntatu. itu tuvarāpati eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, akattiyaṉār..... tuvarāpatip pōntu nilaṅ kaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇal vaḻikkaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmaraiyum patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷiruḷḷiṭṭāraiyum aruvāḷaraiyuṅ koṇṭu pōntu kāṭukeṭuttu nāṭākki eṉat tol kāppiyap pāyiravuraiyilum, malaiya mātavaṉ nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇaluḻai narapatiyaruṭaṉ koṇarnta patiṉeṇ vakaik kuṭippiṟanta vēḷirkkum eṉa akattiṇai yiyal 32 ām nūṟpāvuraiyilum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
paṇṭaic cēranāṭṭil maicūrc cīmaiyiṉ teṉ pakutiyum cērntiruntatu.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil maicūrt tuvarainakarai āṇṭavaṉ iruṅkōvēḷ eṉṉum tamiḻac ciṟṟaracaṉ. avaṉ vaṭapakkat til oru muṉivariṉ ōma kuṇṭattil tōṉṟit tuvarai nakarai nāṟpattoṉpatu talaimuṟaiyākat toṉṟutoṭṭu āṇṭu vanta vēḷirkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ eṉṟum, oru muṉivar tavañceytu koṇṭirukkaiyil avarkku iṭaiyūṟu ceyyavanta oru puliyai avar ēvaṟpaṭi koṉṟamaiyāl pulikaṭi māl eṉap paṭṭāṉeṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvaṉ.
piṟkālattil 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tuvāracamuttirattil (Halebid) niṟuvappaṭṭa hoycaḷa pallāḷa marapu kaṭaikkaḻakak kālap pulikaṭi māliṉ vaḻiyatē. pallāḷaṉ eṉṉum peyar vallāḷaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripu. va-pa, pōli, ō. nō: vaṇṭi - paṇṭi, vaku - paku, vallāḷaṉ = valiya āṇmaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉ. oru maṟavaṉuṭaiya illaiyum ūraiyum iyalpaiyum colli avaṉ āṇmait taṉmaiyai mikuttuk kūṟum puṟattuṟaikku vallāṇmullai (pu.ve. 177) eṉṟu peyar.
naḷḷātār miṭalcāytta
vallāḷaniṉ makiḻirukkaiyē  (puṟam. 125)
eṉat tērvaṇ malaiyaṉum,
vicaitteṟi kūṭamoṭu porūum
ulaikkal laṉṉa vallā ḷaṉṉē  (puṟam. 170)
eṉap piṭṭaṅkoṟṟaṉum,
aṭi poliyak kaḻaṟaiiya
vallāḷaṉai vayavēntē  (puṟam. 40)
eṉac cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉum pāṭappaṭṭaṉar.
kaṭuntiṟamaiyuḷḷa irupālāraiyum vallāḷa kaṇṭaṉ vallāḷa kaṇṭi eṉap pukaḻvatu iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭulakavaḻakku.
tiruvaṇṇāmalaiyil vallāḷa makārācaṉ eṉṉum ōr aracaṉ iṭaikkālattil āṇṭatāka aruṇācalapurāṇam kūṟum.
mēṟkut toṭarcci malaikku mēṟkum arapik kaṭalukkuk kiḻakkum kūrccarattiṟkut teṟkum kōvāvukku vaṭakkumāka vuḷḷa koṅkaṇa tēcam paṇṭaik kālattil koṭuntamiḻa nāṭukaḷuṉ oṉṟāyiruntatāka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ kūṟum.
kaṉṉit teṉkaraik kaṭpaḻan tīvam
ciṅkaḷam kollam kūviḷam eṉṉum
ellaiyiṉ puṟattavum īḻam pallavam
kaṉṉaṭam vaṭuku kaliṅkam teliṅkam
koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟam
eṉpaṉa kuṭapāl irupuṟac caiyat
tuṭaṉuṟaipu paḻakun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum
muṭiyuṭai mūvarum iṭunila vāṭci
aracu mēmpaṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭikaḷ
patiṉmarum uṭaṉirup piruvarum paṭaitta
paṉṉiru ticaiyil coṉṉaya muṭaiyavum
eṉṟār akattiyaṉār eṉpatu naṉṉūl mayilai nātar urai. (pa. 161.) iv akattiya nūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭum peyarkaḷ mutalāvatu nāṭṭaik kuṟittavai yeṉṟum piṉpu moḻi tirintapiṉ moḻiyaik kuṟittaṉaveṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
cēranāṭu kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattilēyē kuṭa (mēṟkut toṭarcci) malaikku mēṟpāl vēṟum kīḻppāl vēṟumākap pirintu pōyiṟṟu. kīḻppāl nāṭu mīṇṭum teṟkil koṅku nāṭum vaṭakkil kaṅkanāṭum iṭaiyil atikaināṭu tuvaraināṭu mutali yaṉavumākap pirintuviṭṭatu. atikaināṭu takaṭūrai (iṟṟait tarmapuriyai)t talainakarākak koṇṭu atikamāṉ marapiṉar āṇṭu vantatu, kaṅkanāṭu, ataṉ vaṭakkil kaṅkamarapiṉar kuvaḷāla purattai (kōlār)t talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭu vantatu. itu kaṅkapāṭi eṉṟu kalveṭṭukaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
vēṅkai nāṭuṅ kaṅka pāṭiyam  (S.S.I. I. 94)
kaṅka marapiṉarāṉa ciṟṟaracar kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattilē, maṟattiṟ ciṟantu peyar peṟṟavarāyiruntaṉar.
naṉṉa ṉēṟṟai ṟaṟumpū ṇatti
tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭuntiṟaṟ kaṅkaṉ kaṭṭi  (akam. 44)
paṅkaḷar kaṅkar palvēṟ kaṭṭiyar  (cilap. 25: 157)
eṉap paḻaiya nūlkaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka.
ik kaṅka marapaic cērntavaṉē, 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ kālattaṉum, amarāparaṇaṉ srīmat kuvaḷālapura paramēcuvaraṉ, kaṅkakulōṟpavaṉ eṉṟu taṉ meykkīrttikaḷiṟ pārāṭṭappeṟupavaṉum, pavaṇanti muṉi varaik koṇṭu naṉṉūlai ākkuvittavaṉumākiya cīyakaṅkaṉ eṉpavaṉ. ivaṉ oru tamiḻilakkaṇattai iyaṟṟu vittati ṉālum, naṉṉūṟ ciṟappuppāyiram,
kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭam
eṉunāṉ kellaiyiṉ iruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ
eṉṟu kūṟuvatālum, maicūr nāṭṭiṉ vēṅkaṭa nēr ellai varai 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyumāvatu tamiḻ tavira vēṟoru moḻiyum vaḻaṅkavillai yeṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum.
ākavē, pampāy mākāṇamum aitarāpāttuc cīmaiyum 
ceṉṉai mākāṇamum kūṭukiṉṟa iṭattuk koṭuntamiḻ vaḻakku, vaṭacoṟ kalappāl 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ kaṉṉaṭameṉa vēṟu moḻiyākap pirintu, piṉpu ciṟitu ciṟi tākat teṟkē taḷḷivantu taṟpōtu nīlamalai varai paraviyuḷḷa teṉka.
koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅkaru nāṭar
koṅkaṇak kūttaruṅ koṭuṅkaru nāṭarum
eṉṟu cilappatikāraṅ kūṟuvatāl koṅkaṇa nāṭṭaic cērnta karunaṭa nāṭṭilēyē kaṉṉaṭam mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ippōtu koṅkaṇa nāṭṭiṟkum malapārukkum iṭaippaṭṭa mēlkarai nāṭē, teṉ kaṉṉaṭam vaṭakaṉṉaṭam eṉa iru pakutiyāyp pakukkappaṭṭuk kaṉṉaṭam (kannada) eṉ ṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. vaṭakaṉṉaṭam koṅkaṇa nāṭṭup pakutiyē, itaṉāl, karaināṭu eṉpatē karunāṭu eṉa maruviṟṟu eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
koṅkaṇam eṉpatu koṇkaṉam eṉṉum colliṉ tiripu. koṇkaṉ eṉpatu neytalnilat talaivaṉ peyar. koṅ kaṇam oru neytal nāṭātalāl, koṇku (neytalnilam) eṉṉum peyar muṟaiyē koṇkaṉam - koṅkaṇam eṉṟu tirintirukkalām. koṅkaṇa nāṭṭiṉar, koṅkaṇar eṉṟum koṅkaṇavar eṉṟum koṅkaṇiyar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvar. koṅkaṇar eṉṟu oru paṇṭait tamiḻc cittar iruntār.
maicūrc cīmaiyiluḷḷa palavūrkaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻc coṟ kaḷaiyē tam peyarīṟākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
(e-ṭu): ūr (maicūr), puram (kuvaḷālapuram), paḷḷi allatu haḷḷi (cikṉavakaṉ haḷḷi), paṭṭiṉam (cīraṅkapaṭṭiṉam), kara (aricikkarai), pēṭṭai mutaliyaṉa. kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷa kauṭar (kavuṇṭar), tikaḻar (tamiḻar), kuṟumpar, uppārar, korakar (kuṟavar), holiyar (pulaiyar), pēṭar (vēṭar) mutaliya kulattiṉar tamiḻ marapiṉarē. kaṉṉaṭiyar eṉṉum peyarum moḻipaṟṟik kulaṅkuṟitta tamiḻccollē, maicūr aracariṉ iṟpeyarāṉa uṭaiyār eṉpatu taṉit tamiḻc collāyiruppatu mika mika makiḻattakkatu.
koṅkaṇa muṉivar vācukiyammaiyārāl kaṭintu koḷḷap paṭṭatiṉālō, koṅkaṇa nāṭṭāriṉ iyalpāṉa pētamai yālō, koṅkaṇaṉ koṅkaṇavaṉ koṅkaṇiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ pētaimai kuṟitta peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.

ticaic coṟkaḷ
muttappa allatu muttātta = pūṭṭaṉ (pāṭṭaṉ tantai), muttamma allatu muttajji = pūṭṭi (pāṭṭitāy), ajja = pāṭṭaṉ, ajji = pāṭṭi, toṭṭappa = periyappaṉ. toṭṭamma allatu toṭṭavva = periyammai, cikkappa = ciṟṟappaṉ, cikkamma allatu cikkavva = ciṟiya tāy.
kuṟippu: mutu + appa = muttappa, accaṉ - ajjaṉ, acci - ajji, toṭṭa (tōṭu) = periya, cikka = ciṟiya.
4. makkaṭ peyar
āḷu, makkaḷu, aracu, tōṭṭakāraṉu, kumpāra (kumpākāraṉ = kuyavaṉ), olekāra (kaṭitam koṇṭu pōkiṟavaṉ), māttāḷi (pēccāḷi, māṟṟam = col), māṟāḷi (viṟkiṟavaṉ. māṟu = vil), camartta, manti (māntar), kuṇṭṭa (muṭavaṉ), kuruṭa, mūṭa, ūme, mutuka (kiḻavaṉ), cōmāri (cōmpēṟi), cikkavaṉu (ciṟiyavaṉ), caṇṇavaṉu (ciṉṉavaṉ), hoceyaṉu (putiyaṉ), eḷekavaṉu (iḷaiyavaṉ), kariyavaṉu, kariyaṉu, nereyavaṉu (neruṅkiyavaṉ), piḷiyavaṉu (veḷiyavaṉ), haḷepaṉu (paḻai yaṉ), kaḷḷaṉu, oṭeya tūta, kammāra, kāvalukāra, ara makaḷ, nāṇili, ammaṇṇi, āṇṭi oṭṭa (oṭṭaṉ), kaṉṉe (kaṉṉi), kuturekāra, korava (kuṟavaṉ), cempu kuṭṭika (cempu koṭṭi), talāri (talaiyāri), tiṇṭip pōtta (tiṉṟippōttu - ākupeyar).
5. vilaṅkup peyar
āvu, āṉe (āṉai), ettu (erutu), oṇṭṭe (oṭṭai), karu (kaṉṟu), kuture, āṭu, kuri (koṟi = āṭu), nari, nāyi, ciratte (ciṟuttai) mari, (maṟi=kuṭṭi), huliyu (puli), kaḻte (kaḻutai), kaṭacu (kiṭāri), takaru (takar), mēkke (mōttai = veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṭā), kūḷi, hōttu (pōttu), molavu (muyal), kuṇi molavu (kuḻi muyal), hanti (paṉṟi), pekku (veruku), ey, emme (erumai), kaṭave (kaṭamai), karaṭi, kantu (kaṉṟu), kuṭṭi, kuraṅki.
6. paṟavaip peyar
īccal (īcal), anti (antu), kiḷi, kiṇi (kiḷi), kōḷi (kōḻi), kāki (kākkai), pairi (vairi), kūke (kūkai), kaṭal kāki, nīru kōḷi, navil (mayil), partu (paruntu), pāval, (vāval), ānteka (āntai), kukil (kuyil), kuḷavi, kokkarē (kokku).
7. ūrvaṉavaṟṟiṉ peyar
aṇil, uṭu (uṭumpu), iṟumpu (eṟumpu), hāvu (pāmpu), huḷu (puḻu), hēṉu (pēṉ), eli, halli (palli), araṇe, hacuruoti (paccōnti), kettal (cital), ōti (ōnti), uṭutte (uṟuttai = aṇil), kīra (kīri), cuṇṭili, tēḷ.
8. nīrvāḻvaṉavaṟṟiṉ peyar

 25. toḻiṟpeyar
(1) 	mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kaḷ, hake, tappu.
(2) 	mutaṉilai tirinta toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kēṭu, īṭu.
(3) 	mutaṉilai tirintu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: tāha (tākam).
(4)	mutaṉilai vali yiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: ūṭṭe (ūṟṟu, ākupeyar).
(5) 	mutaṉilai tirintu vali yiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kuti - kūttu
(6) 	mutaṉilai vali yiraṭṭittu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar
 	e-ṭu: āṭṭa (āṭṭam).
(7) 	vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: vikuti peyar poruḷ
 	ke (kai) aṭike (aṭukai = camaittal)
 	te (tai) naṭatte (naṭattai)
 	ū (uḷ) peḷeyu (viḷaiyuḷ)
 	alu (al) pikkalu (vikkal)
 	vu (u) aṭavu (aṭaivu), cāvu.
 	pu atirppu
 	aṇa (aṇam) kaṭṭaṇa (kaṭṭaṇam)
 	pi (vi) kalpi (kalvi)
(8) 	mutaṉilai valittu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: paḻakke (vaḻakkam), aṭakka (aṭakkam).
(9) 	pakuti vaḻakkaṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar
 	e-ṭu: kaṭṭaḷai.

kaṭṭiṭac coṟkaḷ
ā, akō, atō, āka = aṉṟu, hāke (appaṭi), antu (aṉṟu), antta (aṉaiya), anttu (appaṭi).
viṉāvaṭic coṟkaḷ
ē, ēke (ēṉ), ēṉ entta (eṉaiya). enttu (eppaṭi), ō.

ayyō (aiyō), kuyyō, morrō (muṟaiyō), cī, ammamma, jalajala (calacala), cummaṉe, curīreṉa, cuṟucuṟeṉa, taḷataḷa.

viḷipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ.
 ōyi, ō, elē.
pālkāṭṭum vikutikaḷ
 ā.pā: aṉ > aṉe, aṉu, a. kāraṉ > kāra > āra.
 pe.pā: aḷ > aḷe, aḷu.
 i, iti, itti > vitti > kitti. aṉi > iṉi
 palarpāl: ar > are, aru. kaḷ > kaḷu.
 o.pā: atu > tu, itu, utu.
 pa.pā: kaḷ > kaḷu, avai > ave. avu.

coṟṟoṭarkaḷ
atu yāru? kaḷḷaru ōṭi hōtaru (pōṉār), kōlu muritu hōyīttu (muṟintu pōyiṟṟu), maṉeke hōkuva hot tāyittu = maṉaikkup pōkiṟa poḻutāyiṟṟu.
ī ūriṉalli naṉake ontu maṉeyu itē = ivvūril eṉakku oru maṉai irukkiṟatu. huṭu karu tōṭṭatalvi tirukāṭuttā marakaḷaṉṉu hattūttā haṇṇu tiṉṉuttā opparaṉopparu kūkuttā hekaraṉṉu hiṭitu karaiyuttā nakuttā āṭuttā ittaru = paiyaṉkaḷ tōṭṭattil tirintu koṇṭum maraṅ kaḷil ēṟikkoṇṭum paḻaṅkaḷ tiṉṟu koṇṭum oruvarai yoruvar kūvi (kūppiṭṭu)k koṇṭum peyariṭṭuk karaintu (aḻaittuk) koṇṭum nakaittuk koṇṭum viḷaiyāṭik koṇṭum iruntaṉar. huṭukaru = poṭiyar allatu piṭukar.
nīvākali nāṉākali hōkuvatilla = nīṅkaḷākal nāṉākal pōkuvatillai. ākal = āvatu. nīvu ā kateyaṉṉu ōtiti pus tavaṉṉu naṉake tōṟiciri = nīṅkaḷ ak kataiyai ōtiṉa (paṭitta) puttakattai eṉakkuk kāṭṭuṅkaḷ. tōṟu > kitāṉṟu. tōṟiciri (pi.vi.)
naṉṉa ūraṉṉu cēritakūṭale nimake ontu kātakavaṉṉu pareyuveṉu = eṉ ūr pōyc cērntavuṭaṉ umakku oru kākitam varaivēṉ.
makkaḷellaru pāṭacāleyaṉṉu piṭṭu, tamma tamma maṉa kaḷike hoṟaṭṭu hōta paḷikka javāṉaṉu pākilukaḷaṉṉu mucci piṭuvaṉṉu = makka (piḷḷaika), ḷellārum pāṭacālaiyai viṭṭut tamtam malaikaḷukkup puṟappaṭṭup pōṉa piṟaku cēvakaṉ vācalkaḷai mūṭiviṭuvāṉ.
nāḷevāra ūriṉalli iruvirō emputāki avaraṉṉu kēḷu = nāḷai (aṭutta) vāram ūril iruppīrō eṉpatāka avaraik kēḷ.
maḻeyu pāratittare peḷe yākuvavutillai = maḻai varātirun tāl viḷai (viḷaiyuḷ), ākuvatillai.
vitaikkiṟavaṉ uvamai
ikō, pittuvavaṉu pitta horaṭṭaṉu, avaṉu pittuvāka kelavu mārkata hattara pittu. paṣikaḷu pantu ataṉṉutintu piṭṭavu. pēre kelavu pahaḷa vaṇṇu illāta paṇṭestaḷa kaḷalli pittu; atakke āḷavāta maṇṇu illata kāraṇa, caṅkaṭalē moḷiyittu; ātare cūryaṉu mūṭitāka atu pentu atakke pēru illātatarinta oṇaki hōyittu. pēre kelavu muḷḷukaḷa mēlē pittu, muḷḷukaḷu peḷetu, aṇakicitavu, pēre kelavu oḷḷē pūmiya, mēlē pittu, ontu nū, raṣṭu, ontu aravattaṣṭu, ontu mūvattaṣṭu pala koṭṭattu, kēḷuvataṟke kivikaḷuḷḷavaṉu kēḷali.
itō, vittukiṟavaṉ vittap puṟappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ vittukaiyil cila vaḻiyarukē viḻuntu, paṟavaikaḷ vantu atait tiṉṟuviṭṭaṉa. vēṟē cila mikunta maṇ illātapāṟai yiṭaṅkaḷil viḻuntatu. ataṟku āḻamāṉa maṇ illāta kāraṇamāy viraintu muḷait tatu; āṉāl veyil ēṟiṉapōtu atu ventu, ataṟku vēril lātatāl uṇaṅkip pōyiṟṟu, vēṟe cila muṭkaḷ mēlē viḻuntatu. muṭkaḷ vaḷarntu atai nerukki viṭṭaṉa. vēṟē cila nalla nilattil viḻuntu oṉṟu nūṟākavum oṉṟu aṟupatāka vum oṉṟu muppatākavum palaṉ koṭuttatu. kēṭkiṟataṟkuc cevikaḷuḷḷavaṉ kēṭkakkaṭavaṉ.
kuṟippu: meymaṟai (cattiya vētam) eṉṉum kiṟittava maṟaiyiṉ moḻivaṭai viṭaittoṇṭarātalāl, mēṟkūṟiya uvamai yil aḵṟiṇaip paṉmaiyeḻuvāykaḷ orumai viṉaiyoṭu muṭintaṉa; paṉmaic coṟku orumaic col vantatu.  (ti.tā.)
kaṉṉam
 kaṉṉam - karṇa
kal - kaṉ = tuḷai. kaṉ - kaṉṉam = 1. tuḷaiyuḷḷa kātu,                           2. yāṉaic cevi (tivā.). 3. kātaiyaṭutta alakup pakkam.                ma. kaṉṉam, ka. kaṉṉa.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum krut, krū eṉṉum mūlaṅkaḷ poruntā.
krut = 1. ceytukoṇṭu, 2. citaivu, 3. tiruku, cuṟṟu.
kru = 1. koṭṭu, eṟi, citaṟu. 2. citai, 3. aṟi, aṟivi. (va.va: 112)

potuvākak kaṉattiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākac colvatu kallē. kallaippōṟ kaṉakkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. paṇṭai nāḷiṟ kaṉatta eṭaikkellām kallaiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyatāl, paṭikkal eṉṉum vaḻakkeḻuntatu. iṅkilāntilum avvaḻakka miruntatai stone (14 pavuṇṭu) eṉṉum col uṇarttum.
vaṭamoḻiyil kaṉattal eṉṉum viṉaiyillai. mā.vi. akara mutali kāṭṭiyirukkum haṉ (kol) eṉṉum mūlam, vaṭa moḻiyiṟ kaṉa eṉṉum coṟkuḷḷa palvēṟu poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkut tāṉ ēṟkum. (va.va. 121-113)
kaṉi vakaikaḷ:
 kāy teṅku pūcaṇi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṉi;
 kaṉi muntiri nelli mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṉi; (kaṉivāyiruppatu);
 paḻam mā vāḻai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṉi (paḻuppu niṟamāṉatu);

kātal 
kātal eṉpatu, oruvaraiyoruvar iṉṟiyamaiyāk kaḻiperu nēyamāy iruvariṭai nikaḻvatu. atu, kōpperuñ cōḻaṉum picirāntaiyārum pōlum naṇpariṭattum, peṟṟōrum piḷḷaiyum pōlum uṟaviṉariṭattum, pūta pāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ tēviyum pōlum kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviya riṭattum amaivatu, atu ariya piṟavik kuṇam. (ta.ti.muṉ VII)
kātal poruttam
maṇamakkaṭku mutaṉmaiyāka vēṇṭum poruttam ituvē. iḵtillāviṭiṉ, ēṉaic ciṟappellāmiruntum ētum payaṉillai. ātalāl, maṇamakkaḷ icaivu peṟṟē avar peṟṟōr avarkku maṇañ ceytu vaittal vēṇṭum. (ta.ti. 43)

L. candela, a wax or tallow candle, AS. candel OE. candel, AN, OF, candel (I)e E. candle (f. candere, to glow), cylinder of wax, tallow spermaceti, cte., enclosing wick, for giving light.
L. candidus, shining white, white, candidum, n. white colour, a. honest, straight forward. E. candid, white, fair, sincere, honest, E. candescent, glowing with white heat, f. L. candeseere, f. candere, be white.
L. candidatus, clothed in white; a candidate for office, who among the Romans was always clothed in white, for the consulship. E. candidate, one who offers himself or is put forward to be elected to an office, orig. white-robed.
L. Candor, whiteness, sincerity, E. candour, openmindedness, frankness, impartiality.
E. chandler, dealer in candles, M.E., AF. chandeler, OF. chandelier f. L. candela.

kāyā paḻamā viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : nīntat teriyum ciṟuvar nīril niṉṟu kāyā paḻamā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu āṭum āṭṭu, ak kēḷviyaiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭuvār tokai : iruvar mutaṟ palar itai āṭuvar. iruvarkku mēṟpaṭiṉ iṉpañ ciṟakkum.
āṭiṭam : āṟuṅ kuḷamum pōlum nīrnilaikaḷ itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟai : āṭuvārellām nīril niṉṟu koṇṭu, ov voruvaṉāyk kāyā paḻamā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu nīruṭ cuṇṭuvar. cuṇṭum viral nīrmēlpaṭiṉ taḷār eṉṉum ocai yeḻum; nīruḷ muṟṟum muḻukiṉ ōcaiyē kēḷātu : pāti muḻukiṉ ṭapakku eṉṉum ōcai piṟakkum. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉavai yiraṇṭum kāyām; piṉṉatoṉṟum paḻamām, kāyāyiṉ kāy eṉṟum paḻamāyiṉ paḻam eṉṟum, piṟar kūṟuvar. palar kāyā yiṉ mīṇṭum cuṇṭuvar. oruvaṉē kāyāyiṉ, avaṉ piṟarai nīruṭ piṭittal vēṇṭum. avar ciṟitu tolaivu ceṉṟa piṉ, tammaip piṭikkac colvār. avaṉ viraintu ceṉṟu, naṭantu celpavarai naṭantu ceṉṟum, nīntic celpavarai nīntic ceṉ ṟum, muḻukic celpavarai muḻukic ceṉṟum, piṭikka muyalvāṉ. yārēṉum toṭappaṭiṉ, avaṉē piṉpu piṟaraip piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu viruppamuḷḷavarai piṭittu āṭappeṟum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : uḷḷāṉ eṉṉum nīrppaṟavai nīruḷ mūḻkic ceṉṟu mīṉ piṭippatiṉiṉṟu, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : nīruḷ mūḻkiya poruḷai yeṭuttaṟkum muttuk kuḷittaṟkum ēṟṟa payiṟciyai, ivviḷaiyāṭṭu aḷikkum.
