 	(niṉatu, niṉa) 	(nimatu, nima) 	(niṅkaḷatu, niṅkaḷa)
7.	niṉṉiṭam 	nimmiṭam 	niṅkaḷiṭam
nīṉ eṉṉum orumaip peyar, piṉṉē nī eṉa īṟukeṭṭu, ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peruvaḻakkākavum ilakkiya vaḻakkil muḻu vaḻakkāka vum vaḻaṅkattalaippaṭṭatu. nīṉ eṉṉum peyar vaḻakkai, iṉ ṟum pāṇṭi nāṭṭu nāṭārkula vaḻakkiṟ kāṇalām. kaṉṉaṭat til appeyar ukaravīṟu peṟṟu nīṉu eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
nīm eṉṉum paṉmaip peyarum, iruvakai vaḻakkilum arukiya vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl nī eṉṉum īṟukeṭṭapeyar ir eṉṉum paṭarkkaip palarpālīṟu peṟṟu nīyir eṉṟāki nīm eṉpataṟkut talaimāṟāka (patilāka) vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
nīm eṉṉum peyar vaḻakkai, iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭṭuc ciṟṟūrp pakutikaḷiṟ kāṇalām.
nīyir eṉpatu. nāḷaṭaivil nīvir eṉat tirintu piṉṉar nīr eṉat tokkatu.
nī, nīr (nīyir, nīvir) eṉṉum iruvaṭivukaṭkum taṉ vēṟṟumai yillai. ataṉāl, muṉṉataṟku ūṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ vēṟ ṟumaiyum, piṉṉataṟku nūm allatu ūm eṉṉum peyariṉ vēṟṟumaiyum, vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. nīr eṉṉum vaṭiviṟkuc coṉṉatē, nīyir, nīvir eṉṉum vaṭivukaṭkum okkum.
teṟkil ēṟattāḻa īrāyiraṅkal tolaivu parantirunta kumari nāṭu muḻukippōṉamaiyāl, atil vaḻaṅkiya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. mutaliru kaḻakattālum iyaṟṟappaṭṭum pōṟṟappaṭṭum vanta āyirakkaṇakkāṉa taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭamaiyāl, avaṟṟil vaḻaṅkiya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ātalāl, mēṟkāṭṭiya taṉmai muṉṉilaip peyarkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṟkum, avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumai vaṭivukaṭkum, iṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭillaiyeṉa aṟika.
eṭṭiṟkumēl aṭutta eṇṇiṉ peyar akkālattil toṇṭu eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
 toṇṭu 	= 	9
 toṇpatu 	= 	90
 toṇṇuṟu 	= 	900
 toḷḷāyiram 	= 	9,000
 toṇpatiṉāyiram 	= 	90,000
 toḷḷilakkam 	= 	900,000
 toṇpatilakkam 	= 	9,000,000
 toṇkōṭi 	= 	90,000,000
oṉṟu mutal pattuvarai eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ taṉic collāka vum ukara vīṟu koṇṭumē iruntaṉa.
palukkaṟ cemmai :
kaṇ, māṉ, ūr, kal, kūḻ, āḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum eṇ, īṉ, pār, col, umiḻ, kēḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉai kaḷum, ukara vīṟṟait tuṇaikkoḷḷāmalum, tāy, vāy eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, cey, cāy eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉaikaḷum, ikara vīṟṟait tuṇaikkoḷḷāmalum olittaṉa.
 maram, avaṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, vantāṉ, pōṉāṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉaikaḷum, iṟuti melliṉamey mūkkoli yaḷavilaṉṟi muṟṟum cevvaiyāka olittu vaḻaṅkiṉa.
 avaḷ, makaḷ eṉpaṉapōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, vantāḷ, pōṉāḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉaikaḷum, iṟuti ḷakaramey olittē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
ilakkaṇa naṭai
irukkiṟatu, iruntatu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paṭarkkai yoṉṟaṉ pāl viṉaikaḷellām, koccaivaṭivilaṉṟi ilakkaṇa vaṭivilēyē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
ellā viṉaimuṟṟukkaḷum eḻuvāyk kēṟṟa īṟu peṟṟē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
e-ṭu :	nāṉ (yāṉ)	allēṉ
	yām, nām 	allēm, allōm
	nī 	allai
	nīm 	allīm
	nīr (nīyir, nīvir) 	allīr
	nīṅkaḷ 	allīṅkaḷ
avaṉallaṉ, avaḷallaḷ, avarallar, avarkaḷallarkaḷ, atuvaṉṟu, avaiyalla.
aṉmai yiṉmaip poruṭ coṟkaḷ teḷivāka vēṟupaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu : 	avaṉ vīṭṭil illai.
 	avaṉ pulavaṉ allaṉ.
irutiṇaip pakuppu
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭilum pakuttaṟivāṟṟalum talaiciṟantiruntataṉāl, pakuttaṟi vaṭippaṭaiyilēyē uyar vakuppu tāḻ vakuppu eṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum iruvakup pāka vakuttu. pakuttaṟivuḷḷa uyirmeykaḷaik kuṟikkuñ coṟ kaṭkē pālīṟu koṭuttum, aḵtillāta uyir meykaḷaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭku eṇṇīṟē koṭuttum, coṟṟoṭar amaittaṉar.
 e-ṭu :	uḻavaṉ uḻukiṉṟāṉ
	uḻatti kaḷaiyeṭukkiṉṟāṉ 	uyar vakuppu
	avaṉ vantāṉ
 	avaḷ pōṉāḷ 
 	kāḷai uḻukiṉṟatu
 	āvu mēykiṉṟatu
	āṇpaṉai vaḷarkiṉṟatu 	tāḻ vakuppu
	peṇpaṉai kāykkiṉṟatu.
	atu vantatu.
	avai pōyiṉa. 
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇam iyampal eṉṉum muṟai yil, ilakkaṇa nūlār pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaḷai uyartiṇai yeṉṟum, avarallāta uyiruḷḷaṉavum illaṉavu māṉa ellā vaṟṟaiyum aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟum, kuṟittaṉar.
makkaḷaip paṭaittavarum ellām aṟintavaruvamāṉa, iṟaivaṉ uyartiṇaiyeṉpataic colla vēṇṭuvatillai.
ivvirutiṇaip pakuppu tamiḻukkē ciṟappām. teluṅkil ittakaiya toṉṟiruppiṉum, atu tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiyatē.
colvaḷam
kumarināṭṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa piṉpum, tamiḻ oppuyarvaṟṟa colvaḷam mikka moḻiyāka uḷḷatu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, iṉṟum, collutalaik kuṟikka aṟai iyampu, icai, urai, eṉ, ōtu, kiḷa, kiḷattu, kūṟu, cāṟṟu, ceppu, col, navil, nutal, nuval, noṭi, pakar, paṟai, paṉṉu, paṉuvu, pukal, pēcu, māṟu, miḻaṟṟu, moḻi, viḷ, viḷattu, viḷampu mutaliya coṟkaḷum.
ōṭutalaik kuṟikka, ōṭu, kiṇṇu, toṅku, pari mutaliya coṟkaḷum,
yāṉaiyaik kuṟikka, āmpal, umpal, uvā, eṟumpi, ōṅkal, kaṭamā, kari, kaṟaiyaṭi, kuñcaram, kaimmalai, kaimmā, tumpi, tūṅkal, tōl, nālvāy, pūṭkai, poṅkaṭi, maṟamali, mātiram, moy, vaḻuvai, vāraṇam, vēḻam mutaliya potuc coṟkaḷum; cinturam piṇimukam, pukarmukam mutaliya ciṟappuc coṟkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
aṟai mutal viḷampu īṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa coṟka ḷellām, collutal eṉṉum potupporuḷil ottaṉa vēṉum, collutal vakaiyāṉa ciṟappup poruḷil vēṟu paṭṭaṉavām. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉaiyavum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tirāviṭa moḻikaṭ kellān tāyāta lāl, muṉṉataṉ oruporuṭ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē, piṉṉavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum ovvoru collait terinteṭuttāḷu kiṉṟatu. itu tāy vīṭṭiluḷḷa oru viṉaikkup payaṉpaṭum palvakaik kalaṅkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟai ovvoru makaḷum eṭuttup paḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatu.
 e-ṭu:	ceppu - ceppu (teluṅku)
		kiḷa - hēḷu (kaṉṉaṭam)
		paṟai - paṟa (malaiyāḷam)
ceppu eṉṉum viṉaic col, tamiḻil viṭai collutal eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷilum, teluṅkiṟ collutal eṉṉum potup poruḷilum, vaḻaṅkum. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉaic coṟkaḷum taṉtaṉ ciṟappup poruḷai iḻakkum.
paṟaital eṉṉum col, ētampaṟain talla ceytu (tiv. tiruvāy. 4; 6; 8) eṉṟu ilakkiya vaḻakkilum, collāmaṟ paṟaiyāmal ōṭip pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
vīṭṭaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷuḷ, il eṉṉum teluṅkuc collum maṉai yeṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac collum maṭṭumaṉṟi, camaṟkirutattilum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkum kuṭi yeṉṉum collum tamiḻiluḷḷateṉṟu, tamiḻiṉ oru poruṭ colvaḷattaik kālṭuvelār viyantiruttalai nōkkuka.
camaṟkirum pirākiruta moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum tamiḻiṉiṉṟum, āṅkilam ulaka moḻikaḷuṭ palavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum, ērāḷamākak kaṭaṉ koṇṭiruppatāl, avaṟṟiluḷḷa oru poruṭ col vaḷattai viyattaṟkillai.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷ iṟṟait tamiḻil, illaiyeṉiṉ, avai kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻil vaḻaṅki iṟantu paṭṭaṉa eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. colliṟkuc coṉṉatē col vaṭiviṟkum.
colvaṭivukaḷuḷ muṉṉataip piṉṉatiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭutti yaṟitaṟku, moḻiyārāycciyoṭu kūṭiya collārāycci vēṇṭum.
nāl - nālku - nāṉku
te. nāluku (g) ka. nālku (k)
 vāluḷaip puravi..... nālkuṭaṉ pūṭṭi  (perumpāṇ. 489)
 nālku eṉpatu tamiḻvaṭivē.
tūymai
mutaliru kaḻakak kālattilum āriyar teṉṉāṭu varāmaiyāl, akkaḻakaṅkaḷ irunta kumarināṭṭuttamiḻ, oliyiṟ pōṉṟē collilum poruḷilum ilakkiyattilum karuttilum muḻut tūymai peṟṟiruntatu. ilakkiyattuḷ ilakkaṇamum aṭaṅkum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar (tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000 - 10,000)
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar, perumpālum aintiṇaippaṭṭa nāṭṭup puṟattārum, ciṟupāṉmai tiṇaimayakkuṟṟa nakaravāṇarumāka, iruvakai vāḻcciyarāy iruntu vantaṉar.
kuṟiñcinilai māntar, kolliyum paṟampum pōṉṟa malai kaḷaiyaṭuttuk kūṭṭaṅkūṭṭamākak kuṭicaikaḷil taṅki, vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum kiḻaṅkakaḻntum tēṉeṭuttum tiṉai viḷaittum, muraṭṭuk kampaḷiyuṭuttum murukaṉai vaṇaṅkiyum, vāḻntu vantaṉar. uṇavup poruḷkaḷaic cuṭuvataṟkum eḷiya muṟaiyiṟ camaippataṟkum, iraviṟ koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷai ōṭṭuvataṟkum, tīkkaṭai kōlāl tīyuṇṭākkik koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu kuṟicciyeṉṟum ciṟukuṭiyeṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kuṟiñcinila makaḷir kuṟicolvatu kuṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉāṟ kuṟavar eṉṟum, vēṭṭaiyāṭuvatāl vēṭṭuvar eṉṟum, kuṟiñci nila māntarkku irupeyarkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
mullai nila māntar, pulveḷiyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷaiyaṇṭi, āṭumāṭerumaiyākiya munniraikaḷaiyum vaḷarttu pāl tayir mōr veṇṇey ney ākiya āṉaintai maruta nilattum pālai nilattum viṟṟum, varaku, cāmai avarai tuvarai mutaliya vāṉavārip payirkaḷai viḷaittum, pañcāṭaiyum meṉ kampaḷiyum uṭuttum, pulvaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta maḻai poḻiyum mukil vaṇṇaṅkoṇṭa māyōṉ (kariyōṉ) eṉṉum teyvattai vaṇaṅkiyum, vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu, pāṭiyeṉṟum cēriyeṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
āyar, iṭaiyar, potuvar, toḻuvar, toṟuvar, kōvar, kōvalar eṉpaṉa mullai nilattār peyarkaḷ.
kō (āṉ) mēyppatil vallavar kōvalaṉ āṉ vallōr eṉṟu tivākarattilum, āṉvallavar eṉṟu cūṭā maṇiyilum, kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
kaṇṇaki kaṇavaṉākiya kōvalaṉ peyarē kōpāla eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripākum. atilum kō eṉpatu teṉcollē.
kāṭṭumāṭukaḷaip piṭippataṟkum vīṭṭumāṭukaḷai aṭakkutaṟ kum maṟavali vēṇṭiyiruntataṉāl, āyar tam makaḷiraik kol lēṟu piṭittaṭakkiya iḷaiñarkkē maṇañceytu koṭut taṉar. kollēṟaṭakkal ēṟutaḻuval eṉappaṭṭatu. āṭṭiṭai yar (pulliṉattāyar) eṉṟum māṭṭiṭaiyar (nalliṉattāyar) eṉṟum, āyar iruvakaiyarāyp pirintaṉar.
marutanila makkaḷ nilavaḷamum nīrvaḷamum mikka iṭaṅkaḷil taṅki, nel karumpu vāḻai mutaliya naṉceyp payirkaḷaiyum cōḷam kēḻvaraku mutaliya puṉceyp payirkaḷaiyum viḷaittu, pañcāṭaiyum paṭṭāṭaiyum kampaḷiyāṭaiyum aṇintu vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) eṉṉum vāṉavar kōṉai vaṇaṅki, maṇ ṇāluṅ kallāluñ ceṅkalālum kaṭṭiya maccu vīṭukaḷilum ōṭṭu mukaṭṭu vīṭukaḷilum kūrai vīṭukaḷilum vatintu. nilai yāka vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu maṭṭum ūr eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟatu.
ur-uṟu. uṟutal = poruntutal, nilattōṭu poruntutal, nilaiyākak kuṭiyiruttal. ur - uṟu. ur-ūr o.nō; uṇ ūṇ.
payirttoḻilil mutalviṉai uḻavātalāl, attoḻilum uḻaveṉappaṭṭatu. ataic ceytavar uḻavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. pōraṭikkuṅ kaḷattil vēlai ceyvatu paṟṟi kaḷamar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉar.
perunilakkiḻārellārum piṟaraik koṇṭē viṉai ceytu, vīṭṭiliruntu niḻalil vāḻntataṉāl, caṟṟu veḷuttup pōy veḷḷāḷareṉṟum veṇkaḷamareṉṟum; ciṟu nilakkiḻārum nilamilikaḷum kaṭuveyililuṅ kāṭṭilum vēlai ceytu karuttup pōṉatiṉāl, kārāḷareṉṟum karuṅkaḷamareṉṟum; collappaṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, uḻutuṇpār, uḻuvittuṇpār eṉa uḻavar iru vakuppārāyiṉar.
marutanila makkaḷ nilaiyāka vāḻntataṉāl, avarkaḷ kuṭiyirup pukkaḷ nāḷaṭaiviṟ pērūrum mūturumāyiṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila nakarum nakaramum āyiṉa. nakaram mānakar.
uḻavarukkup pakkattuṇaiyāka, neyavar, kuyavar, taccar, kol lar, paṟampar (tōlviṉaiñar) mayirviṉaiñar, vaṇṇār, cekkār mutaliya patiṉeṇ toḻilāḷarum, piṟa nuṇviṉaiña rum; paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉar. nakarattil nākarikan tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu.
kaṭal nīrāl uppu viḷaittaṟkum, āṟu kuḷaṅkaḷ nīr vaṟṟiyapiṉ kaṭal mīṉ piṭittaṟkum, muttuk kuḷittaṟkum, cila kūṭṭattār neytal nilattil vatintaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu, kuppam, karai, tuṟai, kāyal, kāṉal eṉap palvēṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa.
neytal nilattār, iṭattiṟkum karuvikkum toḻilukkum ēṟpa, karaiyār eṉṟum, paṭavar - paravar eṉṟum, mukkuvar eṉṟum, aḷavar eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar. avar vaṇaṅkiyatu vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum kaṭal teyvam.
mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyum kaṭuṅkōṭaik kālattiṟ cilaviṭaṅ kaḷil vaṟṟivaṟaṇṭu viḷaiccaliṉṟip pañcanilai ēṟpaṭṭa pōtu, pālaiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. attakaiya camaiyaṅkaḷil, an nila vāṇar vaḻippaṟikkavum akkam pakkaṅkaḷiṟ koḷḷai yaṭikkavum nērntatu. mūvēntarum avarait tam paṭai maṟavarāk kiyum, tām pakaitta aracariṉ nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu āniraikaḷaik kavarntu vara ēviyum, vantaṉar.
pālainilavāṇar, kaḷḷar, maṟavar, eyiṉar, vēṭṭuvar eṉap palavakuppiṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu, kuṭikkāṭu, nattam, paṟantalai, cēri eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ teyvam kāḷi.
vaṇikam
marutanilap pērūrkaḷil, mutaṟkaṇ ovvoru toḻilāḷarum tattam viḷaiporuṭkaḷaiyum ceyporuṭkaḷaiyum nelliṟkum piṟavaṟṟikum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytu vantaṉar.
piṉṉar ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum vāṅkiviṟkum vaṇikar tōṉṟiṉar. mutalil uḷḷūrp poruḷ viṟpaṉaiyum, piṉṉar veḷiyūrp poruḷ viṟpaṉaiyum, ataṉpiṉ veḷināṭṭup poruḷ viṟpaṉaiyumāka, vaṇikam vaḷarntu vantatu. nila vāṇikattiṉ piṉ nīrvāṇikam tōṉṟiṟṟu. nilavāṇikar cāttuvar eṉṟum, nīrvāṇikar nāvikar (nāykar) eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar.
makkaḷ tokai perukiyapiṉ kaḷavuṅ kavarvum mikuntataṉāl poruṭkāppiṟkum vaḻakkut tīrppiṟkum āṭcikkum kāvalaṉ ēṟpaṭṭāṉ. kuṭumpattalaivaṉ nilaimaiyil toṭaṅkiya āṭcip patavi, ūrttalaivaṉ, vēḷ (kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ), kō (perunila aracaṉ), vēntaṉ, māvēntaṉ (pēraracaṉ) eṉappaṭip paṭiyāka vuyarntatu. vēntaṉ ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ avaṉukkaṭaṅkiya ciṟṟaracarkku makuṭam aṇiyum urimai illātu pōyiṟṟu. iyalpākavē paṇṭamāṟṟiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa tiṇaimayakkam, aintiṇai nilaṅkaḷaiyum cērttāṇṭa vēntaṉ kālattil, mika virivaṭaintatu. vēyntāṉ - vēyntaṉ - vēntaṉ = muṭiyaṇin tavaṉ vēytal = talaimēlaṇital. iṅṅaṉam kuṟunila aracar, perunila aracar eṉa āḷvār iruvakaiyarāyiṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉ cōḻaṉ cēraṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil mukkuṭi vēntar tōṉṟi, āṭcikku maruta nilattil oṉṟum, nīr vāṇikattiṟku neytal nilattil oṉṟumāka, iru mānakaramaittu āṇṭaṉar. maruta nilaittalai nakarellām pērāṟṟaṅkarai mēlēyē amain tiruntaṉa.
nākarikam vaḷarntu pātukāppum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, uḻuvit tuṇ ṇum uḻavarāṉa veḷḷāḷar tam oḻivu nēraṅkaḷiṟ kalviyai vaḷarttu vantaṉar. immaikkuriya ulakiyaṟ kalvikkuppiṉ, maṟumaikkuriya mataviyaṟ kalvi tōṉṟiṟṟu. ulaka vāḻkkaiyil veṟuppuṟṟavar tuṟavai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. kaṟṟōruḷ illaṟattār nūlkaḷaip pārppataṉāṟ pārppār allatu pārppaṉar eṉṟum, tuṟavaṟattār ellāvuyirkaḷiṭattum aruḷ pūṇṭamaiyāl antaṇar eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, kaṟṟōr pārppārum antaṇarum eṉa irutiṟattārāyiṉar.
cilar pārppaṉar eṉṉum col pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripeṉṟum, tuṟavu nilai pirāmaṇaṉukkē yuriya teṉṟum, pitaṟṟip pētaiyarai ēmāṟṟuvar. kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻar kālam āriyar eṉṉum iṉak karuvē tōṉṟāta kālam. pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟa pūṭṭaṉ maṇavāta iḷamaik kālattil koṭpēraṉ eṅṅaṉam tōṉṟavillaiyō, aṅṅaṉamē kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻaṉ kālattilum āriyaṉ tōṉṟavillai. ataṉāl avaṉ tirimoḻiyum tōṉṟavillai.
kaṇṇuṭaiyar eṉpavar kaṟṟōr mukattiraṇṭu
puṇṇuṭaiyar kallā tavar
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiṉiṉṟu (33), pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyark karaṇiyattaiyum,
antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrman ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmai pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ.
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiṉiṉṟu (30), antaṇaṉ eṉṉum peyark karaṇiyattaiyuṅ kaṇṭu koḷka.
cilar, pārppaṉaṉ eṉṉum colvaṭivu pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpataip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉpar. āṉ aṉaṉ eṉa iruvakaiyīṟukaḷaip peṟuvatu tamiḻviṉai muṟṟukkaḷiṉ iyalpē.
 e-ṭu:
 i. kā. 	ni. kā. 	e. kā.
 pārttaṉaṉ 	pārkkiṉṟaṉaṉ 	pārppaṉaṉ
 pārttaṉaḷ 	pārkkiṉṟaṉaḷ 	pārpapaṉaḷ
 pārttaṉar 	pārkkiṉṟaṉar 	pārppaṉar
 pārttaṉṟu 	pārkkiṉṟataṉṟu 	
 pārttaṉa 	pārkkiṉṟaṉa 	pārppaṉa
 aṉatu - aṉṟu (aṉ + tu). pārttaṉṟu = pārttatu.
marutanilattil, uḻavar, vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār, eṉṉum nālvakuppārum tōṉṟiyapiṉ. vēḷāṇmai ceytu viruntōmpuvatil veḷḷāḷarum kārāḷarumāṉa iruvakaiyuḻa varum talaiciṟantataṉāl, vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
tiṇaimayakkam
maruta nilattuk kōnakarkaḷum talainakarkaḷum nakaraṅkaḷāka (mānakarkaḷāka) virivaṭaintapōtu, mullai nilamum cila viṭattuk kuṟiñci nilamum paṭippaṭiyāka aṭuttu vantaṉa. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam pōṉṟa neytal nakaraṅkaḷil, marutamum mullaiyuṅ kalantaṉa. cēranāṭṭu vañci pōṉṟa nakaraṅkaḷil aintiṇaiyuṅ kalantaṉa.
neytal nakaraṅkaḷellām paṭṭiṉam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ pirivukaḷ pākkam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
maruta nilattūrkaṭku, mutaṟkaṇ, neytal nila māntar uppum mīṉum caṅkum pavaḷamum pōṉṟa kaṭalpaṭu poruḷkaḷaiyum, mullai nilamāntar āṉaintaiyum, kuṟiñci nilamāntar tēṉum iṟaicciyum maruppum (tantamum) kācaṟaiyum (kastūriyum) pōṉṟa malaipaṭu poruḷkaḷaiyum, koṇarntu nelliṟkum āṭaikkum māṟṟiṉar. piṉṉar, piḻaippiṟkākac cilar nakar toṟum vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. pālai nila maṟavarum kāval toḻiṟkamarntaṉar.
tirunāḷ, perunāḷ, porunāḷ ākiya munnāṭkaḷilum, aittiṇai makkaḷum kalantaṉar. aṅṅaṉaṅ kalantu uraiyāṭum pōtu, maruta vāṇaṉai nōkki uṉ ūr etuveṉṟum, mullai vāṇaṉai nōkki uṉ pāṭi (allatu cēri) etu veṉ ṟum, kuṟiñci vāṇaṉai nōkki uṉ kuṟicci etuveṉṟum, neytal vāṇaṉai nōkki uṉ kuppam (allatu tuṟai) etu veṉṟum, pālai vāṇaṉai nōkki uṉ kuṭikkāṭu etuveṉ ṟum, viṉavuvatē iyalpum vaḻakkamum. pērūrkaḷilum mūtūrkaḷi lum pala toḻilāḷarum nilattārum kalantu. tiṇaimayakkam mikutiyāka ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, aintiṇaik kuṭiyiruppum ūr eṉṉum potuppeyar peṟṟaṉa.
kulavakuppu
mutaṟkaṇ, nilantoṟum oru vakuppāka aintiṇaik kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
piṉṉar maruta nilattil, uḻavarkkup pakkat tuṇaiyākap patiṉeṇ kaittoḻiṟ kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
ataṉpiṉ, vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār eṉṉum kulaṅkaḷ allatu kuṭikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. aracar eṉṟatu, vēntarum vēḷirum pōla vaḻivaḻiyāka vanta aracar kuṭumpaṅkaḷaiyē.
kalvic ciṟappum oḻukka vuyarvum paṟṟip pārppārum cila viṭattu antaṇar eṉap peṟṟaṉar.
ovvoru kulamum nāḷaṭaivil iraṇṭum palavumākak kiḷaittup palkiṟṟu.
ellāk kulaṅkaḷum toḻilaṭippaṭaiyilēyē tōṉṟi yiyaṅkiṉa. oruvaṉ ettoḻilaiyum mēṟkoḷḷalām. ceyyun toḻil paṟṟiyē oruvaṉ kulam amaintatu. āyiṉ, tantai yaṟivum āṟṟalum maṉappāṉmaiyum iyalpākavē makaṉukku amaintuviṭṭataṉālum, ovvoruvaṉum piḷḷaip paruvatti lēyē peṟṟōṉaip piṉpaṟṟi oru toḻiliṟ payiṟci peṟṟu viṭṭataṉālum, talaimuṟai toṟum maraput toḻiṟṟiṟamai vaḷarcciyaṭaintu vantataṉālum, makkaḷ tokai perukap peruka ellāt toḻilāḷarkkum piḻaippu ēṟpaṭṭataṉā lum, perumpālum peṟṟōr toḻilaiyē makkaḷ vaḻivaḻi ceytu varuvārāyiṉar.
ūrkkiḻavaṉ (ūraṉ), ūrāḷi, ūrkkuṭumpaṉ, ūrkkā miṇṭaṉ (kavuṇṭaṉ), nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ, nāṭaṉ, nāṭāṉ, nāṭṭāṉ, periyataṉakkāraṉ, ampalakāraṉ, cērvaikāraṉ, mūppaṉ, maṉṟāṭi, paṇṇaiyāṭi, mutali, talaivaṉ, tēvaṉ eṉa iṭattiṟkum kulattiṟkum ēṟpap palapeyar peṟṟirunta ūrttalaivaṉ patavi pōṉṟē, aracar patavikaḷum tol varavākat toṭarntu vantaṉa.
iyalpāṉa uṟavaṉpum poruḷutaviyum pātukāppum nōkki, potumakkaḷ perumpālum uṟaviṉarkkuḷḷēyē koḷ vaṉaiyuṅ koṭuppaṉaiyuñ ceytu vantaṉar. aracarkkō evvakai vilakkum taṭaiyum iruntatillai. vēḷir makaḷirai yum maṟakkuṭi makaḷiraiyum ayal nāṭṭaracar makaḷiraiyum avar maṇantu vantaṉar.
ekkulattārāyiṉum, tuppuravum oḻukkamumē uṇṭāṭṭuṟa viṟkuk kavaṉikkappaṭṭaṉa. kalvi, oḻukkam, tavam, atikāram, celvam, īkai, maṟam, āṟṟal eṉpavaṟṟā laṉṟip piṟappāṟ ciṟappillai.
atti, matti, ātaṉ, pūtaṉ, utiyaṉ, titiyaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, paṇṇaṉ, kāṭaṉ, kōṭaṉ, cāttaṉ, kūttaṉ, nākaṉ, pēkaṉ, naṉṉaṉ, poṉṉaṉ, nāṇaṉ, vāṇaṉ, vēntaṉ, cēntaṉ, naḷḷi, kiḷḷi, pāri, kāri, piṭṭaṉ, vaṭṭaṉ, tattaṉ, tittaṉ, mūlaṉ, vēlaṉ, iṟaiyaṉ, poṟaiyaṉ, mūvaṉ, tēvaṉ, koṟṟaṉ, veṟṟaṉ, marutaṉ, virutaṉ, cēraṉ, kīraṉ, vaṅkaṉ, koṅkaṉ, veḷḷaiyaṉ, piḷḷaiyaṉ, karuppaṉ, poruppaṉ, paccai, ceccai, malaiyaṉ, kalaiyaṉ, māṟaṉ, vīṟaṉ, nallaṉ, cellaṉ, nampi, tampi, appaṉ, kuppaṉ, vēṭṭaṉ, cēṭṭaṉ mutaliyaṉa vākat taṉittum puṇarntum aṭaiyaṭuttum varum peyarkaḷ ettoḻilāruṭaiyaṉavāyiṉum, ikkālattu vaḻaṅkum kulappaṭṭamiṉṟi, ēṉai nāṭṭār peyarkaḷ pōṉṟē akkālattu vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
vaṇṇār, aṭuttōr (mayirviṉaiñar) mutaliya ūrkkuṭi makkaḷ, paṟampar (tōlviṉaiñar) pōṉṟē, orē vakuppārāy ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntaṉar.
mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ pōṉṟē, uṇavuvakaiyiṉāl ēṟṟatāḻvillā tiruntatu.
ellāt tirukkōvilkaḷilum tamiḻ oṉṟē vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyāyiruntatu. uvaccar, kurukkaḷ, tirukkaḷ, pulavar, paṇṭāriyār, nampimār, pōṟṟimār, cāttuvār mutaliya tamiḻp pūcāriyarē, civaṉ tirumāl kāḷi eṉṉum mutteyvak kōvilkaḷilum vaḻipāṭu naṭatti vantaṉar. kōviṟ pukavurimai yum vaḻipāṭṭurimaiyum ellā vakuppārkkuñ camamāyiruntatu. kōvilai yaṭuttirunta tirukkuḷattil kuḷitta piṉ allatu kaikāl kaḻuviya piṉ, ellārum kāṇikkai yoṭu kōviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar.
tiruviḻākkaḷilum puṉalāṭṭu viḻākkaḷilum ellā vakup pārum kalantē koṇṭāṭiṉar. āṟukuḷaṅkaḷil vakuppiṟ kōriṭamāka varaiyaṟukkappaṭa villai.
iṭukāṭu ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntatu. piṇattaic cuṭuvatu tamiḻar vaḻakkamaṉṟu.
vēḷāḷar, vaṇikar, aracar, antaṇar eṉpatē iyaṟkai muṟai yāyiṉum, poruḷilakkaṇa nūlār, ākkavaḻippāṟṟal paṟṟi yum mukkāla aṟivupaṟṟiyum muḻuttūymai paṟṟiyum antaṇarai muṉṉum, iṟaivaṉ pōl ellāraiyum muṟaiceytu kāttal paṟṟi aracarai iraṇṭāvatum, nāṭṭai vaḷampaṭuttiyum pōrkkālattil aracarkkup poruḷ koṭuttum utavum vaṇikarai mūṉṟāvatum, vaittu, antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉat talaikīḻākac ceyaṟkai muṟaippaṭuttiṉar.
kumari nāṭṭu nākarikam
 nākarikam eṉpatu tiruntiya paḻakka vaḻakkam. atu mutaṉ mutal nakarattil allatu nakariyil tōṉṟiṉataṉāl nākarikam eṉappaṭṭatu.
o.nō : L. civis = citizen, civil = polite, civil - civilize - civilization = advanced stage in social development.
 L. urbis = city, urbane = courteous, urbanity = polished manners,
akanākarikam puṟanākarikam eṉa nākarikam iruvakaippaṭum. aka nākarikamāvatu tiruntiya oḻukkam; puṟanākarikamāvatu tiruntiya ulaka vāḻkkai. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu paṇpāṭu eṉṟum, piṉ ṉatu aṭaiyiṉṟip potuvāka nākarikam eṉṟum, collap paṭum.
	nākarikam
uṟaiyuḷ 
vēntarum kōkkaḷum māperuñcelvarum vatiyum nakaraṅ kaḷum, vēḷirum maṇṭilat talaivarum vatiyum nakarkaḷum, kallālum ceṅkallālum cuṇṇāmpuk kāraiyiṭṭukkaṭṭic cippi nīṟṟuc cutaiyāl tīṟṟiya, māṭa māḷikaikaḷum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum koṇṭu, māṭciyāṉa kāṭciyaḷittaṉa.
cippic cutaiyiṉ veṇmaic ciṟappai,
oḷiyārmuṉ oḷḷiya rātal veḷiyārmuṉ
vāṉcutai vaṇṇaṅ koḷal.
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷālum (714),
putuppiṟai yaṉṉa cutaicey māṭattu
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟu aṭiyālum, (378),
varuṅkuṉṟa moṉṟurit tōṉtillai yampala vaṉmalayat
tiruṅkuṉṟa vāṇa riḷaṅkoṭi yēyiṭa reytalemmūrp
paruṅkuṉṟa māḷikai nuṇkaḷa pattoḷi pāyanummūrk
karuṅkuṉṟam veṇṇiṟak kañcukam ēykkum kaṉaṅkuḻaiyē.
eṉṉun tirukkōvaic ceyyuḷālum (15), aṟiyalām.
māṭaṅkaḷilellām kaṇkavar vaṇṇa vōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunilai maṉaitoṟum
maiyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
evvakai yuyirkaḷum uvamaṅ kāṭṭi
veṇcutai viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya
kaṇkavar ōviyam.
 eṉṟu maṇimēkalaiyum (3: 127-131)
māṭakkuc cittiramum eṉṟu naṉṉūṟpāyiramum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
nakarantoṟum vāṉaḷāvum eḻunilaik kōpuramum kaṭṭap paṭṭiruntatu. mutaṟkaṇ aracarvatiyum araṇmaṉaik kāvaṟ kōpuramāka irunta kaṭṭiṭam, piṟkālattil vāṉaḷāvun tirukkōviṟ kōpuramāka vaḷarntatu.
kō = aracaṉ, puram = uyarnta kaṭṭiṭam.
puraiyuyar vākum.  (tol, uri. 4)
kōnakarun talainakarum kōṭṭai matilāluñ cūḻappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. kōṭṭaiyuḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ puri eṉṟum, kōpura muḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ puram eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
purital = vaḷaital. puri = vaḷainta (cūḻnta) kōṭṭaiyuḷḷatu. puri - puricai = kōṭṭai matil.
matil, eyil, iñci, cō eṉa matilaraṇ nālvakaippaṭ ṭiruntatu. mika uyaramāṉatu matil; muṟṟukaiyiṭum pakaivar mītu ampeyyum ēvaṟaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu eyil; cempai yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiyiṟukiyatu iñci; ariya kaṭum poṟikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu cō.
kōnakarkaḷil aṅkaṇa nīraik kaṇṇiṟ paṭāmaṟ pōkku vataṟku, karantupaṭaiyeṉṉum putaicālakam iruntatu. atu maṟuku (perunteru) naṭuviṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu yāṉaikkūṭṭam mēṟ cellumpaṭi, karuṅkallāl mūṭappaṭṭi runtatu. atiṟ ceṉṟanīr yāṉait tutikkai pōṉṟa tūmpiṉ vāyilāy akaḻiyil viḻuntatu.
aracar kuṭumpattuṭaṉ pōrkkālattil tappik koḷvataṟku, araṇmaṉai yiṉ kīḻum kōnakarkaḷiṭaiyum, curaṅkap pātaikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
āṭai
pañcu, paṭṭu, mayir ākiya mūvakaik karuvipporuḷālum iḻaikkappaṭṭa nuṇṇūliṉāl, pūvitaḻ pōṉṟa meṉmai-yum, iḻai pirittaṟiyāc ceṟivum, pāmpuccaṭṭaiyum mūṅkiṟ coliyum nīrāviyum naṟumpukaiyum otta naṉitava noymmai-yum, kaṇkavar palvaṇṇap pūttoḻilum, uṭaiya palvakai āṭaikaḷ aṟuvakaip paruvattiṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu neyyap paṭṭaṉa.
mayir eṉṟatu, āṭṭumayiraiyum oruvakai elimayiraiyum.
ūṇ
ulakil mutaṉmutal uṇavai nākarikamāyc camaittuṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
uṇṇal tiṉṉal nakkal parukal eṉṉum nālvakai ūṇ viṉaiyum oruṅkē cērntatu cāppiṭutal.
uṇavaic cōṟum kuḻampum kaṟiyum eṉa mūṉṟākap pakuttu, nellariciyaic cōṟākac camaittu, kuḻampaiyum kaṟiyaiyum kaṇṇiṟku aḻakum mūkkiṟku naṟumaṇamum nāviṟku iṉcuvai yum uṇarttuvaṉavum, uṭampai valuppaṭuttuvaṉavum, nōy varātu taṭuttu vāḻnāḷai nīṭṭippaṉavumāṉa palavakai maruntuc carakkukaḷaic cērttu ākki, mutaliṟ kuḻampum iṭaiyil miḷaku nīrum iṟutiyil tayir allatu mōrum ūṟṟi, iyaṟkaiyāṉa melliya vāḻaiyilaiyil aṟucuvaiyuṇṭi toṉṟutoṭṭu iṉpamākac cāppiṭṭu varupavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
arici vakaiyil, nellarici talai; varakutiṉai cāmai kutirai vāli kāṭaik kaṇṇi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ arici iṭai; kañci kaḷi kūḻukku maṭṭum utavum cōḷam kampu kēḻvaraku kaṭai.
nellariciyil cīrakaccampā ciṟumaṇiccampā eṉpavai talai piṟa campā iṭai; maṭṭaiyarici kaṭai.
cōḷam kampu kēḻvarakuṇṇum pāṭṭāḷi makkaḷum cuvai yāṉa kuḻampum toṭukaṟiyum virumpuvar.
kuḻampiṟ kutavum payiṟṟu vakaikaḷuḷ, tuvarai talai; avarai moccai paccai iṭai; uḻuntu taṭṭāṉ kal karampai koḷ (kāṇam) iṭai.
(miḷaku) nīr, nīṭṭāṇam (soup), cāṟu, kuḻampu kūṭṭu eṉpaṉa kuḻampu vakaiyil muṟaiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tiṇṇiyavai.
palvakaik kāykaḷum mīṉum iṟaicciyum, vāṭṭal, vatakkal, vaṟuval, poriyal, tuvaṭṭal, puraṭṭal, aviyal, oṭiyal eṉap palvēṟu muṟaiyil kaṟiyākkappaṭṭaṉa.
kuḻampiṟkum kaṟikkum iṟutiviṉai tāḷippu (ucilippu). ataṟku eṇṇeyum āneyyum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
iṟaicci vakaiyil, muḻuvuṭumpu, mukkāl kāṭai, arai kōḻi, kāl āṭu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
aṇi
poṉṉum muttum maṇiyum kumarināṭṭil ērāḷamāyk kiṭaittaṉa. nuṇṇiya ōviya vēlaippāṭuḷḷa aṇikaḷum kalaṅkaḷum taṭṭumuṭṭukkaḷum uruvaṅkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
kaittoḻil
ellāt toḻilkaḷaiyuñ cevvaiyākac ceytaṟku, irumpi ṉālum cempiṉālum veṇkalattiṉālum ciṟanta karuvikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. irumpu mutaṉmutal tamiḻakattilēyē kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku, ataṉ tamiḻp peyar āriya moḻi kaḷil vaḻaṅkuvatē cāṉṟām.
E. iron, OE. iren, isen, Ger. eisen, Skt. ayas.
makkaṭ karuttukkaḷ :
ovvoruvarum pāl vakuppu vēṟupāṭiṉṟi aṟam poru ḷiṉpam vīṭu aṭaital vēṇṭum. entavūrum contavūr. ellārum ōriṉam. aṟivāṟṟal kuṇañceyalkaḷālaṉṟip piṟappāṟciṟappillai. oruvaṉukkup perumaiyum ciṟumaiyum taṉṉālēyē varum. iṉpamum tuṉpamum varutaṟku avaravar paḻa viṉaiyē kāraṇam; piṟarallar. piṟantavaiyellām iṟakkum. ataṟku añcal kūṭātu. atu oru nilaimaiyiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru nilaimaikkup pukuvāyilē. ovvoruvarkkum vāḻkkaiyil oru kaṭamaiyuṇṭu. atu niṟaivēṟiyapiṉ iṟappu varum.
uṭaluḻaippiṟkum maṉavuḻaippiṟkum uyar patavikkum tāḻ patavikkum ēṟṟavāṟu, kuṇaṅkaḷum tiṟamaikaḷum piṟappilēyē amaintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, periyōraip pukaḻtalum ciṟiyōrai ikaḻtalum takkataṉṟu. ovvoruvarum tammaip pōṟ piṟaraik karututal vēṇṭum. iṟantapiṉ uṭaṉ varuvaṉa avaravar ceyta iruvakai viṉaikaḷē.
araciyal
aracar tammai uyirākavum tam kuṭikaḷai uṭampākavum karuti, aimperuṅkuḻu, eṇpērāyam, aivakai uṟutic, cuṟṟam, nālvakaip paṭai, nālvakaiyaraṇ ākiyavaṟṟait tuṇaik koṇṭu, naṭunilaiyāka muṟaiceytu ceṅkōlāṭci ceytu vantaṉar.
potukkalvi
ikkālattiṟpōl araciyaluṟuppāṉa potukkalvit tiṇaik kaḷam akkālattil illaiyēṉum, ēṟattāḻa ellārum tamiḻil eḻutap paṭikkat terintavarāyiruntaṉar. pāṭṭāḷiyuṭ paṭap pala toḻilārum pāvalarākavumiruntaṉar.
ceyyuḷ
akkālat tilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷākavēyiruntatu. atu veṇpā, āciriyappā (akavaṟpā), kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum nālvakaip pākkaḷāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ceyyuḷ nūliṟkup poruḷ kūṟum uraiyum eḷiya ceyyuḷākavēyiruntatu veṇpāviṟkum kalippāviṟkum iṇaiyāṉa yāppuvakai vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇaviyalātu.
māttirai, eḻuttu, acai, cīr, aṭi, yāppu, marapu, tūkku, toṭai, nōkku, pā, aḷavu, tiṇai, kaikōḷ, kūṟṟu, kēṭpōr, kaḷaṉ, kālam, payaṉ, meyppāṭu, eccam, muṉṉam, poruḷ, tuṟai, māṭṭu, vaṇṇam eṉac ceyyuḷuṟuppukkaḷ irupattāṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
	aṟiviyal
ilakkaṇam
ettuṇaic ciṟantaṉavēṉum, ēṉai yuyartaṉic cemmoḻikaḷi lellām, eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉa moḻiyilak kaṇam nālvakaippaṭṭatē. tamiḻilakkaṇam maṭṭum, ulakil mutaṉmutal iyaṟṟappaṭṭatēṉum, coṟṟoṭarum ceyyuḷum nūlum kūṟum poruḷukkum ilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭatākum.
akam, puṟam eṉap poruḷ irupāṟpaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ov voṉṟum evvēḻu tiṇaikaḷaik koṇṭatu. potuvāka akam kātal vāḻkkaiyaiyum, puṟam pōrviṉaiyaiyum, kūṟum. ivai yiraṇṭumallāta ellāp poruḷkaḷum, vākai yeṉṉum puṟattiṇaikkuḷ aṭakkappaṭum.
kaṇakku
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar kaṇakkil mikat tērcci peṟṟiruntaṉar.
 oṉṟiliruntu mēṟpaṭṭatu mēlvāyilakkam eṉṟum, oṉṟiṟku kīḻppaṭṭatu kīḻvāyilakkam eṉṟum, collappaṭṭaṉa.
kumpam = nūṟu kōṭi
caṅkam (caṅku) = ilakkaṅ kōṭi
tāmarai = kōṭā kōṭi
vāraṇam = nūṟu kōṭā kōṭi (kōṭā kōṭi?)
ivaṟṟiṉ matippu vēṟuvakaiyākavuñ collappaṭum. kuvaḷai, veḷḷam, āmpal, neytal eṉpaṉavum aṭukkiya kōṭi kaḷaik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷākum. kīḻvāyilakkam mukkāl, arai, kāl, araikkāl, mākāṇi (vīcam), mā(1/20), kāṇi (1/80), muntiri (1/320), kīḻmuntiri (muntiriyil 1/320) eṉpaṉa.
ciṟṟilakkattiṟkum pērilakkattiṟkum perukkal vāypāṭirun tatu pōṉṟē, catura vāypāṭum iruntatu. atu kuḻikkaṇakku eṉappaṭṭatu. ciṟṟilakkak kuḻippu ciṟukuḻi yeṉṟum, pērilakkak kuḻippu peruṅkuḻi yeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kaṇiyam
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻak kaṇiyar nuḻai matiyaruṅ kūrṅ kaṇṇarumā yiruntataṉāl, puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟalai, mūtirai, kaḻai, koṭiṟu, aravu, koṭunukam, kaṇai, uttaram, kai, aṟuvai, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉai, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, muṟkuḷam, kaṭaikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākkai, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉṉum irupatteḻu nāṭkaḷaiyum; mēḻam, viṭai, āṭavai, alavaṉ, maṭaṅkal, kaṉṉi, tulai, naḷi, cilai, cuṟavam, kumpam, mīṉam, eṉṉum paṉṉīrōraikaḷaiyum; ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy, aṟivaṉ (putaṉ), viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, kāri (caṉi) eṉṉum ēḻu kōḷkaḷaiyum, kaṇṭu, kaṇiya nūlai vaḷarttu vantaṉar.
eḻukōḷkaḷiṉ peyarāl eḻunāṭkiḻamai (vāram) vakukkap paṭṭatu. atu ulaka muḻutum paravi iṉṟuvarai vaḻaṅki varu kiṉṟatu.
kiḻamaip peyarkaḷ camaṟkirutattilum, ōraip peyarkaḷ camaṟkirutattilum ilattīṉilum, moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭuḷa.
iṉṉicai
mural (curam), paṇ, meṭṭu, tāḷam eṉṉum nāṟkūṟu koṇṭa iṉṉicai muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭaintiruntatu.
paṇ (eḻumural), paṇṇiyal (aṟumural), tiṟam (aimmural) tiṟat tiṟam (nāṉmural) eṉa nālvakaippaṭṭa paṇkaḷ narappaṭai vāl 11,991 ākak kaṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āyappālai, vaṭṭap pālai, caturappālai, mukkōṇappālai (tirikōṇappālai) eṉṉum nālvakai muṟaiyil, eḻuperumpālaikaḷum avaṟ ṟiṉ kiḷaikaḷum tirikkappaṭṭaṉa. attirivu muṟaikaḷ, muṟaiyē, muḻumural, araimural, kālmural, araikkāl mural ākiya mural nilaikaḷait taḻuviyaṉa eṉpar. innuṭpaṅkaḷ iṟṟai icaivāṇarkkut teriyāvāṟu, āriyattāl maṟaiyuṇṭu pōyiṉa.
tōl tuḷai narampu uṟai (kañcam) eṉṉum nālvakai icaik karuvikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu narappuk karuvi. narappuk karuvikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu yāḻ eṉṉum vīṇai. yāḻkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ. ataṉ vaḻiyiṉatē iṟṟai vīṇai. viṇṇeṉal = narampu teṟittal. viṇ - vīṇai. tōliṟ ciṟantatu mattaḷam (periyatu) allatu mataṅkam (mirutaṅkam). tuḷaiyiṟ ciṟantatu pullāṅkuḻal.
nāṭakam
icaiyoṭu naṭamum naṭippum cērntatu nāṭakam. naṭi + akam = nāṭakam.
naṭam eṉpatu, katai taḻuviyatum taḻuvātatum eṉa iruvakaittu. nāṭakam eṉpatu, potuvāka ulaka vaḻakkiṟ katai taḻuvi varum naṭippaiyē kuṟikkum. kūttu eṉṉum peyar naṭattiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum potu. naṭam (naṭaṉam) eṉpatu āṭal eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
āṭalkaḷ, nilaittaṉavum avvappōtu āṭalāciriyaṉāṟ putituputitāyp payiṟṟappaṭuvaṉavum eṉa irupāṟpaṭum. nāṭaka mellām putituputitāyt tōṉṟuvaṉavē.
āṭalkaḷ, vēttiyal, potuviyal, tēviyal eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. kaṭaiyam, marakkāl, kuṭai, tuṭi, alliyam, mal, kuṭam, pēṭu, pāvai, pāṇṭaraṅkam, koṭṭi eṉṉum patiṉōrāṭalum, piṟkālattil teyvaṅkaḷ āṭiṉavākak kataikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. kuravai, vari eṉpaṉavum tēviyaliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
naṭak karaṇaṅkaḷ aṟupattu nāṉku. naṭaviṉaik kai, oṟṟaik kai (piṇṭikkai) iṇaikkai eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. oṟṟaik kai muppattumūṉṟu; iṇaikkai patiṉaintu.
naṭippiṟkuriya naḷinayam (apinaya) muppattiraṇṭu.
ceyyuṭkum iṉṉicaikkum nāṭakattiṟkum uriya cuvaikaḷ; nakai, aḻukai, iḷivaral, maruṭkai, accam, perumitam, vekuḷi, uvakai eṉa eṭṭu.
iṉṉicaiyilum nāṭakattilum kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar, āḻntu īṭupaṭṭum ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟum nirampa iṉpuṟṟum vantata ṉāl, iṉṉicai nāṭakattaiyum tamiḻoṭu cērttu, iyaṟ ṟamiḻ, icaittamiḻ, nāṭakattamiḻ eṉa mukkūṟṟatākki muttamiḻ eṉṟaṉar. ittakaip puṇarppu vēṟenta nāṭṭilumillai.
maruttuvam
ūtai pittuk kōḻai yeṉṉum munnāṭiyiyalpai, teyvat taṉmaiyāṉa nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattā laṟintu, ellā nōy kaṭkum maruttuvam aṟuvai eṉṉum iruvakaip paṇṭuvam ceyvatu tamiḻ maruttuvam. ataṉ talaiciṟanta maruntu irumpu cempu mutaliya poṉṉaṅkaḷait tūya poṉṉākkuvatum, kaḻi neṭuṅkālam (1,00,000 āṇṭu?) vāḻac ceyvatumāṉa muppu (mūvuppu) eṉṉum oruvakaik kalavaiyuppu. atu ceyyum vakai maṟaiyuṇṭu pōyiṟṟu.
mataviyal
kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya cēyōṉ (murukaṉ) vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu civaṉiyamum, mullai nilattiṟkuriya māyōṉ (kariyavaṉ, māl) vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu māliyamum, ākiya peruntēva matam iraṇṭum vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa.
ōkappayiṟciyālum uḷḷattūymaiyālum eṇṇattiṇmai yālum iṟaippaṟṟālum, nuṇmai, parumai, noymmai, kaṉati, karutiyatu peṟutal, karutiyaviṭañ cellutal karutiya vaṭi veṭuttal, kūṭuviṭṭuk kūṭupāytal ākiya eṇvakaip peṟṟi kaḷaip peṟṟa peṟṟiyar (cittar), iru peruntēva matattiṟkum potuvāṉa kaṭavuḷ matattaik kaṇṭaṉar. kaṭavuḷ matamāvatu, kālam iṭam vaṭivu kuṇam mutaliya ellā vaṟṟaiyuṅ kaṭantu iyalpāṉa muṟṟaṟivum muḻuvallamaiyuḷḷa orutaṉip param poruḷai, uḷḷattil toḻutal.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar iṅṅaṉam ulakiyalilum mataviyalilum uyarnilaiyaṭaintiruntataṟkuk karaṇiyam, avar neṟṟiyiṟ peṟṟirunta oruvakai aṟivukkaṇ eṉṟum, atu kaṇṇāṟ kāṇātataik kāṇavum kātāṟ kēḷātataik kēṭkavum vallatu eṉṟum, ataip piṉṉōr neṭuṅkālam payaṉpaṭuttāmai yāl atai iḻantuviṭṭaṉar eṉṟum, ārāycciyāḷar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ikkaruttai, civaperumāṉukku neṟṟik kaṇ uṇṭeṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu ōraḷavu valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
	paṇpāṭu
1. vēḷāḷar paṇpāṭu
putitāka illattiṟku vantavarkkum vaḻic cellum ayalārk kum ciṟanta vuṇavaḷippatum, irappōrkku illaiyeṉṉātu īvatum, vēḷāḷar paṇpāṭu.
2. vaṇikar paṇpāṭu
poruḷkaḷai vāṅkumpōtu aḷavaik kūṭṭip peṟāmalum, viṟkumpōtu kuṟaittuk koṭāmalum, nērmaiyāka vaṇikañ ceyvatum; uṟuppilikaṭku ūṭṭuppuraiyum, ayalār taṅkac cattirañ cāvaṭiyum, kaṭṭi vaippatum; vaḻippōkkarkkuc cōlaiyoṭu kūṭiya kuḷam veṭṭivaippatum; nīr nilai yillāk kāṭṭu vaḻikaḷil nelli maraṅkaḷ vaḷarttu vaippa tum; vaṇikar paṇpāṭu.
aracar paṇpāṭu
kāṭcika keḷiyāraiyum kaṭuñ collarallarāyu miruppatum, naṭunilaiyāka muṟaiceytu ceṅkōlāṭci celuttuvatum, vaḻakkiḻantavar tammiṭam muṟaiyiṭa araṇmaṉai vāyilil ārāyccimaṇi kaṭṭivaippatum, vaṟaṭcik kālattil varinīkkuva tum, tirunāḷ perunāṭkaḷil ciṟaiñarai viṭutalai ceyvatum, matampaṟṟiya uṇmaiyai makkaḷaṟitaṟkup paṭṭimaṉṟam naṭattu vatum, kūṉar kuṟaḷar mutaliya eccap piṟavikaḷai araṇmaṉaik kuṟṟēvaṟ kamarttuvatum, pōr toṭaṅkumuṉ peṇṭir piḷḷaikaḷ piṇiyāḷar mutiyōr mutaliyōraip pātukāp pāṉa iṭattiṟku akaṟṟi viṭuvatum paṭaikkalamiḻantavaṉum eḷiya paṭaikkalamuḷḷavaṉum kīḻē viḻuntavaṉum muṭikulainta vaṉum āṭaiyaviḻntavaṉum tōṟṟōṭukiṉṟavaṉum ākiyōr mītu paṭaikkalam ēvāmai yum, tōṟṟa aracaṉait tiṟaikaṭṭac ceytu avaṉoṭu maṇavuṟavu koḷvatum, pōril mutukiṟ puṇpaṭṭa pōtum vāḻkkaiyil māṉaṅkeṭa nērntapōtum uṇṇā nōṉpiruntu uyirtuṟappatum, aracar paṇpāṭām.
pulavar paṇpāṭu
aracar matiyātu tarum paricilaip peṟātu pōvatum, vaṟumai yiṟ cemmaiyāy oḻukutalum, pērācaiyiṉmaiyum, aracar tīṅkuceyyiṉ taṭuppatum, avarkku añcātu aṟivurai kūṟuva tum potunalam pēṇuvatum, coṟpoḻivaraṅkiṟkuc cel liṉ iṟutivaraiyiliruntu kēṭpatum pulavar paṇpāṭām.
akumarināṭṭu ilakkiyam
ilakkiyam (1) eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyam, (2) eḻutappaṭā ilak kiyam eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyamum, (1) urai naṭaiyilakkiyam (2) ceyyuḷilakkiyam eṉṟum, (1) potu vilakkiyam (2) ciṟappilakkiyam eṉṟum, naṭai paṟṟiyum payaṉ paṭuttum makkaḷ paṟṟiyum ivviruvakaippaṭum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻilakkiyam, naṭaipaṟṟic ceyyuḷilakkiyamum payaṉpaṭuttuvār paṟṟip potucciṟap pilakkiyamumākum.
potuvilakkiyam
pāṭṭu, urai nūl, vāymoḻi allatu mantiram, pici aṅkatam, mutucol eṉṉum eḻuvakai nilaikkaḷattiṟ ceyyuḷilakkiyam tōṉṟiṟṟu. nilaikkaḷam yāppu vakai,
avaṟṟuḷ : pāṭṭu eṇvakai vaṉappākap pakukkappaṭṭatu.
eṇvakai vaṉappukkaḷ
 1. ammai
ciṉmeṉ moḻiyāṟ cīrpuṉain tiyāppiṉ
ammai tāṉē aṭinimir piṉṟē.  (tol. cey. 233)
 2. aḻaku
ceyyuḷ moḻiyāṟ cīrpuṉain tiyāppiṉ
avvakai tāṉē aḻakeṉap paṭumē.  (tol.cey. 234)
 3. toṉmai
toṉmai tāṉē
 uraiyōṭu puṇarnta paḻaimai mēṟṟē  (tol.cey. . 235)
4. tōl
iḻumeṉ moḻiyāṉ viḻumiyatu nuvaliṉum
paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭinimirn toḻukiṉum
tōleṉa moḻi toṉmoḻip pulavar.  (tol.cey. 236)
5. viruntu
viruntē tāṉum
putuvatu kiḷanta yāppiṉ mēṟṟē  (tol.cey. 237)
6. iyaipu
ñakāra mutalā ṉakāra vīṟṟup
puḷḷi yiṟuti iyaipeṉap paṭumē.  (tol.cey. 238)
7. pulaṉ
 cēri moḻiyāṟ cevvitiṟ kiḷantu
 tērtal vēṇṭātu kuṟittatu tōṉṟiṉ
 pulaṉeṉa moḻipa pulaṉuṇarn tōrē.  (tol.cey. 239)
8. iḻaipu
oṟṟoṭu puṇarnta valleḻut taṭakkātu
kuṟaḷaṭi mutalā aintaṭi yoppit
tōṅkiya moḻiyāṉ āṅṅaṉam oḻukiṉ
 iḻaipiṉ ilakkaṇam iyainta tākum.  (tol.cey. 240)
ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kūṭa ikkālattillai. cilappatikāramum maṇi mēkalaiyum ṉakaramayyīṟṟup pāṭṭukkaḷaik koṇṭiruppa tāl, iyaipu eṉṉum vaṉappiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. āyiṉ, avai tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭavai yalla. tolkāp piyak kālam ki.mu. 6-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu; cilappatikāra maṇimēkalaik kālamō ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa āriyamallāta ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷum moḻic ceytikaḷum, mutaliru kaḻakak kālat tiṟkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟit tolkāppiyar kālattiṟ kuriyaṉavalla.
muntu nūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa eṇṇip
pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval.
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉārum, eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar, nuṇ ṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟē, eṉamoḻipa otta teṉpa vuṇaru mōrē, celvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē eṉṟum, piṟa vāṟum, cārpu nūlāciriyar muṟaiyil tolkāppiyaṉārum, kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
iyaipu
vaṉappup pāṭṭukkaḷ ña, ṇ, n, m, ṉ eṉṉum aim melliṉa meykaḷuḷ oṉṟil iṟum. cilappatikāra maṇimēkalaiyākiya iraṭṭai vaṉappup pāṭṭukkaḷ ṉ eṉṉum orē meyyil iṟukiṉṟaṉa. oru meykku oruvaṉappu eṉṟu vaittuk koḷḷi ṉum, aimmeykkum aivaṉappāvatu iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. oṉṟukūṭa iṉṟillai. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉai vaṉappukkaḷum.
viruntu
eṉpatu, avvappōtu putituputitākat tōṉṟum palvakaip paṉuvalkaṭṭum potuppeyar. tolkāppiyattil viruntup paṉuvaleṉṟu oṉṟum vitantu kuṟikkappeṟavillai.
kuḻavi maruṅkiṉuṅ kiḻava tākum.
eṉpatu (tol. puṟat. 29) piḷḷaittamiḻ eṉṉum paṉuvalaiyum,
ūroṭu tōṟṟamum uritteṉa moḻipa.
eṉpatu (tol.cey. 30) ulā allatu ūriṉṉicaiyaiyum kuṟikkalām.
urai eṉpatu, viḷaṅkāta nūṉ mūlattiṟkup poruḷ kūṟuvatu. atuvuñ ceyyuḷākavē iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu; āyiṉ, alliyara cāṇimālai pavaḷak koṭimālai eṉpaṉa pōla eḷiya naṭaiyil amaintiruntatu. urainaṭaiyil irukkavēṇṭiya uraiyum ceyyuḷil iyaṉṟamai, akkālattut tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ ceyyuḷ tiṟattaic ciṟappak kāṭṭum.
nūl eṉpatu aṟiviyal. ilakkaṇam ōr aṟiviyal. ovvoru kalaiyilum teriviyal (Theory) paṟṟiya tellām ilakkaṇam pōṉṟa aṟiviyalē.
vāymoḻi allatu mantiram eṉpatu tirumūlar tirumantiram pōlvatu.
pici yeṉpatu viṭukatai
aṅkatam eṉpatu kaṇṭaṉanūl allatu maṟuppunūl. atu etirnūl eṉavumpaṭum.
mutucol eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
pāṭṭuppōṉṟē, ēṉaiyaṟuvakai yāppuc ceyyuḷum iṉ ṟillai paṇṇatti yeṉṉum oruvakai yāppum iṉṟillai. eḻuvakai yāppum veṇpā, akavaṟpā (āciriyappā) kalippā, vañcippā, eṉṉum tūya pākkaḷum, maruṭpā, paripāṭal eṉṉum kalavaip pākkaḷum ākiya aṟuvakaip pākkaḷāl iyaṉṟaṉa.
pākkaṭkuriya vaṇṇaṅkaḷ : pāa vaṇṇam, tāa vaṇṇam, vallicai vaṇṇam, mellicai vaṇṇam, iyaipu vaṇṇam, aḷapeṭai vaṇṇam, neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam, kuṟucīr vaṇṇam, cittira vaṇṇam, nalipu vaṇṇam, akappāṭṭu vaṇṇam, puṟap pāṭṭu vaṇṇam, oḻukuvaṇṇam, orūu vaṇṇam, eṇṇu vaṇṇam, akaippuvaṇṇam, tūṅkal vaṇṇam, ēntal vaṇṇam, uruṭṭu vaṇṇam, muṭuku vaṇṇam eṉa irupatu.
vaṇṇam, cittiram eṉpaṉa teṉcoṟkaḷē.
vaḷḷutal = vaḷaittal, vaḷaitteḻutal. vaḷ-vaṇ-vaṇṇam= eḻutum eḻuttu allatu varaiyum paṭam, eḻuttiṉ niṟam, niṟam pōṉṟa ceyyuḷoli vakai, vakai.
vaḷ-var-vari - varaṇam = eḻuttu, niṟam, vakai, vakuppu, icaippāvakai.
o.nō : tiḷ-tiṇ-tiṇṇai. tiḷ-tir-tiraḷ - tiraḷai - tiraṇai.
cettal = ottal. ce + tiram = cettiram - cittiram = ōviyam.
o.nō : centiram - cintiram = civappu, centūḷ, cennīṟu, cempoṟimuka yāṉai.
	ciṟappilakkiyam
icaiyum nāṭakamum
tolkāppiyattiṟku muntiyatu akattiyam. atu muttamiḻilak kaṇam
ilakkiya miṉṟi ilakkaṇa miṉṟē
eḷḷiṉ ṟākil eṇṇeyu miṉṟē
eḷḷiṉiṉ ṟeṇṇe yeṭuppatu pōla
ilakki yattiṉiṉ ṟeṭupaṭum ilakkaṇam.  (akat.)
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampaliṉ.  (naṉ. 141)
eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.
akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇam eṉpatāl, iyaṟṟamiḻilakkiyam pōṉṟē icaittamiḻ nāṭakattamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷum avvilak kaṇa nūṟku muṉpirut tiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. akattiyam tolkāppiyattiṟkuc cilapala nūṟṟāṇṭu muntiya tēṉum, iṭaikkaḻakat tiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum.
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa icaiyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.  (tol. eḻut. 33)
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puḷpaṟai
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokaii
avvakai piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa.  (tol. akat. 18)
 eṉpavaṟṟāl iṉṉicaiyilakkiyamum,
nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam.  (tol. akat. 53)
eṉpataṉāl nāṭakavilakkiyamum, kumarināṭṭiliruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
kaṇakku
ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti
alpeyar eṇṇiṉum āyiyal nilaiyum.  (puḷḷi. 98)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil 	
kuvaḷai, tāmarai, caṅkam, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉṉum mā pēreṇkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
caṅkam = ilakkaṅkōṭi
tāmarai = kōṭā kōṭi
iraṇṭumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (kuṟṟiya. 76) mā eṉṉum aḷavaik kuṟippatāl, kīḻvāyilakkattaic cērnta ēṉaiyaḷavu kaḷum tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpu vaḻaṅkiṉa veṉṟē koḷḷappaṭum.
kaṇiyam
tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa.  (tol. uyir. 84)
ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ colleṉa varūum.  (tol. kiḷavi. 58)
maṟainta voḻukkattu ōraiyum nāḷum.  (tol. kaḷa. 44)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya aṭikaḷ kaṇiyak kuṟippaik koṇṭaṉa.
maruttuvam
potiya malaiyil vatinta akattiyar oru maruttuva nūlāciriyar eṉṟu collappaṭuvataṉālum, peṟṟiya (citta) maruttuvam toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻa maruttuvamātalālum, oru cila maruntup peyarkaḷ akattiyar peyarai aṭaiyākap peṟṟiruppataṉālum,
vāyuṟai vāḻttē vayaṅka nāṭiṉ
vēmpuṅ kaṭuvum pōla veñcol
tāṅkuta liṉṟi vaḻinaṉi payakkumeṉ
ṟōmpaṭaik kiḷaviyiṉ vāyuṟut taṟṟē.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviluḷḷa (cey. 111) maruttuvak kuṟippiṉālum, kumarināṭṭil maruttuva ilakkiya miruntamai uyttuṇarappaṭum.
moḻinūl
centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum moḻip pakuppum iyaṟcol tiricol eṉṉum centamiḻc coṟpakuppum,
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi.
eṉṉum (tol. ec. 4) koṭuntamiḻc col viḷakkamum valittal melittal virittal tokuttal nīṭṭal kuṟukkal eṉṉum coṟṟirivu muṟaikaḷum, moḻinūliṉ karunilaiyē yāyiṉum, ulakil mutaṉmutaṟ collappaṭṭataṉāl, moḻi nūliṉ toṭakkamēyām.
uḷanūl
eḷḷal iḷamai pētaimai maṭaṉeṉ
ṟuḷḷap paṭṭa nakaināṉ keṉpa.  (tol. meyp. 4)
iḷivē iḻavē acaivē vaṟumaiyeṉa
viḷivil koḷkai aḻukai nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.5)
mūppē piṇiyē varuttam meṉmaiyōṭu
yāppuṟa vanta iḷivaral nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.6)
putumai perumai ciṟumai ākkamoṭu
matimai cālā maruṭkai nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.7)
aṇaṅkē vilaṅkē kaḷvartam iṟaiyeṉap
piṇaṅkal cālā accam nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.8)
kalvi taṟukaṇ icaimai koṭaiyeṉac
collap paṭṭa perumitam nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.9)
uṟuppaṟai kuṭikōḷ alaikolai yeṉṟa
veṟuppa vanta vekuḷi nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.10)
celvam pulaṉē puṇarvuviḷai yāṭṭeṉ
 ṟallal nītta uvakai nāṉkē.  (tol. meyp.11)
ivaiyum piṟa meyppāṭṭiyal nūṟpākkaḷum uḷanūṟ karuvām ēraṇam (logic)
